# Ethiopian Synaxarium: Yakkatit (Mois de yakkātit)

**Author**: Anonymous (Ethiopian Synaxarion)  
**Series**: PO Tome 45  
**Columns**: pp. 452–606  

[p. 452] {#po.45.p452}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፤ ወወልድ ፡፤ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡፤ አምላክ ። ወርኃ ፡ የካቲት ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ሰዓታቲሁ ፡ ፲ወ፳ ሰዓት ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ይትዌሰክ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. The month of yakkätit (is) blessed. The hours of its (first day are) eleven, then (the days) lengthen.

### READING FOR THE FIRST OF YAKKATIT
አመ ፡ ፳ለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ በዓለ፤ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ አበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ፻ወዛ ኤሊስ ፡ ቆአሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ በሀገረ ፣ ቀሩኑስጥንጥንያ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፣ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለቴዎዶስዮስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ። ወኮነ ፡ ተጋብኦቶሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መቅዶንዮስ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀሩኑስጥንጥንያ ። ወክህደ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ቅዱሰ" ፡ ወይቤ ፡ በእከየ ፡ ልቡ ፡ እስመ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፍጡር ፡ በከመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ፍጡራን ። ወበእንተ ፡ ሰብልዮስ" ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኤስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሎንያ ፡ ወክህደ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ገጽ ፡ ወአሐዱ ፡ህላዌ። ወበእንተ ፡ አቡሊናርዮስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ኢነሥአ ፡ ሥጋ!"፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ እንስሳዌ ፡ እራቁ ፡ እምነፍስ፤፤ ፡ ነባቢት ፡ ለባዊት ። መለኮት ፡ 'ኮነ ፡ ለሥጋ፤ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ወልብ ።
On this day (there is) the feast of the commemoration of the council, the assembly of the holy fathers, the one hundred and fifty bishops, who gathered in the city of Constantinople at the time of the reign of Tëwodosyos (= Theodosius) the Great. Their meeting took place because of Maqdonyos (= Macedonius) who was patriarch of the city of Questentenyä (= Constantinople) - he was impious toward the Holy Spirit and said in the malice of his heart: "The Holy Spirit (is) created like all creatures”; because also of Sabelyos (= Sabellius); he was bishop of the city of Lonyä (= Libya) - he was impious and said: "The Father, the Son and the Holy Spirit (are) one person (gas) and one nature (helläwë); and because of 'Abulinäryos (= Apollinaris) the impious who said: "The Son did not take the (whole) body, but (only) the animal body devoid of the rational (and) intelligent soul; the divinity held for the body the place of the soul and the heart."
ወሶበ ፡ ደፈሩ ፤ እሉ ፡ ይወክሕዱ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ክሕደተ ፤ ሕሱመ፤ ፡ ወተጋብኡ ፤ ቦእምነ! ፡ አበው ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተጋብእ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይነጽሩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ክህደቶሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ፻ከሀድያን ፤ ወኢይት ወከፉ፤ ፡ ቃሎሙ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዳሚሶስ"፤ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ። ወኀበ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወኅበ ፡ ምሊጦስ" ፡ ሊቀ፣ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፤ አንጾኪያ ። ወኅበ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽኡ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ 'ቆአሳተ ፡ ማእምራነ" ፡ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በሓውርቲሆሙ ። ወበጽሑ" ፡ እሉ ፡ አበው ፡ ፻ወዛ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆአሳት፤ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ተጋብኦቶሙ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ቀኑስጥንጥንያ ። ወኢበጽሐ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ አላ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ማእምራን ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ወምስሌሆሙ” ፡ መልእክት ፡ "በጽሕፈተ ፤ እዴሁ"" ። ወኮነ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ወሊቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ አባ ፣ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ሊቀ ፡ኣጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወጸውዖ ፡ ለመቅዶንዮስ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ሃይማኖቶ ፡ ርኩስት ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ እስመ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፍጡር ፡ በከመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ፍጡራን ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ "እስመ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስሰ፤ ፡ በኀቤነ ፡ "መንፈሱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር" ። ወለእመ ፡ ንቤ ፣ ከመ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፣ ፍጡር ፡ ንሕነሰ" ፡
When these three (personages) dared to utter this abominable impiety, some among the Fathers gathered before the emperor and asked him to gather a council to examine the impiety of these three impious ones and not to receive their word. Then the emperor dispatched toward Dämisos (= Damasus), patriarch of the city of Romë (= Rome), Timotëwos (= Timothy), patriarch of the city of Alexandria, Melitos (= Meletius), patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch) and Qérellos (= Cyril), bishop of the city of Jerusalem; he ordered them to bring with them the learned bishops who (were) in their country. These one hundred and fifty father bishops came and their meeting took place in the city of Constantinople. The patriarch of the city of Romë did not come, but learned people came in his place and, with them, envoys with a writing from his hand. Abbä Timotëwos, patriarch of the city of Alexandria, was the head and the president of this council; he summoned Magdonyos and ordered him to expose his impure faith. The impious one said: "The Holy Spirit (is) created like all creatures." Timotëwos answered him and said: "The Holy Spirit, for us, is the Spirit of God. If we said that the Spirit of God (is) created, we would make the life of God a creature, as you

ንሬሲ፤ ፡ ሕይወቶ፥" ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፤ ፍጥረተ?" ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤ ፡ አንተ ፣ በእበድከ ። ወለእመሰ ፡ ኮነት ፣ ሕይወቱ ፣፤ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ፍጥረት፤ ፡ ወውእ ቱሰ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ 'ወረሰይከ ፡ አንተ ፡ ፍጡር ፤ ውእቱ ። ወይቴይሰከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትመየጥ ፡ እምዛቲ ፡ 'ክሕደት ፡ ሕሱምቶ፥ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ትትኩነን ፡ ውስተ ፤ ገሃነም ፡ ወእሳት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወኢሰምዓ ፡ ወኢተመይጠ ። ወአውቱ ፡
say yourself in your folly. But if the life of God were a creature, he himself would not have life, and you will have made, you, that he be created. It would now be better for you to turn away from this abominable impiety before being punished in Gehenna and the eternal fire." (Maqdonyos) did not listen and did not come to repentance. (Timotëwos) excommunicated him, anathematized him, deposed him, and drove him out.
ወረገሞ ፡ ወመተሮ ፡ እምሚመቱ ፡ ወሰደዶ ። ወእምዝ ፤ ይቤሎ ፡ ለስብልዮስ፤ ፡ ተናገር ፡ ግፅዘከ እኩይ ፡ ወርኩስ ። ወተናገረ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ገጽ ፤ ወአሐዱ ፡፤ህላዌ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ አባ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ፣ ሥሉስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ህላዌ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤ ፡ በዕበድከ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ተሥዕረ ፡ ዝክረ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ወተስዕረት ፤ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትናከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፣ ተጠመቀ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወ ልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአንተሰ ፡ ክህድከ ፡ ሥሉሰ ፡ ቅዱሰ ። ወበከመሰ ፡ ትቤ ፣ በእበድከ ፣ ይከውንኑ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተሠገወ ፡ ወሐመ ፡ ወሞተ ። ወተስዕረኑ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ውስተ ፡ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወአብ ፡ ይጸርኅ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ዘኣፈቅር ፡ ወኪያሁ ፡ ስምዕዎ ። ወይቴይሰከ ፡ ከመ ፣ ትትመየጥ ፡ "እምዝንቱ ፡ ክህደት ፡ ርኩስት" ። እስመ ፡ ለእመ ፡ኮነ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ክሕዶ ፡ ለወልድ ፤ ወመቅዶንዮስኒ ፡ ክሕዶ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወአንተሂ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ሱታፌ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወአንተ፤፤ ፡ ከሐድከ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ወመንፈሰ ፡ ቅዱሰ ። 'ወኢሰምዓ ፡
He then said to Sebelyos: "Expose your bad and impure sentiment." (Sebelyos) spoke and said: "The Father, the Son and the Holy Spirit (are) one person and one nature." Abbä Timotëwos answered and said to him: "If the holy Trinity (is) one nature, as you say in your folly, behold the mention of the Trinity is rendered vain and your Christian baptism vain, for you, you were baptized in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, but you deny the holy Trinity! According to what you said in your folly, must the holy Trinity have been incarnated, have suffered, and have died? Is the word of the holy Gospel rendered vain which says: The Son was standing in the Yordänos (= Jordan), the Holy Spirit descended upon him, and the Father cried out from heaven saying: This is my Son, whom I love, listen to him? It would be better for you to turn away from this impure impiety, for, if 'Aryos (= Arius) was impious toward the Son and (if) Maqdonyos was impious toward the Holy Spirit, you, you are associated with them: you, you have been impious toward the Son and the Holy Spirit." (Sebelyos) did not listen and did not turn away from his impiety, and (Timotëwos) excommunicated him, anathematized him, deposed him, and drove him out.
ወኢተመይጠ።" ፡ እምክሕደቱ ፡ ወአውገዞ ፡ ወረገሞ ፡ 'ወመተሮ ፡ ወሰደዶ፥ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለአቡሊናርዮስ ፡ ተናገር፤ ፡ ግዕዘከ ፡ እኩየ ። ወይቤ ፡ በእ በዱ፤" ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ኢነሥአ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ 'እንስሳዌ ፡ ዕራቁ ፡ እምነፍስ” ፡ ነባቢት ፡ ለባዊት ፡ ወመለኮት ፡ 'ኮነ ፡ ለሥጋ፤ ፡ ህየንተ ፡'ነፍስ ፡ ወልብ"። ወአውሥአ ፡ አባ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቃል ፡ ነሥአ ፤ ሥጋ ፡ እምጠባይዓትነ” ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አሐደ" ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያድኅነነ ። ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ኢነሥአ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ እንስሳዊ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ዕራቁ ፣ እምነፍስ ፡ ነባቢት ፡ ለባዊት ፡ ወውእቱሰ ፣ ኢያድኅኖሙ ፡ ለእጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ አላ" ፡ ለእንስሳ ፣ በከመ ፡ ትቤ ፡ አንተ ፡ በእበድከ ። እስመ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ኢይትነሥኡ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ትንሣኤ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በነፍስ ፡ ነባቢት ፡ ለባዊት ፤ ወምስሌሃ ፡፤ ይነሥእ?፤ ፡ ሥጋ ፡፤ በከመ ፡ ምግባሩ ፡፤ እመሂ ፡ ኩነኔ?" ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ ትፍሥሕተ” ። ወበከመ ፡ እበድከሰ ፡፤ ተሥዕረኑ” ፡ ኪነ ፡ "ጥበበ ፤ ትስብእቱ" ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወአንተሰ ፡ ትቤ፣ በእበድከ ፤ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ አሐደ ፡ በነፍስ' ፡ ነባቢት ፡ ለባዊት ።
Then he said to 'Abulinäryos: "Expose your bad sentiment." ('Abulinäryos) said in his folly: "The Son did not take the (whole) body, but (only) the animal body devoid of the rational (and) intelligent soul; the divinity held, for the body, the place of the soul and the heart." Abbä Timotëwos answered and said to him: "If God the Word took a body of our nature (tabäye") and became one with it, (it is) to save us. If he had not taken the (whole) body, but only the animal body devoid of rational (and) intelligent soul, he would not save men but animals, as you, you say in your folly; men, in effect, will not resurrect on the day of the resurrection if it is not with the rational (and) intelligent soul, and, with it, the body will receive, according to its acts, either punishment or joy. According to your folly, is the wise operation of the incarnation of our Lord rendered vain? How does he say about himself that he is a man? You, you say in your folly that he did not become one with the rational (and) intelligent soul. It would be better for you to turn away from this opinion

ወይጌይሰከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትመየጥ ፡ "እምዝንቱ ፡ ምክር ፡ እኩይ ፡ ወእምዝንቱ ፡ ክሕደት ፤ ርኩስት ። ወኢሰምዓ ፡ ወኢተመይጠ” ፡ ወአውገዞ ፡ ወረገሞ ፡ ወመተሮ" ፡ ምስለ ፡ እሉንቱ። ወአውገዝዎሙ ፡ለእሉ ፡፻ ከሓዲያን ፡ እምአፉሆሙ፤ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ማኅበረ።፣ ቅዱሳን ፡ ፻ወ፻ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ ወአውገዝዎሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ተለውዎሙ" ፡ ወለእለ ፡ አምኑ ፤ በቃሎሙ ።
bad and from this impure impiety." ('Abulinäryos) did not listen and did not come to repentance, and (Timotëwos) excommunicated him, anathematized him, and deposed him with the (two other heresiarchs). They excommunicated the three impious ones by the mouth of the whole council of the one hundred and fifty holy bishops and they excommunicated those who followed them and those who believed in their word.
ወእምዝ ፡ ወሰኩ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡'እምኀበ ፡ ነአምን ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜታ ። እስመ ፡ አበው ፡ ፻፻፲ ወቿ ኢሠርዑ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይቤ ፤ አልቦ ፡ ማኅለቅተ ፤ ለመንግሥቱ ፡ ወበመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ዝንቱ" ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተፍጻሜት ፡ ዘነበቡ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እሉ ፡ አበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ፻፻፲ወቿ ወወሰኩ ፡ እሉ ፤ አበው ፡፤ ፻ወጻ፤፻ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜታ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሠርዑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደዊሆሙ” ፡ ለዙሎሙ ፡ አሕዛበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ወይገብሩ ፡ ሕጎሙ፤ ። ወኮነ ፡ ተጋብኦቶሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ አበው ፡ ፻ወ፻ኤኢስ ፡ ቆጾሳት ፡ በዓመት ፡ ዛ፻ ወቿ፻ ወ፳"" ለፍጥረተ ፤ ዓለም ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
Then (the Fathers) added in the Creed from "and we believe in the Holy Spirit" until the end; in effect, the three hundred and eighteen Fathers (of Nicaea) had only established (the Creed) until (where) it is said "there is no end to his reign." This "and in the Holy Spirit" was the end of what the three hundred and eighteen holy Fathers pronounced, and the one hundred and fifty Fathers added until the end of the (Creed). Then they established in this council canons, and these (are) still now in the hands of all the Christian peoples (who) practice their law. The meeting of these one hundred and fifty father bishops took place in the year 5881 (= 381 AD) of the creation of the world. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘተሐንጸት ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጴጥሮስ” ፡ ተፍጻሜ ፡ ሰማዕት ፣ ዘአውገዞ ፡ ለአርዮስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፤ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ በተፍጻሜተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ጸድቅ” ፡ ወአንሐለ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ 'ወተጸንጹ ፡ አብ ያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ። ሐነጹ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ በምዕራበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ። ወተቀደሰት ፡ በከመ ፡ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወኮነ ፣ ውስቴታ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ብዙኃት ። ወነበረት ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ነግሥ ፡ ተንበላት ፣፤ ብሔረ ፤ ግብጽ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ በብዙኅ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ ወኮ ነት ፡ ዕውቅተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በምዕራብ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተንሕለት” ፡ ወበድወት!"" ፡ አላ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኑሰ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ ጽንዕት ፡ ወኢይበውእ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ሙስና ፡ ግሙራ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also (there is) the feast of the consecration of the church which was built in the name of the holy Pêtros (= Peter), last of the martyrs, who excommunicated 'Aryos (= Arius). He was a martyr in the city of Alexandria at the end of the reign of Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) the impious. When Quastantinos (= Constantine) the righteous reigned, (that) he overturned the temples of idols and (that) churches were built, believers built a church to the west of the city of Alexandria in the name of the holy Pêtros; it was consecrated the same (day) as today and miracles and numerous wonders occurred there. It remained thus until the Muslims reigned over the land of Egypt. After they had reigned for a long time, it was known (by the name of) "church of the west.” After that, it was destroyed and devastated, but the church of the holy Pêtros in the heavenly Jerusalem is firm and corruption will never enter it. May his (= of Peter) prayer and his blessing be with us, amen
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ዕረፍቶሙ ፡ ለአስከናፍር ፤ ወለብእሲቱ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገብሮሙ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also (there is) the death of 'Askanäfer and his wife. May their prayer and their blessing be with their servant Yohannes (= John) for ever and ever, amen.]

[READING FOR THE 2ND OF YAKKATIT] In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father abbä Langinos (= Longinus), abbot of the monastery of Zegäg (= Zougag) which (is) outside the city of Alexandria. This saint was from the people of the land of Qelgeyä (= Cilicia) and he became a monk there. As for his master, he loved Christ (and) was a righteous monk who fled the glory of men. The abbot of their monastery died then and they wanted to name his (= of Longinus) master, the old man abbä Lukiyänos (= Lucian), abbot. Abbä Lukiyänos took his disciple Langinos and they left Qilgeyä; they arrived in the land of Soryä (= Syria) and dwelt in a church. Our Lord Christ did not want to hide their justice, for a city is not hidden, being on a mountain. God performed through their hands numerous miracles and wonders and praise and glory came to them from men.
እምኅበ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ለንጊኖስ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ መምሀሩ ፡ በምክረ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፤ ኀበ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ዝጋግ ፡ ተቀበልዎ ፡ በፍሥሓ ። ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔተ ፡ ዚአሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመሩ ፡ 'እምገድሉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወሠናይ ፡ ትሩፋቲሁ ፡ ወጽድቁ ፡ ሜምዎ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ዝጋግ! ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ መጽአ ፡ መምህሩ ፡ መነኮስ፤! ፡ አባ ፡ ሉኪያኖስ፤፡ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ድባባተ ፡ አሕማራት፤ ፡ ወይሴሰዩ ፡ እምግብረ ፤ እደዊሆሙ ። ወነበሩ ፡ ነዋኅ፤ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ በአሐዱ ፡ ልብ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዲበ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ተኣምራተ፤ ፡ ብዙኃተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡
As for the holy abbä Langinos, he left his master of his own accord and arrived in the land of Egypt. When he entered the monastery of Zegäg, they welcomed him with joy. He dwelt in this monastery until its abbot died. As (the monks) knew the (spiritual) combat of the saint, his beautiful virtues, and his justice, they named him abbot of the monastery of Zegäg. Some time later his master, the monk abbä Lukiyänos, arrived. They made boat sails and fed themselves from the work of their hands. They dwelt with one heart for long days, and God performed through their hands numerous miracles; then abbä Lukiyänos died.
አባ፤ ፡ ሉኪያኖስ!፤ ። "ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ፤ጾ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለምርቅያኖስ?፤" ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ዘአስተጋብአ" ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ በቂልቄዶንያ፤፤ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ላእካነ ፡ ኀበ ፤ ኩሉ ፡ በሓውርት ፤ ወምስሌሆሙ ፡ ጦማር ፡ ዘሃይማኖት ርኩስት ፡ዘይሬሲ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ክልኤተ ፡ ጠባይዓተ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ፣፤ ቦእምነ ፡ ላእካኒሁ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ዝጋግ ፡ ወወሀብዎ፤ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ጦማረ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ለንጊኖስ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ መርቅያኖስ” ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትእመኑ" ፡ በዘ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡፤ ጦማር- ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለንጊኖስ ፡ አንሰ ፣ ኢይ ክል ፤ እግበር ፡ ወኢምንተኒ” ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በምክሮሙ ፡ ለአበዊየ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ንዑ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንትማከሮሙ፤ ። ወአብኦሙ ፡ ለላእካነ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ኅበ ፡ በዓት ፣ እንተ ፡ ውስቴታ ፤ አብድንቲሆሙ"። ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ አረጋዊያን ። ወአንበረ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ጦማረ ፡፤ ኀበ ፣ አብድንቲሆሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኦአበዊየ ፤ ኢትበሉ ፡
When the time of the impious Mergeyänos (= Marcian) arrived, who convened a council at Qilgëdonyä (= Chalcedon), he sent messengers into all lands and, with them, the tome of the impure faith which attributed two natures to Christ (tabäye"). When some of his messengers arrived at the monastery of Zegäg, they gave the tome to Abbä Langinos and said to him: "The emperor Margeyänos commands you to believe in what is written in this tome." The holy Langinos said: "As for me, I can do nothing except by the counsel of my holy fathers; come with me so that we may consult them." He brought the emperor's envoys into the cave in which were the bodies of the holy elders, and placed the tome near the bodies


[p. 460] {#po.45.p460}
ከመ ፡ አንትሙ ፡፤ ሰከብክሙ፥ ፡ ወአዕረፍክሙ ። ናሁ ፤ አምጽኡ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ጦማረ ፡ ዘጽሑፍ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርኩስት ፡ ዘይሬስዮ፥ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ክልኤተ ፡፤ ጠባይዓተ ። ትኤዝዙኑቶ" ፡ ሊተ ፡ ከመ ፡ እጽሐፍቶ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ወእእመን፥ ፡ በቃሉ ፡ "አው ፡አልቦ ። ወለእመ ፡ ኢነገርክሙኒ ፡ ዘእገብር ፡ ሕያው ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወእመአኮ ፡ አነ ፡ ኣወጽእ ፡ አዕጽምቲክሙ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ መካንሩ፤ ። ወወፅአ ፡ ቃል ፡ እምአብድንቲሆሙ። ፤ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ይሰምዑ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ኢትኅ ድግሩ5 ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ርትዕተ ፡ ዘለአበዊነ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ወአበዊነ ፡ ፻፻፲ ወቿ ወኢትትሉ፣ ዘንተ ፡ ጦማረ ፡ ርኩሰ ፡ አላ ፡ አእትቶ ፡ እምላዕለ ፡ አብድንቲነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ላእካኒሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ፍርሀት ፣ ወረዓድ ፡ ዐቢይ ፤ ወኢተመይጡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አላ ፡ ላጸዩ ፡ ሥዕርተ ፡፤ ርእሶሙ ፡ ወመንኩሱ ፡ ወነበሩ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ደብር፥ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡
of the saints and said to them: "My fathers, do not say that you are lying down and resting; behold, this tome has been brought in which is written the impure faith that attributes two natures to Christ. Do you command me to subscribe to it and believe in its word or not? If you do not tell me what I must do, as surely as God is living, I will have your bones taken out of this place." A voice came out of their bodies and all the people heard it saying: "Do not abandon the right faith of our fathers the apostles and of our fathers the three hundred and eighteen, do not follow this impure tome, but remove it from upon our bodies." When the emperor's messengers heard this word, they were greatly astonished, and fear and a great trembling came upon them; they did not return to the emperor, but they shaved their hair, became monks, remained in this monastery for many years, and then died.
አዕፅረፉ” ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ለንጊኖስ ፡ 'እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ 'ሠናየ ፡ በርሥዕና፤ ፣ ሠናይት ፡ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስብሐት፥ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
As for the holy Langinos, as he had finished his beautiful (spiritual) combat in a beautiful old age and pleased God, he died in peace and received the crown of glory from our Lord Jesus Christ. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳውሊ ፡ 'ዘየዓቢ ፡ እምኩ ሎሙ ፡፣ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወርእሰ ፡ ገዳማዊያን" ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወኮነ ፤ ስሙ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ 'ወሎቱ ፡ እኅወ ፡ ዘስሙ፥፤ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡ ባዕል ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ 'ወኢይትኃለቀ- ፡ ብዝኀ ፡ ንዋዩ ፡ እምወርቅ ፥ ወእምብሩር ፡ ወአልባሳት ፡ ክቡራት ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡ ወተፈጸመ ፡ መዋዕለ ፤ ሐዘኖሙ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡፣ አኀዙ ፡፣ ይትካፈሉ ፡ ርስተ ፤ አቡሆሙ ፡ በማእከሌ ሆሙይዖ‹ ። ወኮነ ፡ እጐሁ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ ክፍለ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ፡ ወይሁቦ ፡ ለጳውሊ፤ ፡ ክፍለ ፡ ዘይንእስ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእሁ ፡ ለምንት ፤ ኢትሁ በኒ ፡ ክፍልየ ፡ እምርስተ ፡ አቡየ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ወትዘሩ ፡ ንዋየከ። ፡ ወአነ ፡ አዓቅቦ ፡ ለከ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ትልህቅ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ተባአሱ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ማእከሌ ሆሙዖ ፣፡ ፍትሐ” ። ወእንዘ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ ፳ምውተ ፡ ወግኑዘ” ፡ እንዘ ፤ ይጸውርዎ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዓራት“ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ይተልውዎ ፡ ወይላሕዉ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። አሜሃ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ ለ፳እምእሉ ፡ እለ ፡ ይተልውዎ፤፤ ፡ ለምውት” ፡ ወተስእሎ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ መኑ ፡ ዝንቱ” ፡ ዘሞተ ፡ ዮም ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወይቤሎ” ፡ አእምር ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘሞተ፣
On this day also died the holy father Abbä Päwli (= Paul), the greatest of all the saints and the chief of the solitaries. This saint was from the city of Alexandria, his name was Päwlos and he had a brother by the name of Pêtros (= Peter); their father was very rich and the amount of his fortune in gold, silver, and precious garments was incalculable. When their father died and the time of their mourning was finished, they undertook to share their father's inheritance between them. His (= Päwli's) brother Pêtros began to take the larger share and to give Päwli the smaller share. The heart of the latter was saddened because of this and he said to his brother: "Why do you not give me my share of my father's inheritance?" (Pêtros) answered and said to him: "You are an adolescent and you would dissipate your fortune, but I am going to keep it for you until you have grown up." They quarreled because of this and went to the judge so that he might decide between them. While they were on the way, they saw a dead man, wrapped in a shroud, who was being carried on a stretcher; many people were following him and weeping over him. Then the holy Päwlos called one of those who were following the dead man, questioned him, and said to him: "Who is this who has died today?" The man answered and said to him: "Know, my son, that this man who has died today was very rich and rejoiced in gold and silver, and behold, he has abandoned all that and is going naked to the tomb by a path by which he will not

ዮም ፡ኮነ”" ፡ ባዕለ ፡ ፈድፋደ?" ፡ ወይትፈጋፅ" ፡ በወርቅ ፡ ወብሩር ። ወናሁ ፡'ኀደጎ፣፡ ለዝንቱ ፤ ኩሉ ፡ ወየሐውር ፤ ዕራቁ፤ ፡፤ ኀበ ፤ መቃብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፤ ዘኢይትመየጥ" ፡ እምኔሃ ። ወሞተ ፡ ውስተ" ፡ መዋግደ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ኃጣውኢሁ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ይደልወነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንትጋደል ፡ በእንተ ፣ መድኃኒተ ፡ ነፍስነ ፡፤ እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፤ ኢናአምር ፡ ማእዜ ፤ ዘንመውት ። አላ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ፣ ዘኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስልጣነ ፡ 'ወንዋየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወየኀድጎ ፤ እስመ" ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ ሀገሮሙ ፡ 'ለሰማዕት ፣ ወለጻድቃን ፣ ዙሎሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ጳውሊ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ገዓረ ፤ እምውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ብየ ፡ አነ ፡ ወንዋየ" ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኀላፊ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አኀድጎ ፡ ወአሐውር፤ ፡ ዕራቅየ ። ወእምዝ ፡
return. He died in the floods of all his sins. Now, my son, we must fight for the salvation of our soul, for we do not know when we will die. But happy is the man who has power and fortune in this world and abandons them, for he will receive great glory in the heavenly Jerusalem, the city of all the martyrs and just ones!" When the holy Abbä Päwli heard this discourse, he groaned from the bottom of his heart and said: "What have I to do with the goods of this transitory world? In a few days, I will leave it and go away naked." Then he turned toward his brother and said to him: "Come, let us return to our house, for, from now on, I will say nothing more about the fortune."
ተመይጠ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ነዓ ፡ ንትመየጥ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤትነ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ እምይእዜ ፤ ወኢይትናገር። ፤ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ንዋይ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጐየ ፡ እምእጐሁ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃ ብር ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ፻መዓልተ ፡ ወ፻ለያልየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ወያስተበቀነዕ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምርሖ ፡ ውስተ ፣፤ ሥምረቱ ። ወእጉሁሰ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ነበረ ፡ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡እንዘ ፡ የዐውድ ፡ በእንቲአሁ” ፡ ውስተ ፡'ኩላ ፡ ሀገር-» ወኢረከቦ ። ወሐዘነ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ "በእንተ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምኔሁ "ወቅዱ ስሰ ፣፤ ጳውሎስ ፡ ወእንዘ” ፡ ይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መቃብር ፡ 'መጠነ ፡ ፻መዓልት!"‹ ፡ ኢተዘከረ ፡ መብልዓ ፡ ወመስቴ ፡ ወኢበጽሖ ፡ ፍርሀት!፤፤ ፡ ወኢድን ጋፄ ፤ እስመ ፡ ኀይል ፡፤ ቅድስት፤ ፡ ኮነት፤! ትጹልል ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወበራብዕት ፡ ዕለት ፡ ፈነወ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ መልአኮ ፡ ወመሰጦ ፡ እምህየ፤" ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጤ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ዘምሥራቅ ። ወአንበሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ኅበ፤!፡ ፥፡ ነቅዓ ፤ ማይ ፡ ወተአምኖፕ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወዓርገ ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳውሊ ፡ ረከበ ፡ ግበባተ ፡ ዘአራዊት ፡ ወቦአ ፤ ውስቴታ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ልብሰ፣ 'እምጸጐረ ፡በቀልት፤ ፡ ወለብሶ ። "ወኮነ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ወይብል፤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ፡ ዕቀበኒ ፡ ወአድኅነኒ ፡ እምእዴሁ ፡ ለጸላኢ ፤ ዘአልቦቱ ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ወትምጻእ ፡ ምሕረትከ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ወታጽንዓኒ ፡ እስከ ፡ እፈጽም ፡ ገድልየ ፡ በሥምረትከ ፡ ኦነጋሜ፤ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ትውልድ፤" ፡ እስመ ፡ ለከ ፣ ኀይል ፡ ወስብሐት ፡'ለዓለም ፡ አሜን፤! ። ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በዓት ፡ ፳ዓመተ፣ ኢርእየ ፡መነሂ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡ ግሙራ፤፤፤ ። ወኮነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይፌኑ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡
After that, he fled from his brother, went outside the city, entered a tomb, and remained there for three days and three nights, praying and beseeching God to guide him toward what He would want. His brother Pêtros spent many days traveling throughout the city for him, but he did not find him. He felt great sorrow because of him, because of what had happened to him. As for the holy Päwlos, while he remained in this tomb for three days, he remembered neither food nor drink, and neither fear nor dread reached him, for a holy power was protecting him. On the fourth day, God sent him his angel; (the latter) took him from there and brought him into the interior of the eastern desert. The angel deposited him in a place near a spring of water, kissed him, and ascended to heaven. As for the holy Abbä Päwli, he found dens of wild beasts, entered them, made himself a garment of palm fibers, and put it on. He prayed and spoke thus: "My Lord Jesus Christ, Son of the living God, guard me and save me from the hand of the Enemy who has no mercy; may your mercy come upon me and strengthen me until I have finished my (spiritual) combat according to your will, O King of all generations, for to you (are) the power and the glory forever, amen." He remained in this cave for eighty years (and) never saw any human being. God sent him a raven every day, at the time of evening - (it had) with it half a loaf of bread - and it gave it to him.
ቋዐ፤፤ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ምሴት ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ወይሁቦ ፡ ኪያሁ!! ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ቅድስናሁ ፡ ወጽድቆ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ መልአኮ ፡ኅበ ፡ አብ ፡ዓቢይ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ 'ሶበ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ

ቀዳሚ ፡ ዘኀደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ። ወመጽአ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ፤፤ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጤ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ዘርሑቅ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፪መዓልት ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘኢይደልዎ፤፤፤ ፡ ለዓለም፤! ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ምክያድ፤፤ ፡ እምክያደ ፡ እገ ሪሁ ። ወበእንቲአሁ፤!፤ ፡ 'ምድር ፡ ርትዕት ፣፤ ትሁብ፤ ፡ ፍሬሃ ፣ ወበእንቲአሁ ፡ ይወርድ ፡ ጠል ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወፀሓይ ፡ ትሠርቅ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ 'ኃጥኣን ፡ ዙሎሙ፤፡ቦ፣ "ወውእቱ ፡ህየንቴሁ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ጊሩቱ ፡ ይጸውር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው፤፤፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ፤ ፡ ተንሥአ!፤ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጤ ፤ ገዳም ፡ እንዘ ፡ የአምን ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይጸይሕ ፡ ፍኖቶ ፡ ወረከበ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አሰሮሙ ፡ ለአራዊት ፡ ብዙኃን ። ወአንሶሰወ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አሰሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ፤። ፡ እስከ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ በዓቱ ፡ 'ወሰምዖ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዜምር ፡ በውስተ ፡ በዓቱ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ እብነ ፡ ወጐድጐደ ፣ ቦቱ ፡ አንቀጸ ፤ በዓት፤" ። ወብፁዕሰ፤" ፡ አባ ፣ ጳውሊ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዖ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጐደጐድ፤፡" ፡ ሐለየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ እብነ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወአንበሮ ፡ ድኅረ፤፡ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ በዓት ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ጸርኃ ፡ ወይቤ ፤ ኀሠሥኩ ፤ ወተውህበኒ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወሰአልኩ ፡ ወረከብኩ ፡ ጐድጐድኩ ፡ ወአርኃወ፤” ፡ ሊተ ። አሜሃ፤ ፡ አርኅወ፤፤፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወአብኦ ፡ ወተአም ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ በአ ምኀ ፡ መንፈሳዊት ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወነበሩ፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ መኑ ፡ ስምከ ፡ ኦአቡ. ። ወአውሥኦ፤" ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳውሊ ፡ ወይቤሎ" ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ አንተ ፤ ኢታአምር ፡ ስምየ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ታንሶሱ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ገዳም ። ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ አርኃወ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ልቦ ፡ ለእንጦንዮስ ፡ ወይቤ ፣ ብፁዕ ፡ አነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ደለወኒ ፤ ከመ ፡ እነጽር ፣ ጳውሎስ ፡ ዳግማዊ! ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይትናገሩ ፡ በዕበዩ፤" ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ መጽአ፤ ፡ቋዕ፣ በጊዜ ፡ ምሴት ፡ ወወገረ፤ ፡ ሎሙ ፡'አሐተ ፡ ኅብስተ! ፡ ፍጽምተ፤፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፣ አባ ፤ ጳውሊ ፡ ለአባ ፣፤ እንጦንዮስ ፤ ይእዜኬ ፤ አእመርኩ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ አንተ ፣ እምአግብርቲሁ፤ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ነበርኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ገዳም፣፤ እስከ ፡ ዮም ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ይፌኑ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ መንፈቀ ፣ ኅብስተ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ እግዚአብሔር፤ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ለከ ፡ ሲሳየከ ፡ ዮም፤ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ እስከ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ኮከበ፤ ፡ ምዕራብ ። ወእምዝ ፡
heart that he was the first who had dwelt in the desert. The angel arrived at Abbä 'Entonyos and said to him: "In the interior of the desert, which is two days' (journey) away from you, there is a man of one of the footprints of whom the world is not worthy: thanks to him, the earth (which is) just gives its fruits; thanks to him, the dew falls on the earth and the sun rises on all sinners; he himself, in exchange for his perfection, carries the whole human race." When Abbä 'Entonyos heard these words, he arose and went into the interior of the desert, believing that God (was) with him and that He was clearing the way for him. He found the tracks of a man with the tracks of many wild beasts; he walked in the tracks of the saint until he arrived at the cave of the latter, and he heard the saint singing in his cave. The holy Abbä 'Entonyos took a stone and struck the door of the cave with it. When the blessed Abbä Päwli heard him knock, he believed that it was the work of Satan; he took a large stone and placed it behind the door of the cave. When 'Entonyos heard (that), he cried out and said: "I have sought and it has been given to me, I have asked and I have obtained, I have knocked and it has been opened to me." Then (Päwli) opened for him, brought him in, and they embraced with a spiritual embrace, prayed together, and sat down. 'Entonyos said to him (= to Päwli): "What is your name, my father?" The blessed elder Abbä Päwli answered him and said to him: "If you do not know my name, why are you traveling in this desert?" At that moment, God opened the heart of 'Entonyos and (the latter) said: "Happy am I, for I have deserved to see the second Päwlos!" While they were speaking among themselves of the greatness of God, behold, a raven arrived at the time of evening and threw them a whole loaf of bread. Abbä Päwli said to Abbä 'Entonyos: "Now I know that you (are) of the servants of God: behold, I have dwelt in this desert for eighty years until today, God sends me half a loaf of bread every day, and behold, God has sent you your food today." After that, they arose together and prayed until the evening star appeared. Then they sat down and ate; after they had eaten, they arose, stood up, prayed, and glorified God until the morning hour.
ነበሩ፤ ፣ ወበልዑ ፤ ወእምድኅረ ፡ በልዑ ፡ ተንሥኡ ፣ ወቆሙ!" ፡ ወጸለዩ፤ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ፤ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እስከ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ጽባሕ ። "ወሶበ ፤ ርእዩ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ ሠረቀ! ፡ ተአም ፣ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወይቤሎ፣ እንጦንዮስ ፤ ኦአቡየ፤፤፤ ፡ እምአይቴ ፤ ትትሜጠው!" ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፤ በዝንቱ፤ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ዛቲ ፡ ገዳም፤ ። ወአውሥኦ ፡
When they saw (that) the light of the morning had risen, they embraced and 'Entonyos said to him (= to Päwli): "My father, from where do you receive the holy Mysteries during all these days while you dwell in this desert?" The

ቅዱስ ፡፤ አባ ፣፤ ጳውሊ ፡፤ ወይቤሎ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይፌኑ ፡ ሊተ ፤ መልአከ ፣፤ ወይሜጥወኒ፤ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት፤፡ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ለለ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ቀዳሚት ፡ ሰንበት ፣ ወዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ፡፤ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ የዓርግ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ሰማይ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ምስሌየ ፣ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕልየ፤፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ 'እፈቅድ ፤ ታይድዓኒ ፡ እመ ፡ ዝንቱ አስኬማ ፡ ይበዝኅ፤ ፣ ዲበ ፡፣ ምድር ፡ አው ፡ አልቦ ። ወአክሞሰሰ ፡ ብፁዕ ፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ገዓረ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እንጦንዮስ፤ ፡ ርኢኩከ ፡ አክማሰስከ፤፤ ፡ ወተፈሣሕኩ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ገዓርከ ፡ ሐዘንኩ፤። ። ወአውሥአ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳውሊ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አድባ ራት ፡ ወገዳማት ፤ ይከውኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማኅደሪሆሙ ፡፣ ለአርጋብ ፡፤ ወያስተጋብእ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለኅሩያኒሁ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ መካናት ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ስም ፣ ዝውእቱ፤ ኅሩይ ፣ የኃልፍ ፤ ወይከውን፤ ፣ ስሞሙ ፣ መነኮሳተ፤ ፡ ወይትፌ ሥሑ!፤‹ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ወያፈልሶሙ፤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ መዓት፣ ወይትፌሥሑ፤ ። ወእምድኅሬሆሙ ፡ ይትነሥኡ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ ዘኢይሰምዑ ፡ ወኢይትኤዘዙ ፡፤ ለመምህራኒሆሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ይተግሁ ፡ "በሌሊት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ነፍሳቲ ሆሙ፤» ፤። አሜሃ ፡ ይትመዓዕ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አድባራት ፡ ወገዳማት ፡ ወያመዘብሮሙም ፡ እምነ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ዓላዊ!?" ፡ እለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ሕገ!?፤ ። ወይነብሩ፤?፡ ፡ አድባራት ፡ ወገዳማት ፡ ባድወ፤” ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ አላ ፡ ተዝካሮሙሰ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፣ ኢየሐልቅ ፤ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ይወዲ ፡፤ ሣህለ ፡ ውስተ ፤ አልባቢሆሙ ፡ ለካልኣን ፣ ሰብእ ፡ ወየሐውሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አድባራት ፡ ወገዳማት ፡ ወየኃድሩ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ምዕረ ፡ ዳግመ ። ወሰይጣንሰ ፡ ጸላኢ፤ ፣ የሐውር፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አድባራት ፡ ወይሬሲ! ፣ ማእከሌሆሙ ፡ ፀብአ ፡ ወየኃድጉ ፡፤ አስኬማ ፡፤ ምንኩስና ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወይወርዱ ፡ ውስተ!! ፡ ዓለም ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኢረከቡ ፡ ጥዕመተ፤ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ እግዚአብ
holy Abbä Päwli answered him and said to him: "God sends me an angel and (the latter) gives me the holy Mysteries every Saturday and Sunday, after which he ascends to heaven; thus he acts with me throughout my life." 'Entonyos said to him: "I want you to let me know if this monastic habit will multiply on the earth or not." The blessed (Päwli) smiled then groaned. 'Entonyos said to him: "I saw you: you smiled and I rejoiced, and, when you groaned, I was saddened." Abbä Päwli answered and said to him: "These mountains and these deserts will become like the dwellings of doves; God will gather his elect there from everywhere. This name - that is to say "elect" - will pass and their name will become "monks"; they will rejoice for many days, God will transfer them before (his) anger and they will rejoice. After them will arise generations that will not listen nor obey their masters who watch at night for their souls. Then God will be angered against the mountains and the deserts and he will strip them of the impious people who have no law. The mountains and the deserts will remain empty for many days, but the commemoration of the saints will not cease. God will put sweetness in the heart of other men, they will go into the mountains and the deserts and will dwell there a second time. But Satan the Enemy will go into the mountains and will establish war among them, they will abandon the monastic habit and will descend into the world, for they will not find the sweetness of the love of God and will not wait for it with constancy, for this (is) written: By your patience, you will possess your souls." 'Entonyos said to him: "Blessed be this hour during which I have deserved to see your face, my holy father Abbä Päwli!"
ሔር ፤ ወኢተዓገሠ ፡ ላቲ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ! ፡ ጽሑፍ፤ቦ ፡ በትዕግሥትክሙ ፡ ታጠርይዋ ፡ ለነፍስክሙ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ቡርክት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ እንተ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ደለወኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እርአይ ፡ ገጸከ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ቅዱስ፤፤ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳውሊ ። "ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳውሊ፤፤ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ወሖር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ማኅደርከ ፡ ወአምጽእ ፤ ሊተ፤ ፣ ምስሌከ፤ ፣ አልባሰ ፣ ዘአልበሰከ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ አባ ፣ አትናት ዮስ፤ጾ5-356 ፣ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ትግንዝ ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ ባቲ ። ወእንጦንዮስ ፡ አንከረ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ነገሩ ፣ ወበእንተ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ወአምነ ፤ በኩሉ ፤ ትንቢታት ፡ዘተነበየ ፤ ሎቱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳውሊ ፡ አፍጥን ፡ ወኢትቁም ፡ እስመ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፀአትየ፣ ቀርበ ፡ ዘኅቱም፤ጾ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ፤ ፡ እንጦንዮስ፤፤" ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ደንገፀ ፤ ፈድፋደ፤" ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ፡ 'ወአንሶሰወ ፡ ፪መዓልተ ፤ ወ፪ለያልየ!፤ ፡ እስከ ፤ በጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ማኅደሩ ። ወነሥኣ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ልብስ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያንሶሱ፤ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ለማኅበረ፤ ፡ መላእክት ፡
The holy Abbä Päwli said to him: "Arise now, go to your dwelling and (bring back with you for me the garment with which Abbä 'Atnätyos (= Athanasius) the patriarch clothed you, to bury my body with it." 'Entonyos was astonished at his discourse and about the patriarch, and he believed in all the prophecies that he had made to him. The holy Abbä Päwli said to him: "Hasten and do not stop, for the moment of my death is near, (a moment) which (is) assigned to all men." When 'Entonyos heard that, he was very shaken and wept; he left him (= Päwli) and walked for two days and two nights until he had arrived at his dwelling. 'Entonyos took the garment and returned; while he was walking, he saw the assembly of the angels glorifying him (= Päwli) in the air and saying: "Salutation to you, the elect of Christ Abbä Päwli,


[p. 468] {#po.45.p468}
እንዘ ፡ ይሴብሕዎ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ዐየር ፡ ወይብሉ፤ ፡ ሰላም፤ ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦኅሩዩ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳውሊ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መላእክት ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ተሰብሕከ፤ ፣ በሰማያት፤" ። ወኀደገ ፣፤ እምኔከ ፣ ጽልመተ ፡ ወነሥኡከ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ብርሃን ። 'ወኀደገ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ወይወስዱከ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ዘለዓለም፤ ። እስመ፤ ፡ አንተ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ብፁዓዌ ፡ በትውልድከ ፡ "አንተ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አንተ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ዳግማይ?፤" ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ስብሐታተ ፡ ተሀብኡ ፤፤ ። ወይቤ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ለአ
man of God; the angels rejoice with you, for you have been glorified in the heavens. You have cast far from you the darkness and (the angels) have received you into the land of light; you have cast far from you the affliction and they will lead you toward eternal joy, for you, you have been blessed in your generation, you, Päwlos man of God, you second Päwlos." When they had said these glorifications, they disappeared. 'Entonyos said: "(It is) the soul of my father Abbä Päwli that they are taking up to the heavens."
ቡየ ፡ 'አባ ፡ ጳውሊ። ፡ እንዘ ፡ የዓርግዋ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት፤ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ቦአ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓት ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳውሊ ፡ ሰጊዶ ፡ በብረኪሁ ፡ ወበገጹ0 ፡ ወእደዊሁ፥ ፡ ስፉሓት0 ፡ ከመ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ወሰፍሐ" ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ወከደኖ ፡ 'ወበከየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል' ፡ ተዘከረኒ ፤ ኦአቡየ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደርከ ፡ ብርሃናዊት ፡፣ ዘኃደርከ፤፤፤ ፡ ውስቴታ፤፤ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ወገነዘ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ልብስ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ወፈጸመ፤፤፤ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ቀኖና፡፤ ፡ ሕገ፤ ፣ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወጸለየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዞጊዜ ፡ ወአንበበ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ይወንጌላተ፤፤ ፡ በጊዜ ። ወነሥአ ፡ ልብሶ ፡ ዘጸጐረ ፡ በቀልት ፤፤ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ የሐሊ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፣፤ ምስሌሁ ፡ መክሪት ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ፤፤ ፣ ፪አናብስት ፤ ወሰገዱ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳውሊ ፡ ወተአም ኅዎ ፡ ወደንገፀ ፡ ወፈርሀ፤ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ለአናብስት ። ወአናብስትሰ ፡ አድነኑ ፤ ርእሶሙ ፡ ኅበ ፣ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ዘይስእልዎ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ይልሕስዎ ፡ እገ ሪሁ ፤ በከመ ፡ ዘያአምርዎ ፡ እምቀዳሚ" ። "ወኮኑ ፡ ዘይኤምርዎ፡፤ ፡ ኀቤሁ”፡፡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይቤልዎ ፡ አይቴ ፡ ንከሪ፤፤ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወአእመረ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ትእምርቶሙ ፡ ወሰፈረ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ኑኃ፤ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወወሰነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ምድረ ፡ በዓት” ፡ ወከረዩ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ትርአሰ ፡ ወአሐዱ ፡ ትርጋጸ ፡ እስከ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፪በእመት ፡ ወአመረ” ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ፤።" ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ የአክል፤፤ ። ወዓርጉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አናብስት ፡ እምውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይቤልዎ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ለነ ፡ ወተዘከረነ ፡ ወባርክ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ወወፅኡ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፤
Then 'Entonyos entered the cave and found the holy Abbä Päwli prostrate on his knees and face, his arms extended like a cross. He took him, extended him - he (was) as if alive -, covered him, and wept over him saying: "Remember me, my father, in the luminous dwelling where you dwell." Then the holy 'Entonyos arose, shrouded the body in the garment, took a (liturgical) book, and performed over him (= the body) the rites of the Church: he prayed over him three times and read over him three (chapters of the) Gospels in three times. He took the garment of palm fibers and reflected on what he would do with it (= the body), for he did not have a pickaxe with him. Then two lions came to him, prostrated themselves before the body of the holy Abbä Päwli, and kissed him. 'Entonyos trembled and was afraid when he saw the lions. As for the lions, they inclined their heads before 'Entonyos as if they were questioning him, they licked his feet as if they knew him from before and made signs to him as if they were saying to him: "Where shall we dig for him?" 'Entonyos understood their signs, he measured for them the length of the body and marked out the pit for them; they dug, one at the head, the other at the feet, until (reaching) four cubits (long). Abbä 'Entonyos made a sign (to them) and said to them: "That is enough." The lions came up from the tomb and prostrated themselves before Abbä 'Entonyos as if they were saying to him: "Forgive us, remember us, and bless us", and they left him. As for the holy 'Entonyos, he shrouded the body of the holy Abbä Päwli and buried him.
እንጦንዮስ ፡ ገነዘ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳውሊ ፡ ወቀበሮ' ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ነሥአ ፤ መንጸፎ፤፤ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ጳውሊ ፡ ወአልባሶ ፡ ዘጸጐረ ፡ በቀ ልት ፡ በከመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ 'ዘወረሶ ፡ ለአቡሁ፤ ። ወአመረ፤ ፡ መካነ ፡ መቃብሪሁ ፡ ወሖረ፤ ፣ 'ወአንሶሰወ ፡ በእገሪሁ ፡ እምሥራቅ ፡ እስከ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምዕራብ ። ወቦአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፤ ወነገሮ" ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ፤ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ "ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ልብሶ ፡ ለብፁዕ ፡
After that, he took the bed of Abbä Päwli and his garment of palm fibers, like a son who inherits from his father. He marked the location of his tomb, set out, and traveled on foot from the east until he arrived at Alexandria in the west. He entered to Abbä 'Atnäsyos the patriarch and told him the story of the saint. The patriarch took the garment of the blessed Abbä Päwli and placed it near him; he would wear (it) three times a year: during


[p. 470] {#po.45.p470}

the feast of the holy Baptism, during the feast of the resurrection of the Savior, and during the feast of the great Ascension. The patriarch sent priests and men with Abbä 'Entonyos - and with them a chariot - to transport the body of the holy Abbä Päwli. They spent many days traveling through the desert, but did not find the tomb of the saint, for it was hidden from them. The holy Abbä Päwli appeared to the patriarch Abbä 'Atnäsyos and said to him: "Make your men return and let them not tire themselves, for God does not want any of the men to see me until the (second) manifestation of our Lord Jesus Christ." The patriarch dispatched and made the men return.
ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ 'ወሜጦሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ ዕደው ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፤ ይክሥት፤፤ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ልብሱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ጳውሊ ፡ ዘጸጐረ ፡ በቀልት ። ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ሞተ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ወሬዛ፣ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። እመኑኒ ፡ ኦሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ አነ ፡ አቡክሙ፣ አትናስዮስ፡። ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ነሣእኩ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ጸጐረ ፡ በቀልት ፡ ዘለብፁዕ ፡ አባ፣ ጳውሊ ፡ ወአንበርክዋ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ውእቱ ፤ ምውት ፤ ወተንሥአ ፡ ሶቤሃ፤ ። ወአነ ፣ ኮንኩ ፡ ስምዓ ፤ በዝንቱ ፡ አነ ፡ አትናስዮስ፤" ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡፤ በአዕይንትየ ። ወአነ ፡ ኤስድሮስ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ኮንኩ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በዝንቱ ። አነ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዘተሠየምኩ ፡ እምእዴሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አትናስዮስ‹ ፡ ኮንኩ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ነገር"፤ ፡ ወጸሐፍኩ ፡ በእዴየ ፣ ውስቴቱ ። 'ወስምዖሙ ፡ ለ፪ዕደው ፡ አው ፡ሾዞእሙን ፡ ውእቱ" ። ወተሰምዐ ፡ 'ዜናሃ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ትእምርት፡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወአንከሩ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ገባሬ ፡መንክራት ፡ በላዕለ ፡ ቅዱሳኒሁ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረ ከቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ አባ ፡ ቡላ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ገዳማዊያን ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡
One day, God wanted to reveal the glory of the garment of palm fibers of the holy Päwli; at that moment, a young Christian man died in the city of Alexandria. "Believe me, people of the Christians, I, your father 'Atnäsyos: I took the garment of palm fibers of the blessed Abbä Päwli and placed it on the dead man, and (the latter) resurrected immediately. I, I was a witness of that, I 'Atnäsyos, I saw that with my eyes. - I, 'Ésderos (= Isidore) the bishop, I was a witness of that. - I, 'Entonyos (= Anthony), the first priest to have been ordained by the hand of Abbä 'Atnäsyos, I was a witness of these words (written) and, with my hand, I subscribed to them. And the testimony of two or three men is truthful." The story of this miracle was known throughout the land of Egypt and (in) the city of Alexandria. All the people were astonished and glorified God, He who does wonders through the intermediary of his saints. May the prayer and the blessing of this great saint Abbä Bulä, the chief of the hermits, be with us forever, amen.

[On this day also, Tomäs (= Thomas) showed a miracle when he went to preach where God had commanded him: having approached to enter (there) at two miles (from a city), he moved a little away from the road and saw by chance a dead young man - his appearance (was) very beautiful. The apostle said: "Lord, is it for this that you brought me here, so that I might see this scandal? However, may your will be done." Having said that, he prayed much for the dead man and, then, a serpent came out from beside a stone, striking the ground with its tail, and it cried out with a loud voice saying: "What have I to do with you, apostle of Christ? Indeed, you have come to correct my work." The apostle said to him: "Yes, speak then." The serpent said: "A beautiful

woman was beside a vineyard, I saw her and loved her. After that, I discovered this young man embracing her, and he united with her on Sunday - there is no need for me to expose before you all the evil that he did, for I know that you (are) the twin of Christ; because of that, I killed him." Satan who (was) in the serpent told the apostle everything he was doing against the children of men. After that, the apostle charged him with imprecations by the name of Jesus so that he would withdraw (his) venom from the man: immediately, the serpent swelled, broke, and died. As for the young man, he rose up with a leap, embraced the feet of the apostle, and was healed. In this place, the apostle made the people believe, and he

built a church for him. After that, the apostle entered the city with this young man whom he had resurrected from the dead and, while he was standing and speaking with the people in this place, a donkey foal arrived, stood before him, opened its mouth, and said: "O follower of Christ and apostle of the Most High, (you) who know what is hidden and (are) associated in the ministry with the son of God, come, return toward God who sent you; mount on me and rest yourself until you have entered the city." Upon hearing (that), the apostle was greatly astonished; he glorified God and said to the donkey foal: "Of what race (are) you, that you expose such mysteries?" The donkey foal said to him: "I (am) of the lineage of the one who served Bala'äm (= Balaam), the one from whom (descends) in this generation my father upon (whom) your Lord and your master mounted. I too, I have been sent now so that you might rest and mount on me." The apostle refused to mount (on him). When the donkey foal had beseeched him much, the apostle mounted on him and arrived at the gates of the city while a numerous people followed him; he descended from him and said to him: "Go, take care of yourself where you will go." At that very moment, (the donkey foal) fell and died; those who saw (that) were frightened and said to the apostle: "Raise him up and give him life." He said to them: "As for me, I could raise him up by the power of my Lord and my God, but surely this is better (thus)." Then he ordered them to dig the earth and bury him. Then he gave them the salutation and left them. May his holy blessing be with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus in the centuries of centuries.] [READING] OF 3 YAKKATIT In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father (and) ascetic Abbä Yä'qob (= James) the monk. This saint despised this world from his childhood and dwelt in a cave for fifteen years, leading a (spiritual) combat in a

ሌሊተ" ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ ጽርዓት ። ወኢወፅአ ፡ እምበዐት ፡፤ ወኢቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር መጠነ ፡ ፲ወ፳ዓመት ፡ ወኢርእየ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ግሙራ ። ወተመክነዩ?‹ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እኩያን ፡ 'እምእለ ፤ ይተልውዎ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ፤! ፡ ወረሰይዋ ፡ ለአሐቲ ፡፤ ዘማዊት፤፤ ፡ በጽሐት፤ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡'ወቦአት ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐቱ! ፡ ወተንሥአት፤፡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ! "ወኮነት ፡ ትትዋነይ፤ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወትጹውዖ!፤" ፡ ለገቢረ ፡ ኃጢአት ። ወገሠጻ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ወአዘከራ ፡ በእሳተ ፡ ገሃነም ፡ ወበኩነኔ ፡ ዘለዓለም! ፡ ወነስሐት ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወተጋደለት ፡ ወአስመረቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ።
prolonged fast, a permanent prayer, while watching at night without rest. He did not go out of the cave nor enter the city for fifteen years, and he never saw the face of a woman. Evil people among those who follow the devil plotted against the saint and brought a prostitute to the saint; she entered his cave, stood against him and began to make lascivious gestures before him and to invite him to commit sin. The just man reprimanded her and reminded her of the fire of hell and the eternal punishment. She repented through his intermediary, fought (spiritually), and pleased God.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተመክነዩ፤፤ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ወለተ፣ አሐዱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡፣ እምልሂቃነ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ ወአውደቃ ፡ ወአንገርገራ ። ወወደየ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ፈውሶታ ፡ ዘእን በለ ፤ ያዕቆብ ፡ 'መነኮስ ፡ ዘውስተ?" ፡ በዐት ። ወነሥኣ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወአብጽሓ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'ወሰአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈውሳ ፡ በጸሎቱ ። ወጸለየ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ወተፈወሰት” ። ወፈርሀ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሥአ፥ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወኢይትመየጥ?፥፤ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወኀደጋ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወኀደገ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ እሃ፥፤ ፡ "ሕፃነ ፡ ንኡሰ ፡ ወሖረ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አኃዘ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ይፃብኦ ፡ ባቲቶ፤ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ወበመዓልት ፡ እስከ ፡ አውደቆ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ በኃጢአት ። 'ወሶበ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ በኃጢአት ፡ ፈርሀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትከሠት ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር" ፡ ወኢይትሀፈር፥ጾ5 ፡ 'ወኢይቅትልዎ ፡ በእንቲ አሃ፥ ፣ ወቀተላ ፡ ወቀተለ" ፡ እሃ ። ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ወደየ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ፣ ቅብጸተ፤ ፡ ተስፋ ። ወወፅአ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ በዓት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወተሣሀለ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዘኢይፈቅድ ፡ ሞቶ ፡ ለኃጥእ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ መነኮሰ ፡ ጸድቀ ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ እንዘ ፡ 'የሐውር ፡ ወተቀበሎ ፡ ወተኣምኖ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ትኩዘ ፡ ወሕዙነ ፡ ተስእሎ። ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ምንት ፡'ዘበጽሐከ ፡ ወምንት ፡ ያቴክዘከ ። ወአይድዖ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ 'ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምኔሁ፤ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ይእቲ፤ ብአእሲት ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ቀተላ ፡ ወቀተለ" ፡ እሃ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ጸድቅ ፤ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ወአጽንፅዕ ፡ ልበከ ፡ ወኢትምትር" ፡ ተስፋከ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፤ መሀሪ ፤ ወመስተሣህል ። ወእምዝ ፡ አንበረ፤ ፡ ላዕሌሁ" ፡ ጾመ ፡ ወጸሎታተቶ፥፤ ፡ ወሰጊደ ። ወተመይጠ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ግብ ፡ ወሞቅሐ፣፡ ርእሶ ፤ ውስቴቱ ፡ ወተዓገሠ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ጸማ ፡ 'ወዓቢይ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወጾም ፡ ወትረ ወጸሎታት ፡ ወትረ ፡ ወትጋህ ፡ ብዙኅ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ ሣዕረ ፡ ወብቁላተ፣ ምድር ። ወኮነ ፡ የሐዝን ፡ በልቡ፤ ፡ ወይቴክዝ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ ይትወከፈኒ ፡
After that, the demons plotted against him: (one of them) entered the daughter of a man among the chiefs of the people (of the city), struck her down, and made her roll (on the ground). Satan put into the heart of her father that no one could heal her except Yä'qob, the monk who (was) in the cave. The father took her, brought her to the saint, and asked him to heal her by his prayer. (Yä'qob) prayed over her and she was healed. Her father feared to take her (back) immediately and that (thus) Satan would return upon her; he left her with the saint, left with her the brother of the latter, a small child, and went away. After that, Satan began to attack him (= Yä'qob) with her night and day until he made him fall into sin with her. When (Yä'qob) fell into sin with her, he feared that this thing would be unveiled, that he would be covered with shame, and that they would kill him because of her (= the girl), and he killed her and killed her brother. Immediately Satan put despair into his heart; (Yä'qob) went out of the cave to go into the world. He who does not want the death of the sinner had pity on him; he sent him a just monk (who) found him on the way while he was walking, came to meet him, and greeted him. When (this monk) saw him saddened and afflicted, he questioned him and said to him: "What has happened to you and what is it that saddens you?" (Yä'qob) informed him of everything that had happened to him, how he had fallen (into sin) with the woman and how he had killed her and had killed her brother. The just monk said to him: "Do not be afraid, make your heart strong and do not abdicate your hope, for our Lord (is) pitiful and merciful." Then he imposed on him fasting, prayers, and prostrations. (Yä'qob) returned, entered a cave, shut himself in, and endured the pain, great miseries, fasting constantly, prayers constantly, and many vigils. He ate grass and plants; he afflicted himself in his heart and was saddened. He said: "Will God receive me and forgive me my fault?"
እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወየኃድግ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አበሳየ ። ወነበረ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ንስሓ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ። ወፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ 'ሎቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተወክፈ ፡ ንስሓሁ ።
He remained thus fighting (spiritually) in a great repentance for many years and God wanted to show him that He had accepted his


[p. 476] {#po.45.p476}
ወአምጽአ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ረኀበ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዓመት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እስመ ፡ ረኃብ ፡ ኢየሐ ልፍ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ግብ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ኤሊስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወነሥኦሙ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ለካህናት ፡ ወለሕዝበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ሀገር ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸሊ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ 'ከመ ፡ ይምሐሮሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር”» ፡ ወዓበየ” ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አነ ፡ ኃጥእ ፣ ወአባሲ"፤ ፡ ወአምዓዕክዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በኃጢአትየ ። ወነገሮ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ወአሜሃ ፡ ተአዘዘ ፡ ለኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ወወፅአ" ፡ ወሖረ” ፡ "ምስለ ፡ ሕዝብ” ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ በብካይ 0፡ እስመ ፡ ሐለዩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወኮነ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ በብካይ ፡ ወይቤ ፤ እፎ ፡ እከሥት ፡ አፉየ ፡ ርኩስተ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ቅድሳቲከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ኣሌዕል ፡ ገጽየ ፡ ዘጸልመ ፡ በብዝኃ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ቅድሳቲከ ። ወጸለየ ፡ ነዋኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፤ ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ዘንመ ፡ ዝናም ፡ ብዙኅ ። ወአእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተወክፈ ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ወንስሓሁ ፡ ወኃደገ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ኃጢአቶ” ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አመክዓበ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ይገብር ፡ 'እምጾም ፡ ወጸሎት ፡ ወትጋህ ፡ ወሰጊድ" ። "ወኮነ ፡ ይጌሥጽ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ተጋደል ፡ ወተዓቀብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትደቅ ፡ ዳግመ፤። ወፈጸመ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ በርሥዕና ፡ ሠናይ ፣ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፤
repentance: God brought a great famine upon the city at that time. God said to the bishop of the city that the famine would not disappear except by the prayer of Yä'qob, the monk who (was) in a cave. The bishop then arose, took with him the priests and the people of the city, and they arrived at the holy Yä'qob; they asked him to pray for them so that God would have pity on them. (Yä'qob) refused and said: "I (am) a sinner, I have sinned and I have angered God by my sin." The bishop told him how God had spoken to him about him (= Yä'qob). Then (Yä'qob) obeyed the bishop, he went out, went with the people, and prayed [saying with tears: "They think that Yä'qob is a man." His prayer was with tears and he said: "How can I open my impure mouth before your holiness, Lord, and how can I lift toward the heaven of your holiness my face which has been blackened by the greatness of (my) sin?" He prayed for a long time] and, before he had finished his prayer, an abundant rain fell. He knew that God had accepted his prayer and his repentance and had forgiven him his sin. After that, he redoubled what he was doing in terms of fasting, prayer, vigil, and prostration. He exhorted himself and said: "Fight and guard yourself so as not to fall again." He finished his days in a beautiful old age, pleased God, and died in peace. May God have pity on us by his prayer and may his blessing be with us, amen.
በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ፍልሰተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤፍሬም ፡ ሶርያዊ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን ።
On this day also (there is) the commemoration of the translation of the body of the holy Abbä ‘Éfrèm (= Ephrem) the Syrian. May his prayer and his blessing be with us in the centuries of centuries, amen and amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ እብሎይ" ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ባሕታውያን ፡ እምትሩፋቲሁ ፡ ዘሠርፀ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሃይማኖት ። እምአመ ፡ ገብአ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘለእስክንድርያ ፣ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘቂሳርያ ፡ ወቤቱ ፤ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘአቡቂር ። ወአኃዙ ፡ ይትናገሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ዘሀለዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡-ምኔታት ፡ዘግብጽ ፡ይቤ ፡አብ፣ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ሀሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ባስልዮስ። ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ አሞኒ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ በዘከመዝ ፡ ነገር ፡ ወይፈቅዱ ፡ ለጠይቆ ፡ ርአየ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናቴዎስ" ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ለየካቲት ፡ ጊዜ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ዐባየ ፡ ዘትበጽሕ ፡ እስከ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወአዕጹቂሃ ፡ ይበጽሑ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ያጸልሉጾ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ አዕጹቂሃ ፡ ወታቦት ፡ ማእከሌሃ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይዴመም ፡ እምራእይ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተናገር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ባስልዮስ ። 'ወርእየ ፣ ባስልዮስ" ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ፤ ፡ ለዕፅ ፡ ወለምሥዋዕ ፡ ወለመላእክት ። ወተርጐመ ፡ ሎሙ ፣ ሚካኤል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ዘርኢክሙ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አባ ፡
[On this day also there is the death of Abbä 'Ebloy (= Apollon?), chief of the solitaries, by whose virtues the fruit of the faith grew. After 'Atnatëwos (= Athanasius), patriarch of Alexandria, had returned, Bäselyos (= Basil), bishop of Qisäryä (= Caesarea), came to him, and both spent the night in the church of 'Abugir (= Aboukir). They began to converse about the saints who were in the monasteries of Egypt. The father patriarch said: "There is Abbä Päkuemis (= Pachomius)." Bäselyos said: "(And) Abbä 'Entonyos (= Anthony) and Abbä 'Amoni (= Amoni)." While they were in this conversation and wanted to be informed (exactly), Abbä 'Atnätëwos saw, on the fifth of Yakkätit, in the middle of the night, a great tree which touched the river and (whose) branches touched the heaven; many people were sheltering under its branches and an altar (was) in its middle. While ('Atnätëwos) was marveling at (this) vision, Mikä'el (= Michael) descended from heaven and said to him: "Speak of this with Bäselyos." And Bäselyos had seen as 'Atnätëwos had seen the tree, the altar, and the angels. Mikä'ël gave them the explanation by saying: "The tree that you saw (is) the monastery of Abbä


[p. 478] {#po.45.p478}
እብሎይ”። ፡ ዘይትሐነጽ ፡ በደወለ ፤ ግብጽ ፤ ወአዕጹቅኒ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወምሥዋዕኒ ቤተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ዘይሔውጽዋ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ውእቱ ፤ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለጴጥሮስ "በእንተዝ ፣ ይስዕር ፡ መናፍስተፆ ።
'Ebloy which has been built on the territory of Egypt; the branches (are) the monks and the altar (is) the house of God that the angels visit, it is the image of Pêtros (= Peter); because of that, it destroys the spirits."
ወሰምዓ ፡ ዜና ፡ አባ ፡ እብሎይ” ፡ ፳ሀቢይ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፣፤ ወአስተብ ሞዮ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ" ፡ በረከቶ ። ወፈነዎ ፡ አብ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ለሀቢ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሂመላእክት ፣ ዘውእቶሙ ፡'መነኮሳት ፡ አባ ፡ ኤስድሮስ ፡ ወዮሐንስ ፡ ሐጺር ፡ ወአባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ 'ወአባ ፡ አሞኒ ፡ ወፊቅጦር ፡ ወአግሮኒኮስ፤"" ፣ ወአባ ፡ ክሉናስ፤‹፤ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ አብ ፤ ወሖረ ፡ ወተራከቦሙ ፡ በፍሥሓ ። ወርእስሰ፤'= ፡ ዘመጽአ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አኃው ፡ ዘአሐቲ ፡ ዓይኑ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተኣምኖ ፡ ለአባ ፡ እብሎይ፤ ፡ ተከሥተ ፡ ዓይኑ ፡ ወጸርሐ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦኮከብ ፡ ብሩህ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ዓለም ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ሀቢ ፡ ለአባ ፡ እብሎይ፤" ፡ ብእሲትየ ፡ ትጻሙ ፡ በለምጽ ፡ ወይእቲ ፤ ታሕተ ፡ አስኬማከ ፡ ወይርከባ ፡ ጸጋከ ፡ እንተ ፡ ረከበኒ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አባ ፡ እብሎይ፤ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ላቲ ፡ ፈውስ ፡ ወረከበት ፡ ጥዒና ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ቆመ ፡ ማእከለ ፣ መነኮሳት ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ፳88እምኔክሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ 'ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ!፤የ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያለብ ወኒ ፡ ንብረተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወሶበ ፡ ሠረቀ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስ ቶስ ፡ ወአርድእቲሁ ፡ ወመላእክቲሁ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፤ አርአዮሙ፤‹ ፡ መሠረተ ፡ ሕንፄሃ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወመዓዶሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ ፍጹማነ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወይንበሩ ፡ በተፋቅሮ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይምዕዶሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተወለጠ ፡ገጹ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይነድድ ፡ ኩለንታሁ ፡ ወብርሃን ፡ ያንጸበርቅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ከመ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈራህነ ፡ ይቤለነ ፣ ኦውሉድየ ፡ ኢትፍርሁ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እትፋነወክሙ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሒሎ ፡ ተመሥጠት ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ወአፄነውነ ፡ ፄና ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ተከሥተ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወይቤ ፡፤ እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ርድ አኒ ፡ ወተወከፍ ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ ኀቤከ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፣፡ መጠወ፤" ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
A prefect of the city of Alexandria learned the story of Abbä 'Ebloy and beseeched the patriarch to receive his (= 'Ebloy's) blessing. The father patriarch sent the latter to the prefect with seven messengers, that is to say the monks Abbä 'Ésderos (= Isidore), Yohannes (= John) the Little, Abbä Besoy (= Bsoi), Abbä 'Amoni, Figtor (= Victor), 'Agronigos, and Abbä Klunäs. The father ('Ebloy or 'Atnätëwos) arose, set out, and met them with joy. The officer who had come with the brothers (monks) was one-eyed. When he greeted Abbä 'Ebloy, his (blind) eye was opened and he cried out saying: "Luminous star who (is) in the world!" After that, the prefect said to Abbä 'Ebloy: "My wife suffers from leprosy and she wears your monastic garment. May your grace which has touched me touch her (also)." Abbä 'Ebloy said: "May healing come to her", and (the wife) recovered her health. One day, ('Ebloy) stood in the middle of the monks and said to them: "Let each of you be present here, for our Savior is going to come with his disciples to make known to me the site (where to build) the church." When the light rose, Jesus Christ came - and with him his disciples and his angels - (and) he showed them (= to the monks) where to found the construction of the church. ('Ebloy) exhorted his (spiritual) children to be accomplished in every beautiful action and to dwell in mutual love. While he was making this exhortation to them, his face changed, his whole being was flaming, and the light was bursting on his face like (on that of) an angel of God. "As we were seized with fear (says the narrator), he said to us: 'My children, do not be afraid, I am taking leave of you.' When he had said that, his soul was taken and we smelled a good odor. Then his eyes opened and he said: 'My Lord Jesus Christ, help me and receive my soul to you.' Having said that, he delivered his soul into the hand of God." May his prayer and his blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu in the centuries of centuries.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ዘይኑን ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፤ ሖረ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ዘኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸሊ ፡ ወወዩኦ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኢማሐሑስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተኣምተ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዜያነዉ ፡ ፅበያቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እስከ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፪ሰዓት ። ወሀሎ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ብእሲ ፡ መሃይምን ፡ ዘይሔወጾ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ በኅብስት ፡ ወማይ ። ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይወስድ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሲሳዮ ፡ 'ነገርዎ ፤ ዘከመ፤! ፣ ሞተ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወነሥኦ ፡ ወጠብለሎ ፡ በልብስ ፡ ወወደዮ ፡ ውስተ ፤ መሣይምት፤!" ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ መልዕልቴሁ ፡ ወብእሲቱኒ ፡ ትተልጾ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ረከቦ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዘይኑን ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወአንበረ ፡ መሣይምተ ፣፤ በቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ወሰአሎሙ ፡ 'ይጸልዩ ፡
[On this day also died Zaynun (= Zeno), the miracle-worker. When he had gone into the temple of Jerusalem to pray, this saint, (having) gone out of it, went to 'Imähus (= Emmaus) to a holy elder. After having greeted each other, they remained telling the greatness of God until the time of the seventh hour. There was in this city a believing man who visited the elder with bread and water. One day, while he was bringing his food to him, he was told that his son had died. He took him, wrapped him in a garment, put him in a basket, and placed the elder's food on top - his wife was following him. When he arrived at the elder, he found Abbä Zaynun with him. He placed the basket before them and asked them to pray over it. (The two monks) said to each other "Pray, you", and the elder obliged Abbä Zaynun to pray. (The latter) began


[p. 480] {#po.45.p480}
ላዕሌሁ፤፤፤ ። ወተባሀሉ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ አንተ ፡ ጸሊ ፡ ወአገበሮ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዘይኑን ፤ ከመ ፡ ይጸሊ ። ወወጠነ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አኃዜ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም፤፤ ተራኅራኅ ፡ ወመሐር ፡ እሎንተ ፡ እለ ፡ አቅረቡ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፤ ፍሬያቲሆሙ ፡ ለአግብርቲከ ፡ ነዳያን ። ወምስለ ፡ ተፍጸሜተ ፡ ቃሉ፤! ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ምውት ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ወበከየ ፤ ወከልሐ፤፤ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መሣይምት ። ወአሜማሃ ፡ ተነጽሐ ፡ አበ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እግረ ፡ አባ ፡ ዘይኑን ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ በእንተ ፤ ዘኢነገርኩከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ወይእዜኒ ፡፤ ተንሥአ ፡ በጸሎትከ ። ወፈርሃ ፡ አባ ፡ ዘይኑን ፤ እምውዳሴ ፡ ሰብእ ፤ ወይቤሎ!! ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ኢትክሥት ፡ ለመኑሂ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ እመውት ፡ ወጐየ ፡ እምህየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይብላዕ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፤ ኀበ ፡ ማኅደሩ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ አፅረፈ ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡
(his) prayer saying: "Lord, master of all the universe, be charitable and merciful toward these who bring their first fruits to your indigent servants." At the end of his discourse, the little dead child resurrected, wept, and cried in the basket. Then the father of the child threw himself at the feet of Abbä Zaynun and said to him: "Forgive me, my father, for not having told you that this son of mine was dead. Now, he is resurrected thanks to your prayer." Abbä Zaynun was afraid of the praise of men and said to him: "My son, do not reveal (this) to anyone until I am dead." Then he fled from there without eating and returned to his dwelling. After a great (spiritual) combat, he died. May his intercession be with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus in the centuries of centuries.]

[READING] OF 4 YAKKATIT In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day the holy apostle 'Agäbos (= Agabus) became a martyr. Our Lord chose this disciple among the seventy-two disciples whom he chose and sent to preach before his passion. He was filled with the Holy Spirit the Paraclete when he was with the twelve apostles in the upper room of Seyon (= Sion). God gave him the gift of prophecy, just as the book of the Acts of the Apostles says about him: He took the belt of Päwlos (= Paul) and bound his feet and head with it and said: "Thus says the Holy Spirit: The man who possesses this belt, the Jews will bind him thus in Jerusalem." This prophecy was fulfilled, then he preached with the apostles the life-giving Gospel. He went into many cities, professing and guiding (the people) in the way of God; he converted many Jews and Greeks, made them enter the way of God, our Lord Christ, and purified them with the holy (and) life-giving Christian baptism. The Jews seized him (who was) alone in Jerusalem and made him undergo a great (and) painful scourging. Then they put a rope around his neck, dragged him to the outside of the city, and stoned him until he gave up the soul. A light then descended from heaven upon him, and the light appeared like a column (going) from his body to heaven; the whole crowd was looking at it. God opened the heart of a Jewish woman in whom there was neither hatred, nor cunning, nor jealousy like (in the) cursed Jews. She was observing the law of the Torah. She said: "This man is just, that is why the light has descended upon him", and she began to exalt God and to cry out; she said:

ላዕሌሁ ፡ ብርሃን ። "ወኮነት ፡ ታዓብዮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወትጸርሕ ፡ ወትቤ ፡ አነ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፡ ኣአምን ፡ በአምላኩ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወወገርዋ ፡ በአእባን። ፣ ወአዕፅረፈት ፣፤ወተቀብረት፤" ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ 8መቃብር ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ፣ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
"I am a Christian, I believe in the God of this righteous man!" They stoned her, she died, and was buried with him (= Agabus) in one and the same tomb. May their prayer and their blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen.

[On this day also died Abba Zacharias (= Zacharias) of virtuous actions and great (spiritual) combat. At the moment when his soul was leaving his body, Abba Moses (= Moses) said to him: "My brother, what do you see?" (Zacharias) said to him: "Silence is better for me." When his soul departed, Abba Isidore (= Isidore) saw the heaven opened and said to him: "Rejoice, my son Zacharias, for behold, the door of the kingdom of heaven is opened for you!" And he died thus. May God have mercy through his prayer upon our king Iyasu for ever and ever.]

[READING] FOR 5 YAKKATIT In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father Agrippinus (= Agrippinus), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarchs, he is the tenth. This father feared God, was holy and pure; he was a priest in the city of Alexandria. When Abba Celadion (= Celadion) died, the patriarch who was (before) him, the people of the city chose this father and instituted him patriarch. When he was instituted at the head of the Church of God on the seat of Mark (= Mark) the Evangelist, he led a virtuous life like the apostles, preaching and teaching the faith of Christ and his life-giving law, watching with his eyes over his people and guarding his flock. He did not acquire a single piece of silver nor a single piece (of gold), but (only) that with which he nourished himself because of hunger and that with which he covered his body because of cold and heat. He read the Scriptures continually to his people and instructed them, the great as well as the small: he watched and prayed for all. He spent twelve years in this (spiritual) combat, pleased God, and died in peace. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
ሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢሶይ፤ ፡ ዘተሠምየ፤ ፡ ጴጥ ሮስ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፤ እምላዕላይ ፣፤ ግብጽ ፡ እምሀገረ፤ ፡ አክሚም ። ወኮነ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ውርዙቱ ፡ ይገብር ፤ ምግባራተ ፡ ሕሠማተ ፡፤ ወይትፈጋዕ፤ ፣ በመብልዕ ፡ ወበመስቴ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኅረዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ዕፁበ ፡ ወቀርበ ፡ ለመዊት ። ወመሰጡ" ፡ ነፍሶ፤ ፣፡ ወአርአይዎ ፡ መካናተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ 'ወዓዘቅተ ፤
On this day also died the holy father Abba Bsoy (= Bsoy) who is called Peter (= Peter). This saint was from Upper Egypt, from the city of Akhmim (= Akhmim). In the time of his youth, he did shameful actions and rejoiced in food and drink. When God chose him, he brought upon him a painful illness, and (Bsoy) was near to death. (The angels)


[p. 484] {#po.45.p484}
ማዕምቅት ፡ ዓቢይ፤፤ ። ወርእየ ፡ በህየ ፡ ፅደወ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይለብሱ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ብሩሃ ፡ ወበእደዊሆሙ ፡ በድነ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወፈለጥዎ ፡ ፪ክፍለ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለቢሶይ፡ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዘይሰርቅ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ገዓረ ፡ እምው ስተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ፡ ወተመይጠት ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወአንሥአ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሰማይ ፤ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ወአምላኪየ" ፡ ለእመ ፡ አሕየውከኒ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ደዌ" ፡ አነ፤ ፡ እኔስሕ ፡ እምኃጢአት ፡ ወኣመልከከ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ወኢይሬኢ ፡ ለብእሲት ፡ እምይእዜ ፡ "በዓይን ፡ እኪት ፡ ግሙራ፥፤ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አሕ የዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር?፥" ፡ እምደዌሁ ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንወይጥ ፡ ወአመከርዎ፥ ፡ ወአልበስዎ ፡ አልባሰ ፣ ምንኩስና ፡፤ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡፤ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወበሰጊድ ፡ ወትጋህ ፡ 'ብዙኃ ፣ ዓመታተቶ" ፡ እስከ ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡፤ ዜናሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወተለዓለ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ብዙኃን፥። ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወደ ረሰ ፡ ድርሳናተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወተግሣጸተ ፡ ወትምህርታተ?ቶ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ወለሕዝባዊ ያን ። ወጾመ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ጊዜ ፣ ፪አውራኅ ፣፡ ወኢበልዓ ፡ ኅብስተ" ፡ ወኢሰትየ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ መጠነ፤ ፡ ወ፳ዓመት ፡እንዘ ፡ ይቀውም ፣ "በመዓልት ፤ ወበሌሊት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ምግባሮሙ ፡ 'ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፣፡ ክሥት ፣ ቅድሜሁ ፡ እመሂ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ ኃጢአት ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሐመ ፡ ንስ ቲተ፣፤ ወአሥመሮ፣ለእግዚአብሔር ፡፡ወአዕረፈ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ
took his soul and made him see the places of punishment and the pit of a deep abyss. There, he saw men clothed in luminous garments; in their hands (there was) the corpse of a man, they were dividing it into four parts. They said to Bsoy: "Thus is it done to whoever steals the property of people." When he heard this, (Bsoy) groaned from the bottom of his heart, shed bitter tears, and his soul returned into him; he lifted his eyes to heaven and said: "My Lord and my God, if you heal me of this illness, I will repent of sin, I will adore you with all my heart, and, from now on, will never again look at a woman with a dishonest eye." Immediately, God healed him of his illness; he rose and went to the monastery of Pleuit (= Pleuit); (the monks) tested him and clothed him with the monastic habit. He led a great (spiritual) combat in fasting, in prayer, and in prostration and vigil for many years, so much so that his fame spread throughout the land of Egypt. He was raised above many saints; he composed many homilies, exhortations, and teachings for the monks and for the laity. Once, he fasted for four months and did not eat bread nor drink water. He remained fighting thus (spiritually) for thirty-five years, being standing day and night. The actions of all men, whether just act or sin, were revealed to him. After that, he had a small illness, pleased God, and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኖብ ፡ በዓለ ፣ መነስነሳት ፡ ዘወርቅ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ ፡ 'ዘአምሳለ ፡ መላእክት ። ወናሁ ፡ ጸሐፍነ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ ወ፳ጸለጥቅምት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ፍልሰተ ፣ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ አረጋዊያን ፣ ጓወ፪ሰማዕታት፤፤ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አቡሊዳስ" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀ ገረ ፤ ሮሜ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also died the holy father Abba Nob, the master of the golden fan. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. On this day also died the holy father Abba Apollon (= Apollon) who (was) in the likeness of the angels. Behold, we have written his history on the twenty-fifth of Tegemt. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. On this day also (there is) the translation of the bodies of the holy fathers, the forty-nine elders (who were) martyrs in the desert of Scetis (= Scetis), in the monastery of the holy Abba Macarius (= Macarius). May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen. On this day also died the holy father Hippolytus (= Hippolytus), patriarch of the city of Rome (= Rome). May his prayer and his blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen.


[p. 486] {#po.45.p486}
ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣፡ አብሎ ፡ ኖላዊ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ "ኮነ ፡ ኖላዌ ፡ አባግዕ፤ ፡ ወወሀበ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ እምውርዙቱ" ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይዜሙ ፡ ወይሰርቅ ፣ ወይቀትል ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ወኢኀደገ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምኃጣውእ ፡ አላ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ኃጢአተ፤ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ጓዓመተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይፌጽም፡ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ ፈቃደ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ በመንፈቀ ፡ መዓልት ፡ ወይፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅርጽ ፡ ጸጐረ ፡ አባግዒሁ ፡ ወወደየ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አነ ፡ እምውርዙትየ ፡ እኤብስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡፤ ወፈጸምኩ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘአዘዘኒ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወኢኀደጉ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምኃጢ አተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ 'ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘገበርኩ ፡ አላ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ተረፈተኒ፣ እፈቅድ ፡፤ ከመ ፡ እስጥክ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ ብአሲት ፡ ፅንስት ፡ ወእሬኢ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ እፎ ፡ ይሰክብ ፡ 'ወልዳ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሣ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይሔሊ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ፅንስት ፡ ዘአልጸቀት ፡ ለወሊድ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ታንሶሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም፣ በጊዜ ፡ ሐሩር?" ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእያ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ሶቤሃ?፤ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ ሥዕርተ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ ወነጽሐ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ መጥባሕቶ ፣ ወሠጠቀ ፡ ከርሣ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ሕፃነ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ እፎ ፡ ስኩበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሣ?" ። 'ወእምድኅረ ፡ ንስቲት ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ሞተ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ወእሙ ፡ በጸማ ፡ ዓቢይ” ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኖላዊ ፣ ዓቢየ ፡ ኃጢአተ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሰጠጠ ፡ አልባሲሁ ፡ ወወደየ ፡ መሬተ” ፡ ዲበ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ወጸርሐ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አሌሊተ ፡
On this day also died the holy father Abba Apollon (= Apollon) the shepherd. This saint was a shepherd of sheep; he gave himself to Satan from his youth. He fornicated, stole, killed people: he disdained no sin, but committed all sins until he had reached forty years; he accomplished all the will of Satan. One day, while he was in the countryside in the middle of the day and wanted to shear the wool of his sheep, Satan put (an idea) into his heart and (Apollon) said: "Behold, I have committed faults from my youth until now; I have accomplished everything that Satan ordered me and I have not left any sin of this world without committing it, but one sin remains for me: I want to split the belly of a pregnant woman and I will see how her child is lying in her belly." While he was thinking of this in his heart, behold (there appeared) a pregnant woman, who was near to giving birth, walking in the countryside at the time of the great heat. When he saw her, he rose immediately without pity, seized her hair, threw her to the ground, took his knife, split her belly, and saw how the child was lying in her belly. After a few moments, the child died, and her mother (died) in great pains. When the shepherd saw the great sin that he had committed, he was very afflicted, tore his clothes, put dust on his head, cried, shed bitter tears, and said: "Woe to me, for I have committed a great sin that will never be forgiven me!"
እስመ ፡ ገበርኩ ፡ ኃጢአተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ዘኢይትኃደግ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ኃደገ ፡ አባግዒሁ ፡ ግዱፋነ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ በትሮ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወአንሶሰወ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ” ፡ እስከ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ። ወኢቦአ ፡ ኀበ ፡ 'አረጋዊያን ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወኢነገሮሙ ፡ ዘገብረ" ። አላ፤ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጤ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ርሑቅ ፡ እምነ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፲ምዕራፍ ። ወኢኮነ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ዘይበልዕ" ፡ ወኢኃደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደር ፡ አላ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አራዊት ፡ ወይሰቲ ፡ ማየ ፡ ምስሌሆሙይ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዓልተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጽርዓት ፤ ወይጸርሕ ፡ ወትረ ፤ ወይቤ ፡ አበስኩ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ 'ብእሲ ፡ ወገበርኩ ፡እኩየ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወአብ ፡ ር ፡ እስመ፣ አልቦ ፡ ገብር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ስርየት ። ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ዓዓመተ ፡ እስከ ፡ የብሰ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወጸልመ5 ፡ እምጠለ ፡ ሰማይ ፤ ወእምሐሩረ ፡ ፀሓይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ "ጥንተ ፡ ዓዓመት ፡ በውስተ፣ ገዳም ሰምዓ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዘይብሎ ፡ ጽናዕ ፡ ወተኃየል ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ተኀድገ ፡ ለከ ፡ "ኩሉ ፤ ኃጣውኢከ፤ ፡ ወኃጢአተ ፡ ዘቀተልካ ፡ ለብእሲት ፡ ተኃድገ ፡ ለከ" ። አላ ፡
Then he left his sheep to abandonment, took his staff in his hand, and walked while shedding bitter tears until he had arrived at the desert of Scetis (= Scetis). He did not enter to the old monks nor did he tell them what he had done, but he penetrated into the interior of the desert, ten miles from the monks. He had no bread to eat and he did not dwell in a dwelling, but he ate with the wild beasts and drank water with them. He wept night and day without ceasing, cried continually, and said: "I have sinned, for I am a man and I have acted badly; forgive me as you (are) God and benevolent father, for there is no servant without sin nor master without forgiveness." He remained fighting thus (spiritually) for forty years until his body withered and blackened because of the dew of heaven and the heat of the sun. When he entered into the fortieth year (that he was spending) in the desert, he heard a voice that said to him: "Be strong and powerful, for behold, all your sins have been forgiven you and the sin (by) which you killed the woman has been forgiven you; but the murder of the child has not been forgiven you until now." It is in his mercy that God


[p. 488] {#po.45.p488}
ቅትለቱሰ ፡፤ ለሕፃን ፤ ኢተኀድገት ፡ ለከ ፤ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ። ወእግዚአብሔርሰ ፡ በሣ ህሉ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትሀከይ ፡ ወኢይደቅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኃጢአት” ፡ ምዕረ ፡ ወዳግመ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ዘንተ ፡ ወሰከ ፡ ብካዮ ፡ ወተጋድሎቶ ፡'እስከ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ይእተ ፤ ዓመተ" ፣ ለተፍጻሜተ ፡ ጓዓመት” ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ይመዓልት ፡ ባሕቲቱ ።
said this to him so that he would not become idle and would not fall into sin a second time. When (Apollon) heard this, he redoubled in tears and (spiritual) combat until he had passed this year which completed the forty years to within three days only.

Behold, an angel of God appeared to a hermit who had been dwelling for seventy years in this desert, far from him (= Apollon) in the desert by twelve miles - he had never seen a man. The angel of God said to him: "Go, come out from your place and you will meet an old man. When he confesses to you everything he has done, console him and say to him: Behold, all your sins are forgiven you, and the murder of the child is also forgiven you." The old man rose, met with him (= Apollon), and they greeted each other. (Apollon) was weeping without ceasing, the old man said to him: "Tell me what you have done and why you came here." (Apollon) told him everything that had been with him and how he had split the belly of the woman. The old man said to him: "Do not be afraid, for all your sins have been forgiven you and the (other) sin also - the murder of the woman and the child - has been forgiven you." The hermit said to him (also): "From where do you receive the holy Mysteries?" The shepherd said to him: "My holy father, as true as God is living, behold, I have dwelt in this desert for forty years (and) I have not seen another man than you." The hermit said to him: "My dear father, rejoice, for, tomorrow, an angel of God will come to you and will give you communion in the morning." When it was the time of the morning, on Saturday, there exhaled the perfume of a very sweet balm such as had never been exhaled. Abba Apollon said to the hermit: "My father, my soul wants to depart (from my body) for fear." When he had said this, he saw an angel of God standing before him, in the likeness of a monk; his face (was) very luminous, and he himself was singing, saying: "I was afflicted and God saved me; return, my soul, to your rest, for God is your help, for he has saved my soul from death, my eyes from tears, and my feet from stumbling; may I please God in the land of the living." When Abba Apollon the shepherd heard this, he fell to the ground and was, from fear, like a corpse. The angel stretched out his hand and raised him; (Apollon) saw the angel in the likeness of a man, his heart was strengthened, he received the holy Mysteries from the hand of the angel with great honor, in fear and trembling, and the angel of God ascended to heaven.
ብእሲ፤፤ ፣ ወጸንዓ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወተመጠወ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት፤ ፡ እምእዴሁ ፡ ለመልአክ፤ ፡ በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ በፍርሀት ፡ ወበረዓድ፤፤፤ ፡ ወዐርገ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወሶበ፤። ፡ ተመጠወ ፡ ኖላዊ፤ቶ ፡ ሦርባነ፤፥ ፡ 'እምእዴሁ ፡ ለመልአክ፤ ፡ ኮነ፣
When the shepherd had received communion from the hand of the angel, his body
