ሥጋሁ ፡ ፀዓዳ ፡ ከመ ፡ በረድ ፡ ወነበሩ፤፤ ፡ ይእተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ፤ ፡ ወይሴብሕዎ፤፤ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እስከ ፡ ምሴት ። ወኖላዊሰ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ፤፣፣ ኢያጽርዓ ፡ እምጸሎት ፡ በከመ፤ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አበስኩ ፡ 'እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ሰብእ፤፤ ፡ ወገብር፤ ፡ እኩይ፤ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወአብ፤ ፣ ር ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ገብር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኃጢአት! ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ስርየት፤ ። ወነበሩ ፣ በዕለተ!" ፡ እሑድ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡እስከ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ጹነወ! ጹና ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ዘይፈደፍድ ፡፤ እምቀዳሚ ። ወይቤሎ!፤ ፡ 'ገዳማዊ ፡ 'ለአባ ፣ አብሎ፤፤" 'አኦእቱየ ፡ ነዓ፤፤ ፡ ተሐፀብ ፡ በንስቲት ፡ ማይ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዛቲ!" ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ዘተወከፍከ፤፤ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጸማ ፡ በእንቲአሃ፤ ፡ ተፈሣሕ፤ ፡ ኦእየ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ፤ ፡ ታዓርፍ ፡ እምይ እዜ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ መጠዎሙ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ለክልኤሆሙ ፡ ምሥጢረ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ፤" ፡ ኖላዊ ፡ ወዖርዎ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ 'ወሰረሩ ፡ ባቲ ፡ እስከ፤ ፡ አርያም ። ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ገዳማዊ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወይቤ ፡-ምንተ፡ ዘእገብር ፡ አነ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ወኢይክል ፡ ዕፁር ፡ ሥጋከ፤ ፡ ወአልብየ ፡ በዘ ፡ እከሪ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ክልኤቱ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ፤ ፡ ወአመሩ ፤ በአርእስቲሆሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አረጋዊ፤፤ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዘይቤልዎ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ዘንገብር ። ወአመረ ፡ ሎሙ፤። ፡ ወከረዩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መቃብረ ፡ ወዖርዎ ፡
became white as snow, and they (= Apollon and the hermit) remained that day rejoicing and glorifying God until evening. Abba Apollon the shepherd did not cease to pray as before, saying: "I have sinned, for I am a man and a bad servant, forgive me as you (are) God and benevolent father, for there is no servant without sin nor master without forgiveness." (Apollon and the hermit) remained on Sunday glorifying God until the middle of the night; at that moment, there exhaled a sweet odor that surpassed the previous one. The hermit said to Abba Apollon: "My brother, come, wash yourself with a little water, for it (is) the hour for which you have accepted all (your) torments; rejoice, my brother, for you, you are going to rest from now on." Then an angel of God administered the holy Mystery to them both and, immediately, Abba Apollon the shepherd died. The angels carried his soul and flew away with it into the heights. The hermit wept over him and said: "What shall I do? I am (an) old man and I cannot carry your body, and I have nothing with which to dig (a grave)." Immediately, two lions arrived, prostrated themselves before the body of the holy Abba Apollon, and made signs with their heads toward the old man as if they were saying to him: "What must we do?" He indicated (it) to them and they dug a grave for him (= the body); they carried him there and buried him there as (if they were) men, the hermit blessed them, and they went away in peace.


[p. 492] {#po.45.p492}
ወቀበርዎ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወባረኮሙ ፡፣ ገዳማዊ፤ ፡ ወሐሩ ፡ በሰላም ። ወሐዘነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ገዳማዊ፤ ፡ ወይቤ፤ ቦኑ ፡ እረክብ ፡ ዘይቀብር ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ ወመጽአ፤ ፣ ቃል፤ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ አነ ፡ እቀብር ፡ ሥጋከ ፡ ወኢተረፈከ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይመዓልት ፤ ወታዐርፍ ። ወናሁ ፡ እፌኑ ፡ ለከ ፡ ይዕደወ ፡ ንግሮሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አብሎ፤ ፡ ኖላዊ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንግሩ ፣ እሙንቱሂ ፡ ለካልኣን ፡ ከመ ፡ እለ ፡ ወድቁ ፡ በኃጢአት ፣፤ ኢይምትሩ!፤ ፡ ተስፋሆሙ ፡ እምሕረተ ፡፤ እግዚአብሔር ። ወበሳኒታሁ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ፻ዕደው ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አብሎ፤?!፣ ኖላዊ ፡ ወአንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወጸሐፉ ፡ ገድሎ ። ወነበሩ ፡ በኀቤሁ፤"" ፣ እስከ ፡ ሣል ስት ፡፤ ፅለት ፤ ወእምዝ ፡፣ ጸለየ ፤ ገዳማዊ፤ ፣ ወመጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ እልክቱ ፡ ፪አናብስት ፡ ወከረዩ ፡ 'ሎቱ ፡ ኅበ፤ ፡ መቃ ብረ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ፤ ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፡ 'ከመ ፡ ሰብእ፤ ፡ ወሐሩ! ። ወተለውዎሙ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡፻ ፅደው ፡ እስከ ፡ አብጽሕዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ በገዳመ፤ ፣ አስቄጥስ ፡ወነገርዎሙ ፡'ለቅዱሳን ፡ መነኮሳት ፤ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ዘኮነ ፡ ወአንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወጸሐፉ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አብሎ፤‹' ፡ ኖላዊ፤፤ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ያነብብዎ ፡ 'ለለዕለተ!"፡ ፡
The hermit was afflicted and said: "Will I find someone who will bury my body!" A voice arrived and said to him: "I, I will bury your body; you have only three days left and you will die. Behold, I am going to send you three men; tell them all the (spiritual) combat of Abba Apollon the shepherd so that they too (may) tell it to others so that those who will have fallen into sin do not lose their hope in the mercy of God." The next day, three men came to him; he told them the (spiritual) combat of Abba Apollon the shepherd, they were very astonished, and wrote his (spiritual) combat. They remained with him (= the hermit) until the third day, then the hermit prayed and committed his soul into the hand of God. The two lions arrived, dug (a grave) for him near the tomb of Abba Apollon, buried him as (if they were) men, and went away. The three men followed them until they had arrived at the monastery of Abba Macarius (= Macarius) in the desert of Scetis (= Scetis). They told the holy monks everything that had happened; (the latter) were very astonished, wrote the (spiritual) combat of Abba Apollon the shepherd, and one began to read it


[p. 494] {#po.45.p494}
እሑድ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ለየካቲት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዕረፍቶሙ ፡ ለበንዋ ፡ ወቡላ ፤ ወአሞኒ ፡ ወእስያ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ለአባ ፡ እብሎ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ፤"" ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለሚናስ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ርቱዓ ፣ ሃይማኖት ። በረከቱ ፡ ወዓስበ ፣ ረድኤቱ ፡ ወግፍዑ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጸሓፊሁ ፤ ቴዎድ ሮስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
every Sunday. His death took place on the fifth of Yakkatit. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. [On this day also there is the death of Banwa, Bula (= Paul), Amoni (= Amoni), and Esya, the father and mother of Abba Eblo. May their blessing be with their dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.] [On this day also there is the death of Minas, the king of Ethiopia of the right faith. May his blessing and the price of his help and his violence be with his scribe Tewodros (= Theodore) for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR 6 YAKKATIT
አመ ፡ ፄሄለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ዐርገ! ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ" ፡ አቡሊዳስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡'መምህረ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ዓለም ፡ እምባሕር፤ ።ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ ማእምረ ፡ በትውልዱ ። ወኅረዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ ሮሜ ፣ እምድኅረ ፡፣ አብ ፡ አውኪያኖስ ፣ በቀዳሚ ፣ ዓመት ፡ ዘተሠይመ ፡ አብ ፡ ከላዲያኑ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ወትረ! ፣ ለሕዝቡ ፡ ወየዐቅቦሙ፤!፤ ፣፡ እምግዕዞሙ፤ ፡ለአረማዊያን ፡ ወያጸንዖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ክርስቶስ ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣፤ ቀላውድዮስ!፤፤ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ አኀዞ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አቡሊዳስ፤ ፡ ወቀሠፎ ፡ ቅስፈተ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ እብነ ፡ ክቡደ፤፤ ፡ ወወገሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕረ" ፡ ኢርትራ፤ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ለየካቲት ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በሳ ኒታሁ፤ ፡ አመ ፡ ሄለየካቲት ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተረክበ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ! እንዘ ፡ ይትዋነይ፤ 'ዲበ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ገጸ፤፤ ፡ ማይ ፡ 'ወእብን ፡ እሱር። ውስተ ፡ እገሪሁ፤ ። ወነሥኦ ፡ ፳እምነ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወአንበሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወገነዞ ፡ "በአልባሳት ፤ ክቡራት ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዜና ፤ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወኩሉ፣ ደወላ ፡ ወኀሠዎቻ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውዕዮ ፡ በእሳት ። ወኃብኦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day came up from the sea the body of the holy Hippolytus (= Hippolytus), patriarch of the city of Rome (= Rome), the doctor of all the ends of the earth. This saint was an honored man, accomplished (and) learned in his generation. God chose him so that he would be patriarch of the city of Rome after the father Anicetus (= Anicetus?), the year when the father Celadion (= Celadion) was named (patriarch) of the city of Alexandria. This father taught his people continually, protected them from the opinions of the pagans, and fortified them in the faith of our Lord Christ. His fame was known to the impious emperor Claudius (= Claudius); (the latter had) the saint Hippolytus seized and made him undergo a great scourging. When he was tired of torturing him, he (had) a heavy stone tied to his feet and (had) him thrown into the Red Sea on the fifth of Yakkatit. When it was the next day, the sixth of Yakkatit - as today -, the body of the saint was found dancing (?) on the surface of the water, and the stone (was) tied to his feet. A believer took him, deposited him in his house, and buried him in precious garments. This news spread in the city of Rome and all its territory. The emperor (had) him (= the body) sought to burn him in the fire, but the man at whose house he was hid him and did not show him.
"ዘኮነ ፤ በኅኀቤሁ፤ ፡ ወኢከሠቶ" ። ወደረሰ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ድርሳናተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡'ዘእግዚእነ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡
This saint composed many homilies, some on the right faith in our Lord Christ and on the incarnation of the Son of God in the nature


[p. 496] {#po.45.p496}
በጠባይዓተ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወቦ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ትምህርት ፡ ወተግሣጽ ፡ በእንተ" ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ሥምረቱ ፤ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወሠርዐ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ቋወቋ0በእንተ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያንቦ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ይትረከቡ ፡ 'በውስተ ፤ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ዚአነ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡፤ ኩሎሙ ፡፣ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ 'ዘኩሎሙ ፡፣ አሕዛበ ፡ ክርስቲያን፤ ።
human, some (are) a teaching and an exhortation to do the will of God. He established thirty-eight rules concerning the law of the Church, and they are found in our church (and) in all the churches of all Christian peoples. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፤ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ፥ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡'አባ ፡ ቂር፥ ፡ ወዮሐንስ ፡ "ወሾጾደናግል ፤ ወእሞሙ፤ ። ወአስማቲሆን ፡ ቲዮዳራቶ ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሃ ፡ ሀብተ ፡፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወቲዮፊና፥ ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሃ ፣ ሃይማኖተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወቲዮድክ ስያ ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሃ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአትናስያ ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሃ ፡ ሕያዊት ፡ እንተ ፡ ኢትመውት ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ 'አባ ፡ ቂር፥፤ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መነኮሰ ፡ ጽሙደ ፡ መስተጋድለ ፡ እምንእሱ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ሐራ ፡ እምሰራዊተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኮኑ። ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ኃደሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተአመኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ 'ወእሙንቱ ፡ ወእሞሙ ፡ ወደ ናግል ፣ እለ ፣ ምስሌሆሙቶ፥0፤ ፣ በቅድመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ወተስእለ+፥ ፡ እምአይቴ ፤ እሙንቱ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፤ ወአዘዘ" ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ኀቤሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወቆሙ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወተአምኑ ፤ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወኩነኖሙ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ እምኩነኔሆሙ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ። "ወኮነት ፡ ቅድ ስት ፤ አትናስያ ፣ ታጸንዖን ፣ ለደናግል ፤ አዋልዲሃ ፤ ወታስተዔግሦን፤ ፣ ወታጠይ ቆን ፡ ከመ ፤ እማንቱ። ፣ ይከውና ፤ መርዓታቲሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘበአማን ፣ ለእመ ፡ ኮንክን ፡ ሰማዕታተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ። "ወከማሁ ፡ ቅዱስ፡'አባ፣ ቂር ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይከሥት ፡ ሎን ፡ ወይዘክር ፡ ሎን ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ትክላ ፡ ለሐዋርያዊት ፡ እምንዳቤ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ያቀርብዎን ፡ ለደናግል ፡ ኀበ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ሰይፍ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮና ፡ ሰማዕተ ። ወበድኅሬሆን ፡ 'ኮነት ፡ እሞን ፡ ሰማዕተ ። "ወእምድኅረ ፤ ዝንቱ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ 'አባ ፡ ቂር" ፡ ወአባ ። ዮሐንስ ፡ ሰማዕታት ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግድፉ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፥ ለአራዊት ፤ ወለአፅዋፈ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወሀለዉ ፡ በህየ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ መሃይምናን ፣ ወነሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ወገነዝዎሙ ፡ ወአንበርዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሣጹን ፡ እስከ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ ስደት ፡ ወተሐንጸ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአንበሩ ፤ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ተአምራት ፤ ወመንክራት ፡ ዐበይት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡፣
On this day also there are the holy martyrs Abba Qir (= Abougir), Yohannes (= John), three virgins, and their mother. Their names (are) Tiyodara (= Theodora), which, translated, (means) "gift of God", Tiyofina (= Theophany), which, translated, (means) "faith of God", Tiyodeksya (= Theodoxa), which, translated, (means) "glory of God", and Atnasya (= Athanasia), which, translated, (means) "living one who does not die." The holy Abba Qir was a devout monk (and) ascetic since his childhood. The saint Yohannes was a soldier of the troops of the emperor. They were people of the city of Alexandria, then they dwelt in the city of Antioch (= Antioch). Then they confessed our Lord Christ, they, their mother, and the virgins who (were) with them, before the emperor Diocletian (= Diocletian). (The latter) asked where they (were) from; they told him (that they were) from the city of Alexandria; he gave an order and they were led there. When they arrived at the city of Alexandria and stood before the governor, they confessed our Lord Christ; (the governor) made them undergo great torture and, when he was tired of torturing them, he ordered them to be beheaded with the sword. The saint Athanasia fortified the virgins her daughters, exhorted them, and taught them that they would become the brides of Christ, the true king, "if (she said) you become martyrs for his holy name." Likewise, the holy Abba Qir revealed to them and reported to them what had happened in the way of torments to the saint Thecla (= Thecla) the apostolic. The soldiers presented the virgins to the executioner one after the other until they had become martyrs. After them, their mother became a martyr and, after that, the saints Abba Qir and Abba Yohannes became martyrs. Then the governor ordered their bodies to be thrown to the wild beasts and the birds of the sky. There were believers there, they took their bodies in secret, buried them, and placed them in a coffin until the days of the end of the persecution. A church was (then) built for them and their bodies were placed there. Great miracles and wonders occurred from them. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፤ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ አዕረፈት ፡ ኃጥእት ፡ ማርያም ፡ እንተ ፡ ቀብዓቶ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ዕፍረተ ። ወዛቲ ፡ ብፅዕት ፡ ኮነት ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ኃጥእተ ፡ ወፈጸመት፤ ፡
[On this day also died the sinner Mary (= Mary) who anointed our Lord with perfume. This blessed woman was first a sinner and
ኩሎ ፡ ንብረታ ፡ በዝሙት ። ወኮነት ፡ ትሠረገው ፡ በዘዘዚአሁ ፡ አልባስ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ታስሕቶሙ ፡ ለወራዙት ። ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ተሠርገወት ፡ አልባሲሃ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዳ ፡ ወተቀብዓት ፡ ዕፍረተ ፡ አፈው ፡ ወርእየት ፡ ገጻ ፡ በመጽሔት ፡ ወአደማ ፡ ቂሐተ ፡ መላትሒሃ ፡ ወላህየ ፡ አፅይንቲሃ ፡ ወጐንደየት ፡ መጠነ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ታነ ክር ። ወእምዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ሠናይ ፡ 'ወተዘከረት ፡ ሞተ“ ፡ ወኅልፈተ ፡ ዓለም ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓት ፡ 'በእንተ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትወ ከፍ ፡ ኃጥኣነ ፡ ወይሰሪ ፡ አበሳ ፡ ነሥአት ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋያ ፡ ወተሣየጠት ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ፅፍረተ፣ ዘአልባስጥርስ ፤ ወሖረት ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ይረፍቅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ሰገደት ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወቀብዓቶ ፡ ዕፍረተ ፡ ወሐፀበቶ ፡ በአንብዓ ፤ ወመዝመዘቶ ፤ በሥዕርተ ፡ ርእሳ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ብዝኃ ፤ አፍቅሮታ ፡ ኃደገ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ኃጢ አታ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ይዝክርዋ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰበኩ ፡ ወንጌሎ ። ወእምአሜሃ ፡ ነበረት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትለአኮ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ አንስት ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።]”
spent all her life in fornication. She adorned herself with various garments to seduce young men. One day, she adorned herself with her garments according to her habit, anointed herself with fragrant perfume, looked at her face in a mirror - the redness of her cheeks and the beauty of her eyes pleased her - and lingered an hour marveling. Then a beautiful thought came to her and she remembered death and the transitory character of the world. When she heard about our Lord Jesus Christ that he welcomed sinners and forgave faults, she took all her money, bought nard perfume with it, and went to him (= Christ) while he was at table in the house of Simon (= Simon). She prostrated herself at his feet, anointed him with perfume, washed him with her tears, and wiped him with her hair. When our Lord saw the greatness of her love, he forgave her sin and ordered (the apostles) to make mention of her where they would preach his Gospel. From then on she remained to serve him with the holy women. May her prayer and her blessing be with her dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR 7 YAKKATIT
አመ ፡ ለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፤ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡እልስክንድሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጸጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ጓወ፻ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መነኮሰ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ባታሮን፤ ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሁ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አበው ። ወበፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወኮነ ፡ ጸድቀ ፡ ወንጹሐ ፡ ወማእምረ ፡ ወበጽሖ"፤ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፣ ዓቢይ ፣ በመዋዕለ ፤ ሚመቱ ። እስመ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ዘኮነ ፤ በውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ጫሞ ፡ ለወልዱ ፡ ሀየንቴሁ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መንግሥት ። ወአመዝበሮሙ ፡ ለአድባረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ዘገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፤ "ወእምብገኃ ፡ ክህደቱ ፡ ወእከዩ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዘመንገለ ፡ ሰሜነ ፡ ምስር" ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ሥዕለ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ስርጉት ፡ 'ወዲ ቤሃ ፡ አልባስ ሐሪር ፡ ሠናይ ። ወይቤ ፡ ምንት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ "ወይቤልዎ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሥዕለ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እሙ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወፀረፈ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ወተፍአ ፡ ምራቀ ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ ገጻ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮንኩ ፡ ሕያወ ፡ አንሰ!" ፡ እደመስሶሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፤ ወኣጠፍኦሙ ። ወእምዝ፤፤ ፡ ጸረፈ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡በይእቲ፣ ሌሊት" ፡ ርእየ ፡ በንዋሙ ፡ ድንጋፄ፤፤ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወበጊዜ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ ነገሮ! ፡ ለአቡሁ፣ ወይቤሎ፡፣እስመ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ በጽሐኒ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ወኩነኔ፤ ፡'ብዙኅ፤፥ እምሰይጣናት፤ ። ወርኢኩ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ዓቢየ" ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበር ፡ ዓቢይ!፣
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father Abba Alexander (= Alexander), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarchs, he is the forty-third. This saint was a monk at the monastery of Babaron (= Babaron) which, translated, (means) "monastery of the fathers". By the will of God, he was named patriarch of the city of Alexandria. He was just, pure, and learned and great misfortunes happened to him in the time of his charge, for the king of Egypt who lived in that time named his son in his place at the head of the kingdom. (The latter) pillaged the monasteries of the monks of the desert of Scetis (= Scetis). Because of the greatness of his impiety and his wickedness, he entered a monastery that (was) in the south of Egypt (= Egypt); he saw there an image of our Lady Mary, it was adorned and (there was) on it a beautiful silk garment. He said: "What is this?" They said to him: "It (is) the image of our Lady Mary, the mother of Christ." He insulted her, spat in her face, and said: "If I live, I will make the Christians disappear and will annihilate them." He then insulted our Lord Christ. That night he saw in his sleep a great object of terror. In the morning, he spoke to his father and said to him: "This night, a great misfortune and a strong torment happened to me from the demons: I saw a man of great height sitting on a great throne; he was very


[p. 498] {#po.45.p498}
ወውእቱ ፡ መፍርህ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወግሩም ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወገጹ ፡ ብሩህ ፡ 'ዘየዓቢ ፡ እምብር ሃነ ፤ ፀሐይ"፤ ። ወየአውድዎ ፡ አእላፋት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸውሩ ፡ 'ንዋየ ፡ ሐቅል፡ ፡ ወኮንኩ ፡'አነ ፡ ወአንተ ፡ እሱራን ፡ ድኅሬሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተስእልኩ?። ፡ አነ ፡ መኑ ፤ ውእቱ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ወይቤሉኒ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ክርስቲ ያን ፡ ዘአንተ ፡ ተሣለቀ፤ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ትማልም ። ወእምዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ አሐዱ፣ "እምእለ ፤ ይጸውሩ ፡ ንዋየ ፣፤ ሐቅል" ፡ ወረገዘኒ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ገቦየ ፡ በኩናት" ፡ ወኢያውፅኣ ፡ እምብኅቦየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሞትኩ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሐዘነ ፈድፋደ፤፤ ፡ ወሐመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሬዛ" ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወብሀመ፥ ልሳኑ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ በይእቲ ፡
frightening and very formidable, and his face shone (with a light) greater than the light of the sun. Myriads carrying weapons surrounded him; you and I were chained behind him. When I asked who this (man) was, it was answered to me: "This is Jesus Christ, the king of the Christians, whom you mocked yesterday." Then one of those who carried weapons came to me, pierced my side with a spear, and did not withdraw it from my side until I was dead." When his (= the prince's) father heard this, he was very afflicted. The young man fell ill then, his tongue became mute, and he died that night. Forty days later, his father died also.
ሌሊት ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፤ ጓመዓልት ፡ ሞተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ካዕበ ። ወተሠይመ" ፡ ካልእ ፤ ንጉሥ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ወአመንደቦሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ካዕበ" ። ወአኃዞ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰአለ ፡ እምሕዝቡ ፡ ቋ፻ዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ኪያሃ ። ወአጥፍኦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ "ለውእቱ ፤ ንጉሥቶ፥ ፍጡነ ።
Another king was named after him, he also tormented the people of the Christians. He seized this father Abba Alexander and (had) him tortured until he asked his people for three thousand pieces of gold, and (the patriarch) gave them to him. God made this king perish quickly.
ወእምድኅሬሁ ፤ ተሠይመ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘየአኪ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወአኀዞ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፤ ወኅሠሠቶ፥‹ ፡ እምኔሁ፥፤ ፡ ወ፻ዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ካልእተ፡ ። ወይቤሎ ፡'ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡እሙንቱ ፡ ሰአልኩ ፡እመሃይምናን ፡ወቦ ፡ዘተለቃሕኩ፣ እምካልኣንቶ፥፤ ፡ ወኢተወክፈ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወይቤ ፡ 'ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት" ፡ ተዓ ገሥ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ እስከ ፡ እስእል ፡ እምነ ፡ ካልኣን ፡ ወተዓገሠ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወዓርገ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስእልፅ ፡ ወረከበ፥" ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ገዳማዊ ። መልዕልተ ፡ ደብር ፤ ወምስሌሁ ፡፤ ፪አርድእት ፤ ወአዘዞሙ ፡፣ ለአርድእቱ ፡ ገዳማዊ ፤ ከመ ፤ ይኩስትሩ፤፤ ፤ መካነ ፡ ውስተ" ፡ ደብር ፡ ወረከቡ ፣ ውስቴቱ" ፣ ፳ማኅየ ባተ ፡ ምሉኣነ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወሰረቁ ፡ አርድእቱ ፡ ማኅየበ ፡ ወኃባእዎ ፡ ወአምጽኡቶ፤ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳማዊ ፡ 'መምህሮሙ ፡ 8፪ማኅየባተ ፤ ወርቅ። ወፈነዎሙ፤" ፡ ገዳማዊ ፡፡ኅበ፣ ረድኡጾ ፡ 'ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትራዳእ ፡ ቦሙ፥ ። ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ፪አርድእቱ ፡ ለገዳማዊ ፡ ነሥኡ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ማኅየበ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወገደፉ ፣ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወአውሰቡ፤ ፡ ወተሣየጡ ፡ አግብርተ ፡ ወአእማተ ፡ ወእንስሳ ። ወአኀዞሙ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወቀሠፎሙ ፡ ወተአመኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ ፳ማኅየባተ ፡ ምሉኣነ ፡ ወርቅ ። ወፈነወ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ኅበ ፣፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራሁ ፥፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወፈነወ" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ወማህረከ ፡ ቤቶ፣ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ መነሥአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘረከበ ፡ እምንዋየ" ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ዘአብያተ ፤ ክርስቲያን ። ወእምዝ ፡ አብጽሖ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወአሕሰሮ ፡ ወአሰሮ?፤! ፡ ወሞቅሖ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አምጽእ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ፪ማኅየባተ”" ፡ ዘምሉእ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ጠ፻ዲናረ ። ወይቤሎ ፤ ሊቀጳጳሳት ፡ አልብየ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምንዋየ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኃላፊ ። ወኢሰደዶ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ እስከ ፡ 'ፈነወ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት” ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፤ ወአም ጽአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቋ፻ዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወወሀቦሙ” ፡ ሎቱ” ።
After him was named another king who was worse than the previous one. His general seized this father and demanded from him three thousand other pieces of gold. This father said to him: "Those (= the first three thousand pieces), I asked (them) from the believers, and there are (some) that I borrowed from other (people)." But (the general) did not accept this (explanation) from him. The patriarch said to him: "Give me a delay until I ask (for this money) from other (people)." (The general) gave him a delay. This father went up to Upper Egypt to ask for (the money); it happened (that there was) a hermit on the mountain and, with him, two disciples. The hermit ordered the disciples to sweep a place in the mountain; they found there five jars filled with gold. The disciples stole one jar, hid it, and brought to the hermit their master the four (other) jars of gold. The hermit sent these to the disciple of the patriarch so that (the latter) might help himself with them. As for the two disciples of the hermit, they took the (fifth) jar of gold, went into the world, abandoned the monastic habit, married, and bought servants, maidservants, and beasts of burden. The governor of the city arrested them and (had) them scourged; they confessed that they had found five jars filled with gold. The governor dispatched to the general of the king and informed him of this. The general sent (someone who) pillaged the house of the patriarch and took everything he found in the way of sacred vessels of the Christian churches. Then (the general) had this father brought, treated him with contempt, (had) him chained and imprisoned, and said to him: "Bring me the four jars that (are) filled with gold, of the three thousand pieces." The patriarch said to him: "I possess none of the goods of this transitory world." (The general) did not let the patriarch out of prison until he had dispatched to all the people, had brought him the three thousand pieces of gold, and had given them to him.


[p. 500] {#po.45.p500}
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አጥፍኦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ "ንጉሥ ፡ እኩይ?" ። ወተሠ ይመ” ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ካልእ ፤ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘየአኪ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወአገበሮሙ” ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ 'ከመ ፡ ይሕትሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ህየንተ ፣ መስቀል ፡ ክቡር ፤ ስመ፤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ሐሳዊ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ምድር ፡ ዘተነበየ ፡ በእንቲአሃ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ "ወንጌላዊ ፡ ነባቤ ፣ መለኮት፤ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እኩይ ፡፤ ኅበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወአዘዞ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ ስእለተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይም ሀሮ ፣ እምዝንቱ ፤ ግብር ፡ ወአበዮ" ። ወሰአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትዓገሥ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ይመዓልተ ። ወሰአለ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡'ብዙኃ ፡ ስእለተጾ ፡ ከመ፣ "ኢይግድፎ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወኢያውድቆ ፡ ውስተ ፡ 'ዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ ርኩስ፤ ። ወሰ ምዓ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ስእለቶ ፡ ወሐወጾ ፡ በደዌ ፡ ንስቲት ። ወሰአሎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ጳጳሳት ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ የአዝዝ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወአበዮ ፤ ወመሰሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ ይትሜነይ" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሕትመተ” ፡ እድ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፪8መዓልት ፡ አስተርአዮ ፣፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ በሳኒታ ፤ ታዓርፍ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፡ አስተዳሉ ፡ ለነ” ፡ ሐመረ ፡ እስመ ፡ በሳኒታሁ ፡ የሐውጸኒ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ።
After that, God made this bad king perish. In his place was named another king who was worse than him. He forced the people of the Christians to mark on their hands, in place of the glorious Cross, the name of the impure false prophet - it (is) the sign of the Beast of the earth about which John (= John) the Evangelist, the Theologian, has prophesied. This bad king dispatched to all the cities and ordered them (= the inhabitants) to do this; he also ordered the patriarch to do this. (The latter) addressed many prayers to him so that he might spare him this action, and (the king) refused him. (The patriarch) addressed many prayers to God so that he would not reject him far from him and would not make him fall into this impure action. God heard his prayer and brought upon him a small illness. The patriarch asked the king to permit him to go to his house in the city of Alexandria. (The king) refused him and believed that he desired (this) because of the mark on the hand. Four days later, an angel of God appeared to the patriarch and said to him: "Tomorrow you will enter into rest." The patriarch said to his disciples: "Prepare a ship for us, for, tomorrow, our Lord Christ will visit me." He died, his body was carried in the ship, and he was buried with the bodies of the patriarch fathers.

In the time of this father, the Melkites who (were) in the land of Egypt had a holy patriarch whose name (was) Anastasius (= Anastasius). His people rose against him and hated him because of his love for the Jacobite believers. He thought of saving his soul and not associating himself with his people in their hatred for the Jacobite believers. He abandoned his people, came to this father Abba Alexander, and put himself under his authority. This father rendered him great honors, asked him to sit on the patriarchal seat, to govern the people of the believers, and to let him dwell in a monastery like a monk among (others). Abba Anastasius did not consent to this thing and said: "If I wanted the patriarchate, behold, I have been patriarch, but I want to be your disciple." After much insistence and dispute that there was between them, Anastasius consented to be a bishop. This father (= Alexander) handed over to him a province among the episcopal provinces. He guarded with a beautiful guard the flock at the head of which he had been named. As for the saint Abba Alexander, he remained on the seat of Mark (= Mark) the Evangelist twenty-four years and a half, pleased God, and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ጓወ፳ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ኮነ ፡ መነኮሰ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ 'ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ፤!የ ፡ መርዩጥ፤፤፤ ፡ ወስሙ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ጠቡራ፤፤። ። ወኮነ ፤ ረድአ ፡ ለ፳ብእሲ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቅዱስ፤፤ ፡ ወፍጹም!፤ ። ወርእየ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ይሠየም ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መስተጋድለ ፡ 'በአምልኮቱ፤!፤፤ ፣ ወተጋደለ፤፤ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ አርአያሁ ፤ ወይለብስ ፡፣ ሠቀ ፡፣ ጸኾር ፡ 'ዲበ ፤ ሥጋሁ፤" ፡ ወእመልዕልቴሁ ፡ ለብሰ ፡ ሐጺነ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ፍጹመ፤፤!» ፡ በትሕትናሁ ፡ ወበየውሀቱ ። ወበፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ዘኀረዮ ፣ ተሠይመ!" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ። ወዓቀቦሙ ፡ ለመርዔተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ተዓቅቦ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይምህሮሙ ፡ ወያነብብ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ በእለተ ፡ እሑድ፣ወበበዓላት፤፤፤ ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ፣ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ዛህን ፡ ወሰላም ለቤተ"።። ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡ 'ቅድስት ፡፤ ትፈሪ፤፤ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ ዘይትቃረና፤፤፥።። ። ወፈጸመ! ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በሚመቱ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፤
On this day also died the holy father Abba Theodore (= Theodore), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarchs, he is the forty-fifth. This father was a monk in a monastery (situated) next to the city of Mariout (= Mariout); the name of this monastery (was) Tamnourah (= Tamnourah). He was a disciple of a holy and accomplished old man. (The latter) saw by the Holy Spirit that his disciple Theodore would be named patriarch of the city of Alexandria, and he told this to the people. As for this father, he was ardent in his devotion and led a great (spiritual) combat. His appearance was beautiful, he wore a hair shirt on his body and, over it, was clothed in iron: he was accomplished in humility and in gentleness. By the will of God who had chosen him, he was named patriarch of the city of Alexandria. He guarded the flock of Christ with a beautiful guard; he taught it continually and read the Scriptures to it every day, particularly on Sunday and feast days. All the life of this father was calm and peace for the holy Church - it flourished without anyone attacking it. This father spent in his charge on the seat of Mark (= Mark) the Evangelist eleven years and a half and died in peace. May God have mercy on us through his prayer.

[On this day also there is the death of Abrageyan, Abadir, Abaryanos, and Nebeldemas. May their prayer and their blessing be with us for ever and ever.]

[READING] FOR 8 YAKKATIT In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day (took place) the entry of our Lord Jesus Christ into the Temple forty days after his glorious birth. Joseph (= Joseph) the just who was minister of this mystery presented him - as well as Mary his holy mother - to fulfill the law that he (= Christ) - glory to him! - had prescribed to the people of Israel. They offered a sacrifice according to the Law and Simeon (= Simeon) the priest took him in his arms. This Simeon was a just man. When Ptolemy II (= Ptolemy II) who was enthroned in the year 5204 of our father Adam (= Adam) reigned, the people of the Jews were under his authority. By the will of God, (the king) dispatched to the city of Jerusalem and brought learned Jews among the rabbis and the elders and ordered them to translate for him


[p. 504] {#po.45.p504}
ሎቱ፤!፤ ፡ ኩሎሙ! ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ሕግ ፡ እምልሳነ ፡ ዕብራይስጥ ፡ እስከ ፡ ልሳነ ፡ ጽርዕ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ በፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፍልስ ፡ ሕግ" ፡ እምነ ፣ አይሁድ፤ ፡ ወይኩን፤፤ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዓመታት ፡ ብዙኃት ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፍልጥዎሙ ፡ ለለ ፪እምኔሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፳መካን ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኮኑ ፣ ድወ፪ወአንበርዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ወጠ'ወ፪ሐይመታት ። ወረሰየ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ዕደወ ፡ ይዕቀብዎሙ ፡ ከመ፤ ፡ ኢይትጋ ብኡ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወኢይትከሀሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰናአዉ ፡ በዘ ፡ ይጽሕፉ ፡ ወኢይወልጡ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሕግ ። እስመ ፡ ፅውቅ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡
all the books of the Law from the Hebrew language into the Greek language. This took place by the will of God so that the Law would leave the Jews and would be for the people of the Christians who was to appear many years later. Then the king ordered them to be divided two by two in the same place, for they were seventy-two, and they were installed in thirty-six tents. He placed men over them to watch them so that they would not meet and could not agree on what they would write nor change the word of the Law; in effect, it (is) known that the Jews (are) deceivers.
ጐሕላዊያን!፤ ። ወሶበ፤ ፡ ተርጐሙ፤ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ መጸሕፍተ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ትፀንስ ፡ ወትወ ልድ ፤ ወልደ፡፤ ። ፈርሀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይጽሐፍ ፡ እስመ፥ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ትፀንስ ፡ ወይሳለቅ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኢይትወከፍ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ወየሐሊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወለጠ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሕግ ፡ በዘ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወጸሐፈ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ወለት ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ናፈቀ" ፡ በነፍሱ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኢይትከሀል ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትፅንስ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ወትወልድ ። ወእንዘ ፤፡ ይሔሊ” ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ድቃስ ፡ ወኖመ ፡ ወአስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘናፈቀ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ኢትጥዕሞ ፡ ለሞት ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ትሬእዮ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ዘይትወለድ ፡ እምድንግል ፡ ወትፀውሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ እደዊከ ። ወሐይወ ፡ እምድኅረዝ፤፤ ፡ ፻፻ዓመታተ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ ወአብእዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወኮነ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ፅውር ፡፤ አዕይንቲሁ ፡፣ ወሶበ" ፣ ተቀበሎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዲበ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ተከሥተ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ መን ፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘኮንከ ፡ ትፀንሖ ። ወባረኮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እግዚኦቶ፥ ፡ ሠዐሮ ፡ ለገብርከ ፡ በሰላም፥ ፡ እስመ ፡ 'አነ ፡ ነበርኩ ፡ እሱረ፥" ፡ በእንቲአከ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ሕይወተቶ" ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኃላፊ ፡ ወናሁ ፤ መጻእከ ፡ ወርኢኩከ ። ሰአረኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሖር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ትፍሥሕትቶ ፡ ዘለዓ ለም ። እስመ ፡ ርእያ ፡ አዕይንትየ ፡ አድኅኖተከ ፡ ዘአስተዳሎከ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሕዝ ብከ ። 'እስመ ፡ ከሠትከ፥ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ። ወክብረ ፡ ለሕዝብከ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወይቤላ፥፤ ለእሙ። ፡ ማርያም ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልድኪ ፡ንቡር ፡'ለድቀት ፡ ወለተንሥኦተ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ። ዝብሂል ፡፤ ለድቀቶሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ኢየአምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወለተንሥኦቶሙቶ፤ ፡ ለእለ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አጠየቃ ፡ ዘይበውእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ "ልባ ፣ እምሕማም ፡ ወኑፋቄ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ሕማማቲሁ ፡ ይቤ። ፡ የኃልፍ ፡ 'ኑፋቄ ፡ ኩናት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልብኪ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ" ፡ ዘአዘዘ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ሕግ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ።
When they had translated all the books of the Law, this Sem'on the Just arrived at the word of 'Isäyeyäs (= Isaiah) the prophet who says: "Behold, a virgin shall conceive and shall bear a son." He feared to write "a virgin shall conceive," lest the king laugh at him, not accept his word, and think that he had changed the word of the Law in what he had written for him. He wrote "young girl" instead of "virgin." Then he doubted in his soul and said: "This is impossible, that a virgin should conceive and bear a child." While he was thinking about this, a stupor descended upon him and he fell asleep. An angel of God appeared to him and said: "Since you have doubted this, you shall not taste death until you see the Christ who will be born of a virgin and you carry him in your arms." (Sem'on) lived after that three hundred years until our Lord Christ was born and they brought him into the Temple on the same (day) as today. Sem'on had blind eyes. When he received our Lord Christ in his arms, his eyes opened and, immediately, he saw. The Holy Spirit spoke to him and said: "This is he whom you were awaiting." (Sem'on) blessed God and said: "Now, Lord, dismiss your servant in peace, for I have remained chained for you in the life of this transitory world, and behold, you have come and I have seen you. Dismiss me so that I may go into eternal joy, for my eyes have seen your salvation which you have prepared before all your people, for you have revealed a light to the nations and a glory for your people Israel." He said to his (= Jesus') mother Mary: "Your son is here for the fall and for the rising of many of the children of Israel"—which means: for the fall of those who do not believe in him and for the rising of those who believe in him. Then he taught her what would enter her heart in terms of pain and doubt at the moment of his (= Jesus') passion; he said: "The sword of doubt shall pierce your heart." When he had completed what the Law commanded, he died in peace.


[p. 506] {#po.45.p506}
ወሐናሰ ፡ ነቢያዊት ፡ ዘዘከራ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ እስመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ተነበየት ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ትሴብሖ" ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ወትዜንዎሙ ፡ ለርቱዓን5፤ ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፤ ከመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ መድኅን ፡፣ ዘያድኅኖሙ ፡ እምግብርናተ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወእመዋቅሕተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወጸሎታቲሆሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳኒሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
As for Hannä (= Anna) the prophetess whom the Gospel mentioned, she prophesied about him (= Jesus), she glorified God and told the righteous among the children of Israel that he (was) the Savior who would save them from the slavery of Satan and the chains of hell, (the Savior) to whom glory is due for ever and ever. May the prayers of his saints be with us, amen.

[On this day also died Hannä (= Anna) the prophetess, daughter of Fänu'ël (= Phanuel). She (was) of the tribe of 'Asër (= Asher). Her days (of fruitfulness) had passed; she had remained with her husband seven years, and (had lived as) a widow eighty-four years. She did not leave the Temple day or night (and remained there) in fasting and prayer. When our Lord Jesus Christ was brought into the Temple, on the fortieth day after he was born, she stood before him, praised God, and spoke of him (= the child) to all those who were hoping for the redemption of Jerusalem. After that she died in a green old age. May God, through her prayer, have mercy on our king 'Iyäsu for ever and ever.] [On this day also died the blessed lady 'Ammata Krestos and her two servants. This saint was from the people of the city of Questentenyä (= Constantinople). She had a husband (who was one) of the emperor's dignitaries. He died in the prime of his youth and left her a widow; as for her, her days were twelve years. Some time later, a man among the emperor's great ones wanted to take her by force. She used a pretext with him, saying: "I have a grave illness, wait for me until I am healed." Then she distributed her fortune to the poor and the needy, freed her servants, took two of her servants with her, and left by night without anyone knowing. She entered an excavation under a high hill and remained (there) twelve years. Each day, birds brought her various kinds of fruits. Then, in the time of the emperor Quastantinos (= Constantine), a scribe (?) said: "I went toward the east to carry out a matter; I arrived at a monastery, and the abbot and the monks welcomed me; I greeted them and we sat down. There were various trees there full of fruit, and I saw birds taking branches with their fruits, flying away quickly, and not eating (of them). When I saw (this), I was astonished and said to the monks: 'What is this thing?' They said to me: "It has been twelve years that they have been doing this action, but we do not know what becomes of it." I said to them: "It seems to me that these birds are carrying these fruits to monks who (are) in the mountains." While I was saying this, a raven arrived, took a branch with fruits, and flew away. I followed it with the abbot and the monks to know what it was doing. Having arrived at a

ravine, the raven threw the branch and returned. Having arrived in this place, we threw stones and we heard a voice that said: "If you are Christians, do not kill us." We said to them: "Who (are) you?" They said to us: "If you want to see our face, throw us three garments, for we (are) naked." We threw garments to them and descended toward them by a narrow mountain path. When we had arrived, three women received us and bowed before us; we also bowed before them. One of them sat down and the two (others) stood before her. The abbot said to this one: "Where (are) you from, my mother and my lady, and how did you come here?" She told him all her actions from the beginning to the end. The abbot said to her: "Do you want us to bring food from (our) monastery and join you?" She said to him: "My father, order a priest to bring the Eucharist so that we may participate in the holy mysteries of the life-giving Christ: since we left, we have not received communion." The superior ordered a priest to come with the Eucharist, and ('Ammata Krestos) received the holy Mysteries with her servants. The next day, she made a long prayer and committed her soul into the hand of God. Her servants died with her, one following the other. They shrouded them and buried them with psalms and chants." May their intercession be with their dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ዘገዳመ ፡ ሲሐት ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወትሩፈ ፡ ምግባር ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ለአኮ ፡ ቴዎዶስዮስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ሲሐት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይስእሎሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈንዉ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ መነኮሰ ፡ አረጋዌ ፡ ትሩፈ ፡ ምግባር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትናዘዝ ፡ እምቃሉ ፡ ወፈነዉ ፡ ሎቱ ፡፤ ዘንተ” ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ ጽሒፎሙ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ በእደ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ካልእ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ናሁ ፡ ፈነውነ ፡ ለከ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ ዘይሠመይ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ነቢይ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለአረጋዊ ፡ ለአኩ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ መነኮሳት ፣፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ወይሜስሉ ፡ ገድለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ገድለ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ነቢይ ። ወይቤ ፡ በትሕትና ፡ ወበየውሃት ፡ ኦንጉሥ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ይትዓወቅ ፡እምግፅዙ ፡ ወገድሉ ፡ ለኤልያስ ፡ እንበይነ ፡ ጽድቁ ፡ኮነ ፡ ቋዕ ፡ ያመጽእ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሲሳዮ ፡ ወሊተሰ ፡ ብየ ፡ ሲሳይ ፡ ዘይትፈቀድ ፡ ወኣነብር ፡ ኅብስትየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወይመጽእ ፡ ቋዕ ፡ ወይፀውሮ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አንከረ ፡ እምጣዕመ ፡ ቀሉ ፤ ወይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ንግረኒ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንት ፡ ኢወሀበኒ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡፤ ወልደ ። ወይቤ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡፣ መዋዕል ፡፣ ከመ" ፡ ይከውን ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ምድር ፡ ኑፋቄ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ በእንተዝ ፤ ኢወሀበከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወልደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይደመር፤ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኑፉቃን ። ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቦ ፡ ንዋየ ፤፡ ወኢተመጠወ፣
[On this day also died abbä 'Élyäs (= Elijah) of the desert of Sihat (= Scetis), the illustrious old man of virtuous actions. As for this holy old man, when Tëwodosyos (= Theodosius), the righteous emperor, sent to the desert of Sihat asking the monks to send him an old monk of virtuous actions to be consoled by his word, they sent him this saint, writing a letter by the hand of another monk (where) they said: "Behold, we have sent you a holy man whose name is 'Élyäs, (he is) in the likeness of 'Élyäs the prophet." When ('Élyäs) arrived before the emperor, (the latter) said to the old man: "The monks sent me (a letter where) they express themselves and compare your life to the life of 'Élyäs the prophet." ('Élyäs) said with humility and gentleness: "O righteous emperor, forgive me, every man knows himself by his nature and his life; as concerns 'Élyäs, because of his righteousness, a raven brought him his food. As for me, I have the food that is necessary and, (if) I put my bread in the sun, a raven would come and carry it away." When the emperor heard (this), he was astonished at the sweetness of his (= 'Élyäs') word. The emperor said to him: "Tell me, my father, why God did not give me a son." The old man said: "Because days will come such that there will be division of the faith on the earth. Because of that, God did not give you a son so that he would not be mixed with the dividers." (The emperor) wanted to give him money, but he did not accept it and returned to his dwelling. It is said of him that he did not eat

food until he had returned to his cell. || died in peace. May his holy blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu for ever and ever.] [READING] FOR 9 YAKKATIT In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy (and) illustrious father abbä Barsomä the Syrian, father of the monks of the land of Soryä (= Syria). The parents of this saint were people of the city of Semadäyt (= Samosata). A hermit prophesied about him and said to his father before his birth: "There will come forth from you a fruit of life, tasty (and) eminent, and his fame will spread over all the earth," and he told him what would become of him. When he was born, he grew up in the knowledge of God. He fled far from his parents and arrived at the river 'Afrätos (= Euphrates). He dwelt near a righteous man whose name (was) 'Abrehäm (= Abraham). This one took him into the mountain for fear of his parents. The saint abbä Barsomä dwelt in the rocks and there waged a great (spiritual) combat. His fame spread in all the regions and he had many disciples. The water of the place where he dwelt was bitter; he prayed, God changed (it) for him, and it became sweet. God performed great miracles by his hands; among them (is this one): Once, he was far from his cell—he and his disciples—, the moment when the sun sets was near and they were far from their cells; he prayed to our Lord Christ—glory to him!—and (the latter) stopped the sun for him until he had arrived at his cell. Another time, there was a city whose name (was) Ra'äm, its inhabitants were pagans. The rain was withheld by the order of God and did not descend upon them. When they were (greatly) tested, they came to the saint abbä Barsomä. He rebuked them and agreed with them that, if they believed in our Lord Christ, he would make rain fall upon them. They said to him "Yes." He prayed to our Lord Christ for them, (the latter) made an abundant rain fall upon them and they believed in God. Likewise for another city: the inhabitants of this one were pagans, he converted them to the knowledge of God. He overturned many temples of idols.
ካልእተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ ከሐዲያን ፡ ወሜጦሙቶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወአንሐለ ፡ አብያተ ፤ ጣዖታት ፡ ብዙኃተ ። "ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱቶ" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መካነ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ 'ይቀውም ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ነቢር ፤ ፻ወ፪ዓመተ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይደክም ፡ እምትጋህ ፡ ወጸማ ፡ ይደቅስ ፡
This saint had made himself a cell and he stood there without sitting for fifty-four years. When he was tired by the vigil and the macerations,

ዞኮ. 3|ሩ እንዘ ፡ ቅውም ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወታሕተ ፡ እደዊሁ፥" ፡ ናሕስ ፡ ያሰምቅ፤ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይጸውም ፡ ሱባኤ ፡ ሱባኤ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ዘአምድ፥። ፣ ወፈነወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአዮ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ስምዖን፤ ፣ ወተባረኩ፥ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወእምዝ ፣ ተመይጠቶ0" ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብሩ ። ወሰበከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሰማርያ ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ ዲበ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ 'ሶበ ፣ ገብረ ፣ ውስቴታ+ሩ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ።
he would doze standing; (there was) under his hand a low wall, he would lean on it. He fasted one week out of two. This saint lived in the time of Sem'on (= Simeon) the Stylite, (the latter) sent to see him. This saint came to Sem'on, they blessed each other, then (Barsomä) returned to his monastery. He preached in the city of Samäryä (= Samaria) and (its inhabitants) believed through his intermediary when he had performed numerous miracles there.
ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ ቴዎዶስዮስ ፡ ዘይንእስ ፡ ወያጸንዖ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት፤ ። ወወሀቦ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወኢነሥአ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወጸሐፈ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስልጣነ ፡ ላዕለ ፤፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኤሏኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ዘብሔረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡፤ ሕልቀተ ፡ አጻብዒሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡፤ ጉባኤ ፡ ፪፻ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ በሀገረ" ፤ ኤፌሶን ፡ በእንተ ፣ ንስጡር ፣ ከሐዲ ፤ ወአውገዝዎቶ ፡ ወመተርዎ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ። ወጸሐፈ ፡ ሎቱ ፤ ንጉሥ ፡ ክታበ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትኤዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ፣፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ መኳንንት ፤ ወስዩማን ፡ዘብሔረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይጽሕፍ ፡ መልእክታተ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ወያሐትም ፡ በሕልቀቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ፤ ፡ ወይኤዝዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ሠናየ ። ወጸልእዎ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እኩያን ፡ ወአስተዋደይዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ናሁ ፣፤ አባ ፡ በርሶማ፤ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ ወይሰቲ ፡ ወይለብስቶ ፡ ሠናየ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አሐደ ፤ እምአርካኒሁ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይጠይቅ ፡ ዘተብህሀለ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አባ ፡ በርሶማ፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ዓርኩ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኢረከበ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምእኩይ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ተናገሩ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አብጽሖ ፡'ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኢረከቦ ፡ 'ንጉሥ ፤ ተወለጠ ፡ እምግባራቲሁ ፡ መንፈሳዊት ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ያአምር ፡
He went to the emperor Téwodosyos (= Theodosius) the Younger and strengthened him in the right faith. The emperor (wanted) to give him much money, but (the saint) accepted nothing from him. The emperor wrote to him so that he would have authority over all the bishops of the land of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch) and gave him his ring. When the council of the two hundred bishops met in the city of 'Éféson (= Ephesus) regarding Nestur (= Nestorius) the impious, (that its members) excommunicated him and deposed him, this father was with them. The emperor wrote to him with his hand a document so that all the governors and the chiefs of the land of 'Ansokiyä would obey him. (Barsomä) wrote letters to all the cities, sealed (them) with the emperor's ring, and prescribed them to do good deeds. Bad people took a hatred for him, slandered him to the emperor, and said to the latter: "Behold, abbä Barsomä eats, drinks, and dresses (too) well." The emperor sent one of his friends to inquire about what was being said regarding abbä Barsomä. When the friend of the emperor arrived at the saint, he found nothing against him of the evil that had been told about him. || he then brought him to the emperor and the emperor did not find (that) he had changed from his spiritual conduct of which he had knowledge. The emperor honored him and sent him back to his monastery.
እምኔሁ ፡ ወአክበሮ" ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሜጦ።ጾ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብሩ ። ወሶበ ፡ አስተጋብአ ፡ መርቅያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ በኪልቄዶንያ” ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ መማክርቲሁ፣፤ለንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያምጽእ” ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በርሶማሃ?፤ ፡እስመ ፡እሙንቱ፣ ያአምሩ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘላዕሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትዋሥኦሙ ፡ ወይመው ኦሙ ፥ ወያስተኃፍሮሙ'” ። ወሶበ ፡፣ ተፈጸመ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ርኩስ" ፡ ወፈለጥዎ" ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወረሰይዎ ፣ ፪ጠባይዓተ ፤ ወተቃረኖሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ በርሶማ?" ፡ ወሠዓረ ፡ ቃላቲሆሙ ፡ ወአውገዞሙ ፡ ወረገሞሙ ። ወጸሐፉ" ፡ ኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተዋድይዎ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአብጽሖ" ፡ ወኢተክህሎ ፡ ይትቃረን ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘላዕሌሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አባ ፡ በርሶማ፤ ፡ ረገማ ፣ ለንግሥት ፡ ወሖረ" ፡ ወኢነበረት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ወሞተት ፡ በሞት ፡ እኩይ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ዓላዊያን ፡ ይትቃረንዎ ፡ ወይጽሕፉ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትአዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ኢሰምዑ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወኢኃደጉ ፡ ተአዝዞቶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፤ ወኢትምህርቶ ። ወተሰናአዉ ፡ ፳፻ ዓላዊያን ፣ ዕደው ፡ ወምስሌሆሙ ፡
When the emperor Margeyänos (= Marcian) the impious gathered a council in Kilgëdonyä (= Chalcedon), the emperor's advisors asked him not to bring Barsomä to them—indeed, they knew that the grace of the Holy Spirit (was) upon him—so that he would not refute them: he would defeat them and cover them with shame. When the impure council was finished, (that its members) had divided Christ and had attributed to him two natures (= tabäye'), the saint abbä Barsomä fought them, annihilated their words, excommunicated them, and cursed them. They wrote to the emperor denouncing him. The emperor sent and had him brought, but he could not fight the grace of the Holy Spirit that (was) upon him. Then abbä Barsomä cursed the empress and went away; she lasted only a few days and died a bad death. The impious fought him and wrote to all the cities so that they would not obey him, but they did not listen to them and did not abandon the authority of the saint nor his teaching. Two thousand impious men agreed—and


[p. 514] {#po.45.p514}
ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦስ ፣ ዓላዊ ፡ ወፀንሕዎ ፡ 'ውስተ ፣ ፍኖት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈነዉ‹ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ወይሔሑሩ፤! ፡ ኀበ ፡ቤተ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡ ኅቡረ ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ወአንሶሰወ ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ወገርዎ ፡ በአእባን ፡ ወኮኑ ፣ አእባናቲሆሙ ፣ ይትመየጡ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡ ወጐዩ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ እምኀቤሁ" ፡ 'እንዘ ፡ ኅፉራን ፡ እሙንቱ ።
with them an impious bishop—and laid an ambush for him on the road to kill him. Then they sent to him so that he would come to them and they would go to the church together. When he had come and walked with them on the road, they threw stones at him; the stones returned upon their heads, they fled and left him ashamed.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውፅኦ ፡'እመዋቅሕተ ፡ ዝንቱ፣ ዓለም” ፣ ፈነወ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡፣ መልአኮ ፡ ወናዘዞ!0 ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፪መዓልት ፡ትፈልስ ፡'እምዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም!፤ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ በርሶማ!ቦ፡ ፡ ፈነወ ረድኦ ፤ ኅበ ፡ አህጉር፤0የ ፡ 'እለ ፡ የዐውድዎ፤‹የ ፣ ወናዘዞሙ፤ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ። ወእንዘ ፡ የዐውድ!፤" ፡ ረድኡ ፡ በጽሐ፤" ፡ ኀበ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘውስቴቱ ፡፣ ርእሱ፤" ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፣ መጥምቅ ፣፤ ወተአምኃ፤" ፡ 'ለርእሱ ፣፤ ቅድስት፤!" ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መርቅያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ቃል ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ መጥምቅ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኢትፍራህ፤፤፤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሞተ ፡ እስመ ፡ በር ሶማ፤፤። ፣ ሰከዮ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወቅዱስሰ፤! ፡ አባ ፡ በርሶማ፤፤ ፡ ባረከ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ወአስተርአየ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ኅበ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ በዐቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰማይ ፤ ወርእይዎ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ መሃይምናን ፣ እምርሑቅ ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወረከብዎ ፡ በዘአዕረፈ ፡ ወተባረኩ ፡ እምኔሁ፤!፤ ፡ ወበከዩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወሐዘኑ ፤ ፈድፋደ ፡ በእንተ ፤ ፍልጠቶሙ ፤ እምአቡሆሙ ፡፣ መንፈሳዊ ። ወገነዝዎ ፡ በከመ ፡
After that, when God wanted to bring him out of the bonds of this world, he sent him his angel, (the latter) consoled him and said to him: "In four days, you will leave this world." The saint abbä Barsomä sent his disciple to the cities that were around him and he consoled the believers. While his disciple was making the rounds (of the cities), he arrived at the place where (was) the head of Yohannes (= John) the Baptist: he greeted the holy head, wept, and questioned it about Margeyänos the emperor. A voice came from the head of the saint Yohannes the Baptist saying: "Do not be afraid, he is dead, for Barsomä has accused him before Christ." The saint abbä Barsomä blessed his disciple and died in peace. A column of light appeared at the door of his cell—(it went) up to heaven; all the believers saw it from afar, came to the saint, and found him dead; they were blessed by it and wept over him; they were very afflicted to be separated from their spiritual father, shrouded him as is fitting with many canticles and chants, and put him in the tomb. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ይደሉ ፡ በዝማሬ ፡ ወበማኅሌታት ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብር ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ሶርያዊ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ለዝንቱ ፡ቅዱስ፣ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፤ ሶርያዊያን ፣ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ነጋዲያን፤፤ ። ወሐደሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፤ ወወለድዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ኀደሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስሙነይን፤! ። ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀ ፡'ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ፤!፤ጾ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ወኀደጉ ፡ ሎቱ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ከሐዲያን ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይኬንንዎሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን፤፤ ፣ መሃይምናን!" ፡ እለ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቀትልዎሙ፤፡፤ ፡ ወወሀበ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ለነዳያን፤።። ፡ ወለምስኪናን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምርሖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ዘይሠምር ፡ ባቲ ። ወፈነወ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሱርያል ፡ መልአኮ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘይበጽሖ ፡ እምኩነኔ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አዘዘኒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ አሀሉ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ወኣጽንዕከ፤ ፡ ወኢትፍራህ!፤፤ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ሶቤሃ፤ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አደናው፤፤ ፡ ወተአመነ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርቅዎ ፡ እምአልባሲሁ፤፡" ፡ ወይዝብጥዎ፤ ፡ በአስዋጥ ፡፤ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ረሰዩ ፡ ማኅተወ ፡
On this day also the saint Päwlos (= Paul) the Syrian became a martyr. The parents of this saint were Syrians, they (were) merchants. They lived in the city of Alexandria and there begot this saint; then they lived in the city of 'Esmunayn (= Ashmunein). When this saint had grown up, his parents died and left him many goods. When he learned about the impious emperors that they were torturing the holy believers who believed in the name of our Lord Christ and were killing them, he gave all his goods to the poor and the needy. Then he prayed and asked our Lord Christ to guide him in the way that would please him. God sent him Suryäl (= Suriel) his angel and (the latter) explained to him everything that would happen to him in terms of torment for the name of our Lord Christ. He said to him: "Behold, God has ordered me to be with you and to strengthen you, do not be afraid." (Päwlos) rose immediately, went to the city of 'Adanäw (= Antinoë), and confessed our Lord Christ before the governor. (The latter) ordered to
እሳት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገበዋቲሁ፤፤ ፡ ወኢለከፈቶ ፡ እሳት ፡ ግሙራ ። 'ወአምጽእአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሕዶ ፡ ለክርስቶስ፤0 ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ፤፤፤ ፣ ጳውሎስ ፡'ኦአብድ ፡ አበዊየሰ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ወኃደጉ፤። ፡ ሊተ ፡ ፲ወ ፪መክሊተ፤ ፡ ወርቅ፣ መነንክዎሙ፤፤ ፣ ወኢተመይጥኩ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍቅሩ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ እፎ ፡ "እትመየጥ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንዋይከ፤ ። ወአዘዘ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርስኑ ፡ አብትርተ ፡ ሐጺን ፡፤ ውስተ፤፤ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወይደይዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዘኒሁ ፡ ወአፉሁ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዘንተ፤› ። ወፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሱርያልሃ ፡ መልአከ ፡ ወገሰሰ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወአሕየዎ ። 'ወእምዝ ፡ ሰደዱ! ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፤ አክይስተ ፤ ቀታልያነ ፤ ወኢለከፍዎ ። "ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ!ቦ ፡ ልሳኖ ፡ 'ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዘንተ!፤ ። ወአሕየዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ነሥኦ፤። ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀየ፤ ፡ ወአስተርአዮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወናዘዞ ፡ ወአጽንዖ፤ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አርክ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አባ ፡ ኢስ ፤ ወቴክላ ፡ እኅቱ ። ወነገሮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ። ወኮኑ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፤ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፤ ሞቅሕ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፤ ኀቤሆሙ ፣ ቅዱስ ፤ ጳውሎስ ፡ ወተኣምኖሙ ፡ ወተፈሥሐት ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አዴንው ፡ መተረ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሐይቀ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፣፤ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወነሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፤ ሰብእ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወገነዝዎ ፡ በአልባሳት ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ወኀባእዎ ፡ በኀቤ ሆሙ ፡ወኮነ ፡እምኔሁ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ወፈውስ ፡ለዱያን ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡
strip him of his clothes and to whip him with rods, and they did that to him. Then they put torches of fire on his sides, but the fire did not touch him at all. The governor brought him much money so that he would deny Christ. The saint Päwlos said to him: "Fool, my parents died and left me sixteen talents of gold. I disdained them and did not turn toward them for the love of Christ; how would I turn toward your money?" (The governor) ordered to heat iron bars in the fire and to put them in his ears and his mouth, and they did that to him. God sent him the angel Suryäl, (the latter) touched his body and healed him. Then they released murderous snakes against him and they did not touch him. (The governor) ordered to cut out his tongue, they did that to him, and God healed him. When the governor went to the city of Alexandria, he took him with him (to go) there. Our Lord Christ—glory to him!—appeared to him in the boat, consoled him, and comforted him. The saint had a friend whose name (was) abbä ‘Is (= Abaisi); Teklä (= Thecla) (was the name of) his sister. Our Lord said to him (= to Päwlos) that his body would be with their bodies and (that), likewise, his soul would be with their souls. These saints were in the prison of the city of Alexandria. When the saint Päwlos came to them and greeted them, their soul rejoiced when they saw him. When the governor returned to the city of 'Adënew, he (had) the head of the saint Päwlos cut off on the bank of the river, and (the saint) received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Believing people took his body, shrouded it in beautiful clothes, and hid it in their homes. Many miracles occurred from him as well as the healing of the sick. May his prayer and his blessing be with us forever, amen.

የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለጴጥሮስ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also there is the commemoration of Pêtros (= Peter) the martyr. May his intercession be with us for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR 10 YAKKATIT
አመ ፡ ፲ለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ረድእ ፣ ሰማዕት" ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እልፍዮስ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመቶ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ቡሩክ፤ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሰበከ ፡ ስብከተ ፡ ወንጌል ፤ ቅዱስ ፡ ውስተ” ፡ አህጉር ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምኩራበ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ገሃደ፤" ፡ በሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day the holy disciple, the apostle Yä'qob (= James), the son of 'Elfeyos (= Alphaeus) became a martyr. This (took place thus): after the blessed apostle had preached the holy Gospel in many cities, he returned to Jerusalem, entered the temple of the Jews, and preached openly the faith of our Lord the

ክርስቶስ ፡'ወበትንሣኤ ፡ ምውታን ፤፤፡ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልደ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘበአማን ፣፤ ወውእቱ ፡ ህልው ፡፤ እምቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ምስለ ፡ አብ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ንግበር ፡ ሰብአ ፡ በአርአያነ ፡ ወበአምሳሊነ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰረገላ ፣ ኪሩቤል ፣ ወሱራፌል ፤ ይሴብ ሕዎ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ህልው ፡ በየማነ ፡ ዕበይ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መልዕልት ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዘተጸውረ ፡ በከርሠ ፣ ማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፡፤ ወውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘተሰብአ ። ውእቱ፤ ፡ ቃሉ፣ ለሐዋርያ ፡ በማእከለ ፣፤ ውእቶሙ ፡ ጉቡኣን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይፈርህ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለወልደ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ በሞቱ ፡ ወትንሣኤሁ ፡ ወዕርገቱ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወከመዝ ፡ መሐሮሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ጉቡዓን ፡ ሃይማ ኖተ ፡ ከርስቶስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ጉቡኣን ፡ ነገሮ ፡ ለሐዋርያ ፡ ተምዕፁዑ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ መዓተ ፡ ዘእምአቡሆሙ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሐዋርያሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወኀብሩ ፡ 'ኩሎሙ ፡ ላዕሌሁ! ፡ 'ወመሰጥዎ ፣ አይሁድ፤፤ ፡ ወረሰዩ፤ ፡ ደሞ ፡ ላዕለ ፤ ርእሶሙ ፣ ወአኀዝዎ ፡፤ ወአምጽእዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቀላውድዮስ! ፣ ንጉሥ?" ፣ ዘኮነ ፡፤ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፣ ሮሜ ። ወአስተዋደይዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ረድእ ፣ ኀቤሁ?" ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ይሰብክ ፡ለነ ፡ በካልእ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ዘእንበለ ፡ ቄሣር ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ዘንተ" ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውግርዎ ፡ በአእባን ፡ ለሐዋርያ ፡ ያዕቆብ ። ወአፍጠኑ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወወገርዎ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፣፤ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፤ ሰማያት ። ወነሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወገነዝዎ ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ ሰማዕት ። 'ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ኮነ፤ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ንማርያኖስ፡ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮም ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ተወስበት ፡ እኅቱ ፣ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ወረሰየቶ ፡ ንጉሠ ። ወሶበ ፡ ገብአ ፣ ዮስጦስ ፣ እምፀብእ ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ክሕዶ ፡ "ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በእንተዝ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ንቀትሎ ፡ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ወትነብር"'" ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ መንግሥትከ ። ወኢፈቀደ ፡ ዘንተ ፤ ወከልኦሙ ፡፥ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ፡ ወኀረየ ፡ መንግ ሥተ ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ እመንግሥት ፡ ምድራዊት ። ወቀርበ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወተአምነ ፡ ቅድሜሁ፤ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ መኑ ፡ 'ውእቱ ፡ ዘአገበረከ፡፣ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ 'ከመ ፡ ትግበር" ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃድከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ፈርሀ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ፥፤ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢጸሐፍከ። ፡ ገድልየ ፡ 'ከመ ፡ እፈጽም ፡ ስምዕየ፣ ወእመአኮ ፡ አነ ፡ ኣነሥኦሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ ወኣወፅአከ ፡ እመንግሥት ። ወፈርሀ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ወጸሐፈ፥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውስድዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡
Christ and the resurrection of the dead [and he revealed to them that he (is) the Son of the true God, (that) he was before all the universe with the Father and with his Word—for it is said: "Let us make man in our image and in our likeness"—, that he is seated in heaven on the chariot of the cherubim—and the cherubim glorify him—, that he is at the right hand of the Greatness in the heights, that it is he who was carried in the womb of Mary the Virgin, that he is God who became man.] This (was) the word of the apostle without fear in the midst of the (Jews) gathered: a man was a witness of the birth of the only Son of God, of his death, of his resurrection, and of his ascension to his Father in the heavens. Thus did he teach all those (who were) gathered the faith of Christ. When those (who were) gathered heard the word of the apostle, they were seized with a great anger against the apostle of our Lord Christ, (anger) which (came) from their father Satan who was with them, and they all gathered against him.] The Jews seized him and decided (that) his blood (should fall) on their heads. They grabbed him and brought him to Qaläwdyos (= Claudius), the prefect who represented the emperor of Romë (= Rome). They accused this disciple before him and said to him: "This (man) announces to us another emperor than Qésär (= Caesar)." When (the prefect) heard that, he ordered to stone the apostle Yä'qob. All hastened and stoned him; he died in peace and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Believing people took his body, shrouded it, and buried it near the Temple. May his intercession and his blessing be with us, amen. On this day also the saint Yostos (= Justus) became a martyr. This saint was the son of Nemäryänos (= Numerian) the emperor of Rom (= Rome). While he was at war, his sister married Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) the renegade and made him emperor. When Yostos returned from the war, he realized (that) Diyogletyänos had denied our Lord Christ. He was very afflicted because of that. When the people of the country saw him, they all gathered to him and said to him: "We are going to kill Diyogletyänos and you will sit on your throne." He did not want that, forbade them to kill him (= Diyogletyänos), and preferred the heavenly kingdom to the earthly kingdom. He came before the emperor and confessed our Lord Christ before him. The emperor said to him: "Who is the one who pushed you, my lord Yostos, to do this against your will?" The emperor indeed feared him greatly. The saint Yostos said to him: "(As surely as) my Lord Jesus Christ is alive, if you do not write an order for my (last) combat so that I may accomplish my martyrdom, I will raise the people of this city against you and drive you from the empire." (The emperor) was afraid of him, ordered in writing to


[p. 520] {#po.45.p520}
ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወታውክልያ?፥ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ወአቦሊ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወፈነዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ለመኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡፤ እስክንድርያ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ይቲጦ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወኢያሕዝን ፡ ልቦ ፡ ዮጊ ፡ ይትመየጥ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወይትአዘዝ ፡ ለንጉሥ ። ወለእመ ፡ ኢተመይጠ ፡ 'ወእመአኮ ፤ ያፍልጡ፥ ፡ ማእከሌሁ ፡ ወማእከለ ፡፤ ብእሲቱ ፡ ወወ ልዱሩ‹ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ቦእምነ ፡ አግብርቲሁ፥፤ ፡ ወመጽሐፈ ፡ መልእክቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ። ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ፈርሀ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ ይጊጦ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢትግበር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ወኢታሕስር ፡ ክብረከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ኢታብዝሕ ፡፣ ነገረ ፣ ወፈርሀ ፡ እምኔሁ፤ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወፈነዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ 'ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንዴናው ። "ወፈነዎ ፡ ለአቦሊ" ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ በስጣ ፡ ወለታኣውክልያ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ፃየ ። ወለለ ፤ 88እምኔሆሙ ፡ 'ኮነ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ፳ገብር ፤ እምአግብር ቲሁ፤ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ 'ይፌጽም ፡ ስምዖ ፡ የሐሊ ፡ በሥጋሁ። ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ "እስመ ፡ ውእቱቶ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ አንዴናው ። ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡፤ ስምዕ ፣ ዘኢይማስን ፣ በመንግሥት፤" ፡ ዘለዓ ለም ። ወኮነ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ተአምራት፥ ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ ወፈውስ ፡ ለድውያን ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
lead him into the land of Egypt, him, Täwkelyä (= Theoclea) his wife, and 'Aboli his son, and he sent them into the land of Egypt. He ordered the governor of the city of Alexandria to flatter him greatly and not to afflict his heart in the hope that his heart would return and that he would obey the emperor; if he did not return (from his sentiment), that they (should) separate him, his wife, and his son. When (Yostos) arrived in the land of Egypt, (he had) with him some of his servants and the emperor's letter. When the governor of the city of Alexandria saw him, he was very afraid of him, began to flatter him, and said to him: "Do not do that, my lord, and do not tarnish your glory." The saint said to him: "Do not speak further." The governor was afraid of him and sent him to Upper Egypt in the city of 'Andënäw (= Antinoë). He sent 'Aboli his son to the city of Bastä (= Bubastis) and Ta'äwkelyä his wife to the city of Da (= Sais). Each of them had a servant from among his servants, until he had finished his martyrdom, to take care of his body. As for the saint Yostos, he finished his (spiritual) combat beautifully: they cut off his head with the sword in the city of 'Andënäw and he received the unfading crown of martyrdom in the eternal kingdom. Many miracles occurred from his body as well as the healing of the sick. May his intercession, his prayer, and his blessing be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጽሙድ ፡ ማእምር ፡ መስተ ጋድል ፡ ወመምህረ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አባ ፤ ኢስድሮስ፤ ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ ፍርማ። ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ 'እምብዑላነ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወክቡራኒሃ ። "ወኮነ ፡ ዘመዶሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ አባ ፡ ቴዎፍሎስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወኢኮኖሙ ፡ ለአበዊሁ ፡ ውሉድ ፡ ዘእንበሌሁ ፡ ወመሀርዎ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ መንፈሳዌ ፡ ወሥጋዌ ፡ 'ወመሀርዎ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአጽንዖሙ ፡ ወዓቀቦሙ ፡ ገሃደ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተምህረ ፡ ትምህርታተ ፡ ዮናናዊያን ፡ ወአእምሮ ፡ ከዋክብተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወዘየዐውድ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ አጽናፈ'” ፣ ዓለም ፡ ወተለዐለ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ብዙኃን?፤ ፡ በአእምሮቱ ፡ ወበጥበቡ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ጽሙደ ፡ ወመስተጋድለ” ፡ ወትሑተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ከመ ፡ 'ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር" ፡ ተሰናአዉ ፡ ወተማከሩ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ የአኅኀዝዎ ፡ ወይሚምዎ ፡ሊቀ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ጐየ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብረ” ፡ ፈርማ ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ "ውስተ ፡ ደብር”" ፡ በህየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ እምኔሁ” ፡ ኀበ ፡ በዐት ፡ ንእስት ፡ ወነበረ ፤ ውስቴታ ፡ ባሕቲቱ”" ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ። ወደረሰ ፡ ድርሳናተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፤ ወተግሣጻተ ፡ ቦእምኔሆሙ ፡፣ በእንተ ፡ ነገሥት ፤ ወመኳንንት ። ወተርጐመ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ እመጻጸሕፍተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ብሊት ፡ ወሐ ዳስ ። "ወተረክበ ፤ ኖልቆሙ ፡ ለመጸሕፍት ፡ ወድርሳናት ፡ ወተግሣጸት ፡ ወተስእሎ
On this day also died the holy father, the devout, the learned, the ascetic, the doctor of all the ends of the universe, abbä 'Isderos (= Isidore) of the city of Fermä (= Pelusium). The parents of this saint were among the rich of the country and its notables. He was the relative of the saints abbä Tëwoflos (= Theophilus) and abbä Qèrellos (= Cyril), the patriarchs of the city of Alexandria. His parents had no other children than him and they gave him all the spiritual and physical teaching. They taught him all the books of the Church, he learned them by heart and observed them openly. Then he acquired the Greek sciences and the knowledge of the stars of the sky and of what traverses all the ends of the universe. He was raised above many (people) by his science and by his wisdom; he was devout, ascetic, and humble. When he learned that the people of the city had agreed and were meditating with the bishops to seize him and name him patriarch of the city of Alexandria, he fled by night, arrived at the mountain of Farmä, and became a monk there in a monastery. Then he left it, (went) into a small cell, and dwelt there alone for many years. He composed many homilies and exhortations, some regarding kings and governors. He commented on many books among the ancient and new books of the Church. The number of writings, homilies, exhortations, questions that friends


[p. 522] {#po.45.p522}
ታት ፡ ዘተስእልዎ፤፤ ፡ ካልኣን ፡ 'ወመልእክታት ፡ ዘደረሶሙ" ፡ ወፈነዎሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወኤኢስ ፡ ቆልሳት ፡ ወኀበ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወኮነ ፤ ኖልቆሙ ፡ ፻፻ወቭ፻መልእክታት ፡ ወድርሳናት ፤ ወተግሣጻት ። ወኮነ፤ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይጐስዕ ፡ እምውስተ፤ ፡ ልቡ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይጐሥዑ ፡ አፍላጋት ፡ ወይውሕዙ" ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ቴረ ፡ 'ወሖረ ፡ ዛተ ፡ ሑረተ ፡ ሠናይተ፣ ወተጋድሎ። ፡ ዓባየ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ርሥዕና ፡ ሠናይት ፡ 'ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብ
asked him, letters that he wrote and sent to the patriarchs, to the bishops, and to many people; their number is eighteen thousand letters, homilies, and exhortations. The Holy Spirit bubbled in his heart as rivers bubble and flow. When he had accomplished this virtuous conduct and this great (spiritual) combat, had reached a beautiful old age, and had pleased God, he died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ሔር፤ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ፊሎ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወኤሏስ” ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ዘኩነኖ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኢሦዓ ፡ ለእሳት ፡ ወኢሰገደ ፡ ለፀሐይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ መተረ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለኒቆላዎስ" ፡ ወለስምዖን ፡ ወሰደላኮስ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
On this day also there is the saint abbä Filo (= Philon), martyr and bishop of the land of Färs (= Persia), whom the king of Färs (had) tortured as he did not sacrifice to the fire and did not worship the sun. When he was weary of torturing him, (the king) (had) his head cut off with the sword, and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. [On this day also (there is) the commemoration of Nigoläwos (= Nicholas), Sem'on (= Simon), and Sadaläkos. May their prayer and their blessing be with us for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR 11 YAKKATIT
አመ ፡ ፲፳ለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አብ፤ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ በላትያኖስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ማእምር ፡ ወጠቢብ ፡ ወመስተጋድል ። ወተሠይመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ። ወነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፣ ርእሰ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ፲ ወ ፪ዓመተ ፡ በዛሕን ፡ ወበሰላም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ አምልኮ ፡ ወይሰብክ ፡ ሎሙ ፡፣ 'በሃይማኖት ፡ ወጽድቅ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ቀላውድዮስ ፡ መኩንን ላዕለ ፡ ፊልኦፅስ ፤ ንጉሥ ፡ ወቀተሎ ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ። ወአንሥአ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ላዕለ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ መከራ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወምንዳቤ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ወኮኑ ፡ በእደዊሁ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ብዙኃን ። ወጐዩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡'እለ ፡ ዕውቃን ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኖሙ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ በዓት ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ወሐነጸ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኤፌሶን ፡ ምኩራበ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወአንበረ ፡ ውስቴቱ!" ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ ወሦዓ ፡ ሎሙ። ወቀተሎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢይሠውዑ ፡ ሎሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ይትቃረኖ፤፤ ፣ ወይምህሮሙ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትአዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፈነወ ፤ 'ወአብጽሖ ፡ እምሀገረ፤" ፡ ሮሜ፤ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኤፌሶን ፡ 'ወኀሠሠ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day there is the holy father (and) martyr Balätyänos (= Fabian), patriarch of Romë (= Rome). This saint was an honored, learned, wise, and ascetic man. He was named patriarch of the city of Romë. He remained on the seat of Pêtros (= Peter), the chief of the apostles, twelve years, in tranquility and in peace, teaching them (= the Romans) the good worship and preaching to them the faith and justice. When Qaläwdyos (= Claudius) the general rose against the emperor Filpos (= Philip the Arab), he killed him and reigned in his place. This impious one stirred up against the believers a great persecution and many violences. There were, by his hands, many martyrs. Fled far from him the seven children who (are) known as having slept in a cave for many years. He built in the city of 'Éféson (= Ephesus) a great temple, placed idols there, and sacrificed to them. He killed all those who did not sacrifice to them. When he learned that this saint was opposing him and teaching the believers not to obey him, he sent and had him brought from the city of Romë to the city of 'Éféson. He demanded of him that he sacrifice to the idols; (the saint) did not defer to his order, but mocked him


[p. 524] {#po.45.p524}
እምኔሁ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሠፅ፤ ፡ ለጣዖታት ፡ ወኢሰምዓ ፡ ትእዛዞ ፡ አላ ፡ ተሳለቀ ፡ ላዕ ሌሁ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ ወረገሞሙ ። ወተምዓ?፤ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በመጠነ ፤ ዓመት ፡ ፍጽምት ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ 'ወኢተአዘዘ ፡ ሎቱ፤ ፡ መተረ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስብሐት ፤ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስ ቶስ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
and the idols and cursed them. (The emperor) became angry against him and made him undergo great torture for a full year. When he was weary of torturing him, and (as the saint) (still) did not obey him, he (had) his head cut off with the sword and (the saint) received the crown of glory from our Lord Christ. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፤ አውሎግ ፡ አንበሳዊ ፡ ወመስተጋድል ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ!" ፡ ኮኑ ፣ አበዊሁ ፡፤ እምሀገረ ፡ ንጽቢን ፣ ወብዑላን ፣ እሙንቱ ፡፤ በወርቅ ፡ ወበብሩር ፤ ወመሀርዎ ፣፤ ለወልዶሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል" ፡ አዕረፉ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ወኃደጉ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዘከረ ፡ አባ ፡ አውሎግ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ሚጥ፤፤ ፡ ንዋየከ ፡ ወሀብ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡ ወጹር ፡ መስቀለ ፡ ሞትከ ፡ ወነዓ ፣ ትልወኒ ። ወበጊዜሃ ፡ መጽወተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አውጊን። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ተወከፎ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ በፍሥሓ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ርእየ ፡ ራእየ ፡ ዘይቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኅቤከ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወተወክፎ ወደምሮ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እለ ፡ ይትለአኩቶ፤ ፡ ለአኃው ። ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሜክሮ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ይዓመት ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ቅድስናሁ ፡ ወፍጽምናሁ ፡ አልበሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወአስኬማ ፡ እንተ፤'" ፡ ዘመላእክት ። ወነበረ ፡ በጻጸማ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸውም ፡ እምሰንበት ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ወመብልዑኒ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ወፄው ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይሊ ፡ ፄ፻ጸሎታተ ፡፤ መዓልተ ፡፤ ወሌሊተ ፡ ወኢይነውም ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ገድል ፡፣
[On this day also died abbä 'Awlog (= Eulogius) the lion-like and ascetic. The parents of this saint were from the city of Nesbin (= Nisibis) and were rich in gold and silver. They taught their son all the teaching of the Church. Some time later, his parents died and left him many goods. One day, abbä 'Awlog remembered the word of the Gospel which says: "Sell your goods, give everything to the poor and the needy, carry your cross of death and come, follow me." Immediately he gave everything as alms to the poor and went to the monastery of 'Awgin (= Eugene). When (the latter) saw him, he welcomed him to him with joy, for, before 'Awlog came to him, he had seen (in) a vision someone who had said to him: "Behold, a beautiful young man is going to come to you, welcome him and join him to those who serve the brothers (monks)." ('Awgin) remained testing him for three years. When he saw his holiness and his perfection, he clothed him with the monastic garment and the cowl which (is) that of the angels. ('Awlog) lived in great austerity, fasting from one Sunday to the other; his food (was composed of) bread and salt. He said seven hundred prayers by day and by night and did not sleep. || He remained in this (spiritual) combat for thirty years.
ቋዓመተ። ወእምዝ ፤ አስተብቀ' ፡ ለመምህሩ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓት ፡ ንእስት ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትጋደል ፡ ወይትጋኃነይ ፡፤ ፪8ዓመተ ። ወወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፪አናብስተ ፡ ይትለአክዎ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መፍቅዱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሐመ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ጽኑዓ ፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለአናብስት ፡ እፈቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ እስተይ ፡ ማየ ፡ ምውቀ ። ወሖሩ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወረከቡ ፡ ፳ ኖላዌ፤ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሬኢ ፡ አባግዒሁ ፡ ወጾሮ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ለዝኩ ፡ ኖላዊ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ በዓቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ሰገደ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ትፈቅድ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ታስትየኒ ማየ ፡ ምውቀ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መፍቅዶ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ አባ ፡ አውሎግ ፡ ለአናብስት ፡፤ ከመ ፡ ይሚጥዎቓ ፡ ለኖላዊ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደሩ ። ወበጺሖ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ረከብኩ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ዘኢይከውን ፡ ዓለም ፡ መጠነ ፡ ሰኩና ፡ እገሪሁ ። ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወአም ጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ድውያነ ፡ ወፈወሶሙቶ፥0 ። ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፤ መጺኦ፤ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ መምህሩ ፡ አባ ፡ አውጊን ፡ ይሖር፡፣ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ወይሚጦሙ ፡ ለመምለኪያነ ፡ ጣዖት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርት
Then he begged his master (to let him leave) and went into the desert. He entered a small cave and remained fighting (spiritually) and devoting himself to supplications for fifty years. God gave him two lions to serve him in all his needs. Then he fell gravely ill. He said to the lions: "I desire to drink hot water." The lions went into the mountain and met a shepherd in the process of grazing his sheep. (One of the) lions carried off this shepherd and brought him to the door of the saint's cave. When (the shepherd) saw him, he prostrated himself and said to him: "What do you want, my father?" ('Awlog) said to him: "I want you to give me hot water to drink." (The shepherd) executed his wish, then abbä 'Awlog enjoined the lions to bring the shepherd back to his dwelling. Once arrived, (the latter) spoke to all the people saying: "I have met a man from the soles of whose feet the universe is not worthy (to be trodden)." Having heard his (= 'Awlog's) story, all the people gathered to him and brought him all the sick, and he healed them. Having come in those days, his master abbä 'Awgin wanted to go into the land of Färs (= Persia) and convert the idolaters to the right faith. He desired


[p. 526] {#po.45.p526}
ዕት ፤ ወፈተወ ፡ ይንሥኦ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ለወልዱ ፡ አባ ፡ አውሎግ ። ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይል እክ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ አለበዎ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለአንበሳ ፡ ወጾረ ፡ ዓጽፎ ፡ ወወንጌሎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፤ ወአመሮ ፡ ሐዊረ ፡ ወተለዎ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ማር ፡ አውጊን ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ፣ ተቀበሎ ፡ በፍሥሐ ። እንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ጤግሮስ ፡ ወተከፍለ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወዓደዉ ። ወበጺሖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ሜጥዎሙ ፡ ለመምለኪያነ ፡ ጣዖት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፣ ወአጥመቅዎሙ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፣፣፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርኅቡ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ አዘዞሙ ፡ ማር ፡ አውሎግ ፡ ለአናብስት ፡ ይኅሥሥሠ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ወሐዊሮሙ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ አረጋዌ ፡ ፅዒኖ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዓድጉ ፡ ወዲቤሁ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ወጾርዎ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ለዝኩ ፡ ዓድግ፣ ወአብጽሕዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ ወእምብዝኃ ፡ ፍርሃት ፡ ሞተ ፡ ዝኩ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ አባ ፡ አውሎግ ፡ አሕየዎ ።
to take with him his (spiritual) son abbä 'Awlog. Before he had sent him (a message), the Holy Spirit warned (one of the) lions; (the latter) took the saint's mantle and gospel and indicated to him to set out on the march. (The saint) followed him, arrived near mär 'Awgin, and, when (the latter) saw him, he received him with joy. Making their way, they arrived at the river Tëgros (= Tigris), the water divided for them and they crossed. Arrived at the city, they converted the idolaters to the right faith and baptized them in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit. One day, while the saints were hungry, mär 'Awlog ordered the lions to look for food for them. Having set out on the road, they met an old man having loaded bread on his donkey—on this one, (there was) a small child. The lions carried off the donkey and brought it to the saints; the child died of fear. When abbä 'Awlog prayed, he brought him back to life.
ወእምዝ ፡፤ ተመይጠ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓቱ ፡ ወደወየ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ ወተቀብረ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ክብር ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ እሉ ፡ አናብስት ፡ እንዘ ፡ የዓቅቡ ፡ መቃብሪሁ ፡ ፲ወ፪ዓመተ ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፤ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
After that, he returned to his cell, had a small illness, died, and was buried in great honor. The lions remained guarding his tomb for twelve years. May his blessing be with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፤ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለአብርሃም ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ወአኪንቲ ፡ ወአባ ፡ መቃቢስ ፡ መነኮስ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also there is the commemoration of 'Abrehäm (= Abraham) the bishop, (of) 'Akinti and (of) abbä Magäbis (= Maccabeus) the monk. May their prayer and their blessing be with us for ever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡፣ በትረ ፡ ረድአ ፡ አባ ፡ ስልዋኖስ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ንቡረ ፣ ውስተ ፡ በዓት ፡ ዘደብረ ፡ ሲና ፡ኮነ ፡ የዓቅብ፣ ትኅርምተ ፡ ወበጊዜ ፡ ድራር ፡ ይሁብ ፤ ለሥጋሁ-፥" ፡ መፍቅዶ ። ወሶበ ፡ ረሰይዎ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ፈርኑ ፡ አድከመ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በብዙኅ ፡ ትኅርምት ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ኦአቡነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ኢኮንከ ፡ ትትጌረም ፡ ከመዝ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አሜነሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ በገዳም ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ንዴት ፡፣ ወትኅርምትቶ ፡ ኮንኩ ፡ እሠርዕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይድወይ ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ ወይእዜሰ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ ማእከለ ፤ ማኅበር ፣ ወብየ ፣ መፍቅድ ፡ ብዙኅ ፣፤ ወይዔይስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኣግርር ፤ ሥጋየ ፡ ወእቅንዮ ። ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አዕረፈ ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፤ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፣ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also died abbä Batra, the disciple of abbä Selwänos (= Silvanus). This saint, when he resided in a cell of Dabra Sinä, observed abstinence and, at the time of dinner, gave his body what it needed. When they made him bishop of the city of Farnu, he weakened himself greatly by a great abstinence. His disciples said to him: "Our father, when you were in the desert, you did not observe such abstinence." He said to them: "Then, being in the desert in the midst of poverty and abstinence, I had regulated myself so that my body would not be sick. Now, I am in the midst of a community, I have many needs, and it is preferable that I subdue my body and dominate it." He remained fighting much (spiritually), then he died. May his intercession be with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR 12 YAKKATIT
አመ ፡ ፲፪ለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡፤ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፤ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሩ፤ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ሚካኤል ። እስመ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ "ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት፤ ፡'ለመልአክ ፡ክቡር፣
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day (there is) the commemoration of the glorious angel, the archangel Mikä'el (= Michael). On this day God—glory to him!—sent the angel
