glorious, the archangel Mikä'ël to Somson (= Samson) the giant, and (the archangel) helped him until he defeated the people of Felset'em (= the Philistines). When (the latter) wanted to kill him, God gave him strength against them and he made them perish. He killed a thousand of them in a single day with the jawbone of a donkey. When he was thirsty and was near to dying, Mikä'ël the archangel appeared to him and strengthened him. God made water flow for him from the bone of the donkey's jawbone: (Somson) drank and was saved. When the people of Felset'em gathered and laid a trap for him with his wife, they blinded his eyes and led him into their temple of idols. Mikä'ël appeared to him, gave him strength, and he killed them all.] May his intercession be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ ጽሙድ ፡ ገላስዮስ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ መሃይምናነ ፡ ፈራህያነ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ። ወመሃርዎ፤ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትምህርተ፤! ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ዲያቆነ ። ወመነነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ዓለመ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ ወጾረ ፡ ዓርኡቶ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በምንኩስና ፣ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወሰጊድ ፡፤ ወትጋህ! ። ወኀረዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተሠይመ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መነኮሳተ! ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ተጋድሎቶ ፡፣ ወተፀምዶቶ ፡ ወአምልኮቶ" ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ አስተር አዮ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡፤ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተጋብእ ፡ ወራዙተ፤ ፡ መነኮሳተ ፣ ወይምሀሮሙ ፡ ፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወፍኖተ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወገብረ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት፤ ፡ ወሠርዓ ፡ ሱታፌ ፡ መንፈሳዊተ ፡፤ ወርእዮሙ ፡ መርዔተ ፣ መለኮታዊተ ። ወኢኮነ ፡ 'በማእከሌሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ አላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ገብሮሙ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ዘይንእስ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ።
On this day died the holy father, the (spiritual) combatant, the devout Galäsyos (= Gelasius). The parents of this saint were believers (and) feared Christ. They taught him all the teaching of the Church, and he was ordained deacon. He disdained this world from his childhood and carried the yoke of Christ. He waged a great (spiritual) combat in the monastic state, in fasting and in prayer, prostration and vigil. God chose him and he was ordained priest for the monks of the desert of 'Asqêtes (= Scetis). When he had brought to an end his (spiritual) combat, his devotion, and his adoration, an angel of God appeared to him as he had appeared to the saint Päkuemis (= Pachomius). He ordered him to gather young monks and to teach them the fear of God and the monastic way. He did as the angel of God had ordered (him): he gathered the monks, established a spiritual community, and guarded them with a divine guard. Among them he was not like one of them, but like their servant and like the least among them.
ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ መነነ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጥሪተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ 'ወኮነ ፡ የዋሃ ፣ ፈድፋደ ። ወአጽሐፈ ፡ አሐደ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ኩሎ፣፥ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ብሊተ ፡ ወሐዳሰ ። ወወፅአ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዓስበ ፡ ጽሕፈቱ ፡ ፲ወቿዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አንበሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያን ብቡ ፤ ውስቴቱ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡፣ መነኮሳት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይፈቅዱ ፡ ይትፈወሱ ፡ እምኔሁ፥" ። ወቦአ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ፈላሲ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሐውጾ ፡ ለአረጋዊ ፡ አባ ፡ ገላስዮስ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ውእተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ አደሞ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምህየ ፡ ሰረቆ ፡ ወወ ሰዶ ። 'ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ፈቀደ፥፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚጦ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ፳ብእሲ፤ ፣ ሚመጠን ፡ ሜጡ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ ፲ወፄዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡፣ ውእቱ ፡ ዘይሣየጦ ፡ እወ ፡ እሁበከ" ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤ ፡ አላ ፡ ተዓገሥ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኣርእዮ ፡ ለዓርክየ ። ወወሀቦ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ወነሥኦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሣየጦ ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ገላስዮስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአዮ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፣
This father had disdained all the fortune of this world and was very gentle. He had a large book written, had gathered in it all the books of the Old and New Testament, and had paid eighteen gold pieces for the price of its copying. Then he placed it on the lectern in the church so that all the monks might read it whenever they wished to draw comfort from it. A traveler entered (the church) to visit the old man Abba Galasyos. When he saw the book, it pleased him, and when he left there, he stole it and carried it away. After that, he went to the city (and) wanted to sell it. A man said to him: "What is its price?" He said to him: "Give me sixteen gold pieces." The one who (wanted) to buy it said to him: "Yes, I will give you according to what you say, but grant me a delay until I have shown it to my friend." (The man) gave it to him, and the man who wanted to buy it took it and carried it to the holy Abba Galasyos so that (the latter)

ሠናየ ፡ ወየሀብ ፡ ሜጦ ፡ ለበዓለ ፡ መጽሐፍ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ገላስዮስ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለዘአምፅኦ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሚመጠነ ፡ ኀሠሠ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ሜጦ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ 'ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ኀሠሠ ፡ እምኔየ፤ ፡ ፲ወ ሄዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ገላስዮስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተሣየጦ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወቀሊል ፡ ሜጡ ። ወነሥኦ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ብእሲ ፡ ወገብአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽአ ፡ "ውእቱ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ይኅሥሥቶ ፡ እም ኔሁ ፡ ሜጦ ፡ ወኢነገሮ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አላ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ፣ አርአይክዎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ገላስዮስ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ሜጡ ፡ በዝኃ፥ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ፣ ዘሠረቆ ፡ ኢይቤለከኑ፥ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አልቦ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ዘሠረቆ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይፈቅድ ፡ እሚጦ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ገላስዮስ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወነስሐ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኃጢአትየ ፡ ወንሣእ ፤ መጽሐፈ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ አስሐተኒ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይፈቅድ ፡ እንሥኦ፥ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ፤ ፣ ለእመ ፤ ኢነሣእኮ= ፡ ኦአቡየ ፣፤ ኢይከውነኒ ፤ ሊተ ፡ ዕረፍተ ፡ ወኢይትአተት ፡ እምላዕሌየ ፡ ሐዘን ። ወሰገደ ፡ 'ብዙኃ ፡ ስግደታተ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፤ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምሀሮ ፡ ወይትቀበልቶ፤ ፡ መጽሐፎ ። ወበጻማ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወግብር ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወኢያእመረ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ 'ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምኔሁ" ።
might see if it was beautiful (enough) for (the buyer) to give the price to the owner of the book. When the holy Abba Galasyos saw it, he said to the one who had brought it to him: "What price does he ask of you for it?" The man said to him: "He asks me sixteen gold pieces for it." The holy Abba Galasyos answered him and said to him: "Buy it, for it (is) beautiful and its price (is) modest." The man took it (= the book) and returned to his home. When (the seller) came to his house to demand the price for it, (the buyer) did not speak to him as this father had told him, but he said to him: "I showed it to Abba Galasyos and he said to me: 'The price is high.'" The brother who had stolen it (= the book) said to him: "Did the old man not say any other word to you?" The (buyer) said to him: "No." The one who had stolen it said to him: "I do not want to sell it (anymore)." Then he took it, went to the holy Abba Galasyos, prostrated himself before him, repented, wept before him, implored him, and said to him: "Forgive me my sin and take (back) your book; Satan seduced me." The saint said to him: "I do not want to take it." The brother said to him: "If you do not take it, my father, I will have no rest, and affliction will not depart from me." And he made many prostrations at the feet of the saint and begged him to have pity on him and to accept his book. With great effort and difficulty, (the saint) took it back from him, and no one knew what had become of him (= the thief).
ወወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ገላስዮስ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ትንቢት ፡ ወገ ቢረ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ ዓሣ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብር ፤ ለመነኮሳት ፤ ወአብሰሎ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ጸብሕ ፡ ወአንበሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓት" ፡ ወረሰዮ፤፤ ፡ ለ፳ሕፃን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕቀቦ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ግብሩ ፡ ወበልዓ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ እምነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዓሣ ፡ ብዙኃ" ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ጸብሕ ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ ዘበልዓ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ዐሣ5 ፡ 'ተቄጥዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ፥ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እፎ ፡ ትበልዕ ፤ እምቅድመ ፡ "ጊዜ ፡ በሊዕ ፡ ወእምቅድመ፥ ፡ ይባርኩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አረጋዊያን ፡ ወቅዱሳን ። ወእምግብረ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ቦአ፥ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ቀ፡ጥዓ ፡ ወረገጾ ፡ በእግሩ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ ዲበ ፣ ምድር ፡ ወሞተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ደንገፀ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወበጽሖ ፡ ፍርሃት ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ለአረጋዊ ፡ አባ ፡ ገላስዮስ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጹሮ ፡ ወአስክቦ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ። ወሖረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ፤ ፡ አረጋይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቦኡ ፡'አረጋይ ፡ ወመነኮሳት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ 'ወጸለዩ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ሰርክ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸሙ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ አረጋይ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ "ተንሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይተልዎ ፡ ወኢያእመረ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ እመነኮሳት ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ። ወቀነኖ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ለበዓለ ፡ ጸብሕ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይንግር ፡
God gave this father Abba Galasyos the gift of prophecy and of performing miracles and wonders. One day, a fish was brought to the monastery for the monks; the cook cooked it, put it in a cell, appointed a child to guard it, and went about his business. The child ate much of the fish. When the cook arrived, he noticed that (the child) had eaten the fish. He became angry with him and said to him: "How could you (have) eaten before the time to eat and before the elders and the saints had blessed it!" And, by the work of Satan, fury entered into him and he struck him with his foot. (The child) fell to the ground and died. When (the cook) saw that he was dead, he was greatly distressed and was seized with fear; he went and told the old man Abba Galasyos how (it) had happened. The saint said to him: "Bring him and lay him before the sanctuary (of the church)." The (cook) went and did as the old man had ordered (him). When the old man and the monks entered the church, they performed the evening prayer. When they had finished the prayer, the old man went out of the church, the child rose up following him, and none of the monks knew how (this) had come to pass. The old man imposed a penance on the cook and ordered him not to speak of this to any of the monks except after his (= the old man's) death.
ለመኑሂ ፡ እመነኮሳት ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዕረፍቱ" ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'አባ ፡ ገላስዮስ ፣ ኩሎ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ተጋድሎ። ፡ ረ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዘይናዝዞ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሖረ ፡እምዝንቱ፣፡
When the holy Abba Galasyos had completed all (his) beautiful deeds and all (his) virtuous (spiritual) combat, the moment when God was to console him


[p. 532] {#po.45.p532}
ዓለም ፡፤ ኃላፊ ፡ ወኀደገ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ቴረ ። ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ወይባርከነ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካረ ፣ ድርስቅልስ?፤ ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
arrived; he left this transitory world, left behind this virtuous memory, pleased God, and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us and may he bless us at all times, forever and ever, amen. [On this day also there is the commemoration of Dersqeles. May his intercession be with us, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 13TH OF YAKKATIT
አመ ፡ ፲፻ለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሰርጊስ፤ ፡'ሰማዕት ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ አትሬብ ፡ ወአቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ወአኃዊሁ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ራነ ፡፤ ወኮነ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡፤ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወስማ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ማርያ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ፳ዓመተ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሙት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ፣ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቀ፥ጵርያኖስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወተአምነ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡፤ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንንዎ ፡ ወኩነንዎ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ "ወእምዝ ፡ ሞቅሕዎ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወመሰጥዋ፤ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰማይ ፤ ወርእየ ፣ ማኅደሪሆሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ። ወተናዘዘት ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ወፈወሶ፤! ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡'እምቀስለ ፡ ሕማሙ!፤ ። ወሰምዓ ፡'በእንተ ፡ ተጋድሎቱ ፡ ቀሲስ ዘስሙ ፡ አባ ፡ ማጹን ፡ ወ፪ዲያቆናት ፡ ወተንሥኡ ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንን ፡'በሀ ገረ ፡ አትሪብ፤ ። ወተአምኑ ፡ በቅድሜሁ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ፤ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአ ዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅሥፍዎ ፡ መቅሠፍተ?” ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ የዐውድዎሙ፤ ፡ ወየሐዝኑ ፡ 'ለውእቱ ፡ ቀሲስ፤! ። ወቀሲ ስሰ ፡ሜጠ ፤ገጾ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ወገሠጾሙ ፡ ወመሀሮሙ ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡!ከመ ፡ይጽንዑ"፡'፣ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ዘእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወነዝሐ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፤ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወተአምኑ ፡ "በስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶሙ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይደይዎ ፡ ለቀሲስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምንዳደ ፡ ብለኔ ፡ ወአድኅኖ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምኔሁ፤ ። ወአስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡፻ጊዚዜ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አውፅኦ ፡'መልአከ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምውስተ ፡ እቶን ። ወፈነዎ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, the holy (and) glorious Sargis (= Sergius) who (was) from the city of 'Atrëb (= Athribis) became a martyr, and with him, his father, his mother, his brothers, and many people. The parents of this saint were virtuous; the name of his father was Téwodros (= Theodore) and the name of his mother, Märyä (= Mary). When his days were twenty years, he thought in his heart to die for the name of Christ. He came to Quepreyänos (= Cyprian) the governor and confessed our Lord Christ before him. (The governor) ordered that he be tortured, and he was subjected to great torture; then he was imprisoned. Our Lord Christ gave an order, his soul was carried to heaven, and he saw the dwellings of the saints. His soul was consoled and God healed him of the wounds (due to) his sufferings. A priest whose name (was) Abba Mäsun - as well as two deacons - learned of his (spiritual) combat. They arose and came to the governor in the city of 'Atrib and confessed the name of our Lord Christ before him. (The governor) ordered them to be subjected to great scourging, and it was done to them as he (had) ordered. All the people surrounded them and grieved for the priest. The priest turned his face toward him, exhorted him, instructed him, and enjoined him to be firm in the right faith of our Lord Christ. Then the priest prayed over water and sprinkled it on him, and the Holy Spirit descended upon him. (The people) confessed the name of our Lord Christ, they all had their heads cut off with the sword, and they received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Then the governor ordered that the priest be thrown into the furnace of a bath, and God saved him from it. The angel of God appeared to him and taught him that he would be a martyr three times for the name of Christ; then the angel of God brought him out of the furnace. The governor sent him to the city of Alexandria, he completed his (spiritual) combat there, and received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven.
ወበህየ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ተጋድሎቶ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት?" ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ሰርጊዮስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቀጵርያኖስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ አብጽሖ፥ ፡ ወኩነኖ፣
As for the saint Sargiyos, Quepreyänos the governor had him brought and made him


[p. 534] {#po.45.p534}
ዓቢየ ፡ ኩነኔ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አብጽሐቶ፥፤ ፡ መንኩራኩራተ ፡ ዘብርት ፤ ወአኬድዎ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መልያልያቲሁ ፡ ክፍለ ፡ ክፍለ ፡ 'ወመተረ ፡ ኩሎ። ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወአንሥኦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ሙስና ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡፤ ጣዖታተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስግድ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወረገጾ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በዕግሩ ፣፡ ወወድቀ ፡ ወተቀጥቀጠ ። ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ቀጵርያኖስቶ ፡ አምነ፥ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስ ቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐትቶ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አምላክሰ ፡ ዘኢይክል ፡ ያድኅን ፡ ነፍሶ?" ፡ እፎ ፡ ይክል ፡ ያድኅን ፤ ካልኦ፥ ። ወአውህዮስሰ" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሰራዊት ፡ ኩነኖ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ሰርጊዮስ፤ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡፤ ዓቢየ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጥብሑ ፡ ማእሶ ፣ ወይመዝም ዝዎ ፡ በብሒእ ፡ ወፄው ፡ ድሙር ፡! ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ወኅይለ ። ወሶበ፣ ሰምዓ ፡ ቦቱ ፡እሙ ፡ ወእኅቱ ፡ በጽሓ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእያሁ ፡ በከያ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ "ወመጠወት ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ነፍሳ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ብዝኃ ፡ ሐዘን ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወጸለየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ወኀሠሠ ፡ እምኔሁ፥ ፡ ወተንሥአት ፡ እኅቱ ፡ እሞት ፡ በጊዜሃ ፡ ሕያውታ ። 'ወመጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁቶፅ" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዩልዮስ ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፣ አቅፋሕስ ፡ ጸሓፌ ፡ ገድለ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ወጠየቀ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ ወተካየዶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይሔሊ ፡ በሥጋሁ ፡ ወይገንዞ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ አውህያኖስ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንንዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ በመንኩራኩራት፤፤ ፡ ወይረስዩ= ፡ መሀትወ ፡ እሳት ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዘኒሁ ፡ ወይምልሑ ፡ አጽፋረ ፡ እደዊሁ ፤ ወእገሪሁ፤ ፡ ወይስቅሉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዱ ፡ እብነ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ያስክብዎ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዓራተ ፡ ሐጺን ፡ ወያንድዱ ፤ መትሕቴሁ ፡፤ እሳተ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ። ወእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ያጸንዖ ፡
undergo great torture. Then he (had) bronze wheels brought, they crushed Sargiyos with them - his limbs were (divided) into pieces - and (the governor had) his whole body cut; our Lord Christ - glory to him! - raised him up unharmed, without damage. After that, an idol was brought to him so that he might prostrate himself before it, but the saint struck it with his foot and it fell and broke. When Quepreyänos saw (this), he believed in our Lord Christ - glory to him! - and said: "How could a god who cannot save himself save another?" 'Awhyos (= Euchéus?) the general subjected the saint Sargiyos to great torture; he ordered his skin to be torn off and for him to be rubbed with vinegar and salt mixed together. God gave him grace and strength. When his mother and his sister learned of it, they came to him: when they saw him, they wept over him. His sister gave up the ghost in the greatness of the affliction (that she felt) for him. The saint prayed to God and implored him, and his sister rose alive from the dead at that very moment. The saint Yulyos (= Julius) who (was) from the city of 'Agfähs (= Agfahs), the scribe of the (spiritual) combat of the martyrs, came to him, inquired about his (spiritual) combat, and promised him that he would take care of his body and bury it. Then 'Awhyänos ordered the saint to be tortured with wheels, to have torches of fire put in his ears, to have the nails of his hands and feet torn off, to have a large stone hung around his neck, and then to have him laid on an iron bed and to light a fire under him. This was done as 'Awhyänos had ordered. Our Lord strengthened him, healed his wounds, and raised him up unharmed, without damage.
ወይፌውስ ፡ ቀስላቲሁ ፡ ወያነሥኦ ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሰራዊት ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ወአኃዊሁ ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አዝማዲሁ ፡ ወሰብአ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአይዎ ። ወረከቡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ልጓመ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ይስሕብዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፤ ርእሶ" ። ወረገምዎ ፡ ለመኩንን ፡ ወለጣዖታቲሁ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶሙ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡፤ ምስለ ፤ ቅዱስ ፡ ሰርጊዮስ0 ፡፣ 'ወመተሩ ፡፣ ርእሶሙ ፣ ለኩሎሙ፤ ፣፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወኮነ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ፳ሕካፃን ፡ ንኡስ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ 'ወእንዘ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ያዓርግዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወጸርኃ ፡ በቃል ፡ ልዑል ፡፤ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ተሣሀለኒ ። ወፈርሁ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይስማዕ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወኢያጥፍኦሙ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ይፌጽሙ ፡ አፉሁ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይጸርሕ ፡ ወይኬልሕ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሰከቡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡'ወኮኑ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፡ መጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወአዕረግዋ ፤ ምስሌሆሙ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
When the general was weary of torturing him, he ordered his head to be cut off with the sword. When the saint heard this, he rejoiced greatly. He sent for his father, his mother, and his brothers, and all his relatives, as well as his people, came to see him. They saw a bridle in his mouth, and the soldiers were pulling him to cut off his head. They insulted the governor and his idols; (the latter) ordered all of them to be beheaded with the saint Sargiyos; they all had their heads cut off and they received the crown of life in the kingdom of heaven. There was in the crowd a small child, God opened his eyes, he saw the souls of the holy martyrs, the angels making them ascend into the heavens, and he cried out with a loud voice, saying: "My Lord Jesus Christ, have pity on me!" His parents feared that the governor would hear and that he would have them put to death because of him, and they closed his mouth while he was crying out and exclaiming toward our Lord Christ. After that, they lay upon him and remained thus until he gave up the ghost. He received the crown of life in the kingdom of heaven; the angels took his soul and made it ascend with them. May the prayer and blessing of them all be with us, amen.


[p. 536] {#po.45.p536}
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምኖልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፲፳ወ፪። ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ በጽሖ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወመከራ" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ርትዕት ። ወኮነ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳዊሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ። ወውእቱ ፡ የዐውድ ፡ እምሀገር ፡ እስከ ፡ ሀገር ፤ ወእምደብር ፡ እስከ ፡ ደብር ፡፤ ወእመካን ፡ እስከ ፡ መካን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያጸንዖሙ ፡ ለመሃይምናን” ፡ ምስለ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ። ወአስተርአዩ ፣ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እኩያን ፡ መጽኡሩ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ቀስስጥንጥንያ” ፡ እምአለ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ በግዕዙ ፡ ለአውጣኪ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ዘአስተርአየ'! ፡ በምትሐት ፡፤ ወክሕደ ፡ ሕማማቲሁ”" ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ መድኃኒነ ። ወአውገዞሙ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፤ ወሰደዶሙ ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወአውገዞሙ' ፡ ለእለ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ በቃ ሎሙ” ። ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አንስጣስዮስ ፡ 'ንጉሥ ፡ መሃይምን?”፤ ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ዮስጢያኖስ” ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ከልቄዶናዊ ። ወሜሞ ፡ "ለአባ ፡ሊናርዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፤ ይሚጦሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ እኪት ፤ ዘለማኅበረ ፡፤ ከልቄዶንያ ። ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ቀስጥንጥንያ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳዊሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወለኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሳተ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ እምኔሆሙ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእመኑ፤ ፡ በሃይማ ኖቱ ፤ ርኩስት ፡፤ ዘከልቄዶንያ ፡ ወኢሰምዑ ፡፤ እምትእዛዙ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌ ሆሙቶ፥ ፡ "እምኔሁ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዓቢይ፤ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ። ወነበረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ፲ ወዓመተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ።
On this day also died the holy father Abba Timotëwos (= Timothy), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarch fathers, he is the thirty-second. Great tribulations and trials reached this fighting (spiritual) father because of the right faith. Abba Säwiros (= Severus), patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch), came to the land of Egypt in those days. He circulated from city to city, from monastery to monastery, and from place to place, strengthening the believers with the father Abba Timotëwos. In the time of this father, bad people appeared in the land of Egypt; they had come from the city of Constantinople, (being part) of those who believed in the opinion of 'Awtäki (= Eutyches) the impure, who (thought that the human nature of Jesus was) an illusion and denied the sufferings of Christ our Savior. This father excommunicated them, drove them from the land of Egypt, and excommunicated those who believed in their word. In the time of this father, 'Anestäsyos (= Anastasius) the believing emperor died and Yostiyänos (= Justinian) the Chalcedonian renegade reigned in his place. He appointed Abba Linäryos (= Apollinaris?) patriarch in the place of Abba Timotëwos. He wanted to convert all (those of) the right faith to the evil faith of the council of Kalgëdonyä (= Chalcedon). He gathered a council in the city of Constantinople, brought Abba Säwiros, patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä, and his bishops from the land of the East, and wanted (to demand) of them that they believe in his impure faith of Kalgëdonyä, but they did not obey his order. Because of him, great tribulations befell them, as well as all (those of) the right faith. This father remained on his patriarchal throne for seventeen years and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us forever and ever, amen.
ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ፋሲለደስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ካፍስ፣ "ወአባ ፡ ሕፍስ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also became martyrs Téwodros (= Theodore) son of Fäsiladas (= Basilides), Abba Käfes, and Abba Hefes. May their prayer and their blessing be with us forever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስምዓ ፣ ካልእ ፤ አውሳብዮስ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፤ ወጥ ዑመ ፡ ዜና ፡ ወልደ ፤ አውሳብዮስ ፡ መስፍን ፡ ዘሶርያ ፡ ወሎቱ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ምልእተ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ አውሎፍያ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሐፀንዎ ፡ በተግሣጽ ፡ ወበጥበብ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሐፀዩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ላህይተ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮም ፡ ወአእተዉ ፡ሎቱ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ክብር ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ወይቤላ አምክርኒ ፡ ምክረ ፡ ይቴይሰኒኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንሣእ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ወእንበር ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሕሊናየ ፡ ኢይፈቅድ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አላ ፡ እጽሕቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ እመንን ፡ ዓለመ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ ምንት ፡ ይበቀዓከ ፡ ጣዕመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኃላፊ ፡ ይቴይሰከ ፡ ነቢር ፡ በድንግልና ፡
[On this day also became a martyr the second 'Awsäbyos (= Eusebius) whose mention is beautiful and history sweet, son of 'Awsäbyos governor of Soryä (= Syria); he had a sister full of the Spirit whose name (was) 'Awlofyä (= Eulophia). This saint was raised in discipline and wisdom, then he was betrothed to a charming woman, daughter of the emperor of Rom (= Rome), and she was brought to him in great honor. The very same day, he went to his sister and said to her: "Give me advice: is it better for me to take a wife and to dwell with her (than the opposite)? My thought does not want that; on the contrary, I ardently desire to despise the world." (His sister) said to him: "What will the sweetness of this transitory world serve you? It is better for you to dwell in virginity like Demêtros (= Demetrius) the patriarch."


[p. 538] {#po.45.p538}
ከመ ፤ ድሜጥሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፤ ምሴተ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መር ዓሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀለወት ፣ መርዓት ፡ ዘአዕተዉ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ ክሳዳ ፡ ወሰዓመ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ኦሆ ፡ በልኒ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እንግርኪ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ብእሲቱ፤ ፡ በል ፡ ንግረኒ ። ወተካየደ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ኦእኅትየ ፡ አእምሪ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፍትወቱ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ የኃልፍ ፡ ስንሂ ፣ ላህይ"። ፡ ይማስን ፤ ወኢይበቀዕ ፣ ምንትኒ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፣ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ወይእዜኒ ፡ ይቴይሰነ ፡ ንንበር ፣ በድንግልና ፤ እምናርኩስ ፡ ምኩራበ ፡ ሥጋነ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ አሆ ፡ እቱየ ፡ አነሂ ፤ ኢይፈቅድ ፡ አውስቦ ።
When it was evening, he entered the bridal chamber where the fiancée who had been brought to him was; he took her neck, embraced (her), and said to her: "Have indulgence toward me, that I may tell you a beautiful word." His wife said to him: "Speak, tell me." He made a pact with her and said to her: "My sister, know that the lust of the world passes, (that) its beauty (is) a withering charm, and (that) nothing is profitable except only to practice justice. Now it is better for us to dwell in virginity rather than to defile the temple of our bodies." She said to him: "Yes, my brother, I too do not want to marry."
ወወጠኑ ፤እንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ኅቡረ ፡ በፍኖተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ፪ዓመተ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ በንጽሕና ። ወኢይበልዕ ፡ እክለ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይትመጠዉ ፡ ቀርባነ ፡ ወይሰክቡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፳ዓራት ፤ ወለለኩሉ ፤ ሌሊት ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ። ወመዓልተ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ አትራ ፣ ወይሰግዱ ፡ በበሣ፻ጊዜ ፤ ወመዝ ሙረ ፡ዳዊትኒ ፡ ወወንጌለ ፡ ዮሐንስሂ ፡ ያነብቡ ፡ ወትረ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ርእየቶ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሶርያ ፡ ወአዘዘት ፡ ፪ አግብርቲሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽእዎ ፡ ለአውሳብዮስ ። ወሶበ ፣ አምጽእዎ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ ነጸፈት ፡ ዘርቤታተ ፡ ወአልባሳተ ፡ ደርከኖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓራታ ፡ ወተሠርገወት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዳ ፡ ቃማ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ለአውሳብዮስ ፡ ነዓ ፡ ንበር ፤ ዝየ ፡ እንግርከ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፣፤ ውስተ ፡ ልብየ ። ወይቤላ ፤ አውሳብዮስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይነብር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ ቀዊምየ ፡ ንግርኒ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ አንቀልቀለ ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ ወተመስወ ፡ አዕጽምትየ ፡ እምብዝኃ ፡ አፍቅሮትከ ፡ ወነዓ ፡ ስክብ ፡ ምስሌየ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አውሳብዮስ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይሰክብ ፡ ምስሌኪ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ድልወትየ ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመዝ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደት ፡ ተአኃዞ" ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወሮጸ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ጸውዓቶሙ ፣ ለአግብርቲሃ ፤ ወትቤሎሙ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየኒ ፡ አውሳብዮስ ፡ በአዕይንቲሁ ፡ አኃዘኒ ፡፤ ፃዕር ፤ ወሕማም ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መሠርየ ። ወአዘዘት ፡ ፲ወ፪ረበናተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውፅእዎ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወያምጽእዎ ፡ ኀቤሃ ።
Both began to travel together the path of justice. They dwelt for two years in purity. They did not consume food before having received communion. They lay in the same bed and, every night, stood in a river. During the day, they went to a church whose name (was) 'Aträ, prostrated themselves each time four thousand times, and read continually both the psalms of Däwit (= David) and the gospel of Yohannes (= John). One day, the wife of the king of Soryä saw 'Awsäbyos and she ordered two of her servants to bring him. When they had brought him to her, she spread furs and purple garments on her bed, adorned her neck with a gold necklace, and said to 'Awsäbyos: "Come, sit here, that I may tell you what is in my heart." 'Awsäbyos said to her: "I will not sit with the wife of the king; speak to me while I am standing." She said to him: "My flesh trembles and my bones liquefy because of the greatness of (my) love for you; come, lie with me." The saint 'Awsäbyos answered and said to her: "I will not lie with you, which would not be fitting for me, and the commandment of God is not so." When she wanted to seize him, he left her, ran, and entered a church. Then (the queen) called her servants and said to them: "When 'Awsäbyos looked at me with his eyes, an intense pain and suffering seized me, for he is a magician." She ordered fourteen elders to bring him out of the church and bring him to her.
ወሶበ ፤ አብጽሕዎ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ አዘዘት ፣ ይዝብጥዎ ፡ በአብትረ ፡ ሐዩን ፡ ወይአስሩ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ ፡ ወይስቅልዎ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፳ዕፀው ፡ ወሰቀልዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገር ። ወፈነወት ፡ አሐተ ፡ እምአዕማቲሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትበሎ ፡ አሆ ፡ በላ ፡ ለእግዝእትየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታውርድከ ፡ እምስቅለት ፡ ወኢሰምዓ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዘማ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ወጠነ ፡ ጸልዮ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያውሕዝ ፡ አንብዖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማየ ፡ ክረምት ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኦእግዚኦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ አንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘአዘዝከ ፡ መድኃኒቶ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፡ ወዘአድኃንኮሙ ፡ ለአበዊነ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ መከራ ፡ አድኅነኒ ፡ ለገብርከ ፣ አውሳብዮስ ፡ ዘአድኃንኮ ፡ ለዳንኤል ፡ እምአፈ ፡ አንበሳ” ፡ ወለሶስና ፡ እምእደ ፡ ረበናት ፡ አድኅነኒ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ እምእደ ፡ ዓማዊ ፡ ወገፋኢ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልብየ ፡ ረዳኤ ፡ ዘእንበሌከ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘት ፡ ይሕንቁ ፡ ክሳዶ ፡ በሀብል ፡ ወይንድፍዎ ፡ በ፻ ወ፳ወ፳አሕፃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ገብሩ ፡
When he had been brought to her, she ordered him to be struck with iron bars, to have his hands and feet tied, and to be hung from a tree, and he was hung outside the city. (The queen) sent one of her servants to say to him: "Say yes to my mistress so that she may (have) you come down", but he did not listen to the voice of the adulteress. Then he began to pray, shedding his tears like the water of winter, and he said: "Lord God, God of 'Abrehäm (= Abraham), Yeshaq (= Isaac), and Yä'qob (= Jacob), it is you who ordered the salvation of Yä'qob and who saved our fathers from all temptation. Save me, (me) your servant 'Awsäbyos, (you) who saved Dän'ël (= Daniel) from the mouth of the lion and Sosennä (= Susanna) from the hand of the elders; save me, Lord, from the hand of the unjust and the violent, for I have no (other) help but you." Then the queen ordered his neck to be strangled with a rope and one hundred and sixty-eight arrows to be shot at him.


[p. 540] {#po.45.p540}
ቦቱ ፡ ዘንተ ፤ አዘዞሙ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ለመኳንንቲሁ” ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ወገብርኤል ፡ ወመልሐ ፡ አሕፃ ፡ እምአባሉ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ሙስና ።
When this was done to him, God gave an order to his officers Mikäa'el (= Michael) and Gabr'ël (= Gabriel); they tore the arrows from his limbs and he was unharmed, without damage.
ወሶበ ፡ አተወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ሖረ ፡'ርእዮ ፡ ስቁለ፤' ፡ ተሰአላ፤0፤ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ምንት ፡ አበሳሁ ፡ ለአውሳብዮስ ፡ ወለምንት ፡ ጌጋዩ ፡ ዘመጠውኪዮ፤"። ፡ ለስቅለት ፡ ወለሞት ። ወትቤሎ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ንግሥት ፡ አንሰ ፡ አኃፍር ፡ ከመ ፡ እንግርከ ፡ ወእፈርህ ፡ እምኔከ ። ወይቤላ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በሊ ፡ ንግርኒ ፡ ወኢትፍርሂ ፡ እምኔየ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ መሠርይ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ "እንዘ ፡ እነብር፤የ ፣ ባሕቲትየ ፡ ወሰአለኒ ፡ ግብረ ፤ ዘኢይደሉ ፡ ተናግሮቶ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ አበይክዎ ፡ አሕመመኒ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ያውርድዎ ፡ እምስቅለት ፡ ለአውሳብዮስ ፡ ወያምጽእዎ ፡ ኅቤሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ አብጽሕዎ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለአውሳብዮስ ፡ ለምንት፤" ፡ ሐለይከ ፡ እኩየ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዘኢይደሉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ብእሲትየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አውሳብዮስ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ይመስለከኑ ፡፤ እሙን ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ መፍትወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትናገርዎ ። ወሐተቶሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡፤ ለአግብርቲሁ ፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፡፣ እሙንኑ ፡ ዝነገር ፡ ዘእሰምዕ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ኦእግዚእነ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ንሕነሰ፤" ፡ ሶበ ፡ ቦእነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽርኅ ፡ ረከብናሃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትኬልሕ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ንቤላ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ኮንኪ ፡ ነገረተነ ፡ ከመዝ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተመልአ፤‹ ፡ መዓተ ፡ ወቀ፡ጥዓ፤ ፡ ወጥህረ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ወአ ዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፣፡ ወይግምዱ ፡ መለያልያቲሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ላህም ፡ ወይደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቀሱት ፡ ወይሕትሙ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ በፒሳ ፡ ወያብስልዎ ፡ በእሳት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ወገብርኤል ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ መላእክት ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወአውፅኡ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፤ እምውስተ ፡ ቀሱት ፡ ወአንሥእዎ ፡ 'ወረሰይዎ ፡፤ ሕያወ!" ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ እምንዋመ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ረከብዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወነገርዎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ሕያወ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ያምጽእዎ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ወደዩ ፡ ፪ጋጋተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አባሉ ፡ ፳ውስተ ፡ክሳዱ ፡ ወጳውስተ ፡ገቦሁ ፡ ወ8 ውስተ ፡ሐቓሁ፣0፤ ወ፳8ውስተ ፡ አብራኪሁ ። ወአሠሩ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ ፡ ወወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ። ወመጽኡ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ወገብርኤል ፡ ወአቅሞረሩ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ወመሠጦ ፡ ኡራኤል፤ መልአክ ፡ ወአዕረጎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ ዓመተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ዓዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰብክ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ በእደ ዊሁ ፤ ፳፻፻ወዛጻ፻አረሚ ። ወወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጸትውልድ ፡ በዘጸውዓ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ወለዘገብረ ፡፤ ተዝካሮ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀሮ ፡ በጸሎቱ፤!' ፡
When the king returned from where he had gone, he saw him hanging. He questioned her (= the queen) and said: "What is the fault of 'Awsäbyos and what (is) his crime, that you have delivered him to crucifixion and death?" His wife the queen said to him: "I am ashamed to tell you (it) and I am afraid of you." The king said to her: "Speak, tell me, and do not be afraid of me." She said to him: "This magician man entered to me while I was alone and he asked me for a thing that it is not fitting to say. As I refused (it) to him, he hurt me." The king ordered 'Awsäbyos to be taken down and brought to him. When 'Awsäbyos had been brought to him, the king said to him: "Why did you meditate against my wife an evil thing that is not fitting?" 'Awsäbyos said to the king: "My lord, does this story seem truthful to you? It is not necessary to speak of it." The king questioned his servants and said to them: "Is this story that I hear truthful?" They said to him: "Our lord the king, for us, when we entered the room, we found her (= the queen) crying. When we said to her: 'What is the matter with you?', she spoke to us thus." When the king heard this, he was filled with anger and fury, roared like a lion, and ordered his (= 'Awsäbyos') head to be cut off with the sword, his limbs to be cut up like the flesh of an ox, to be put in a pot, to have the opening of it closed with pitch, and to be boiled in the fire. Then the archangels Mikä'ël and Gabr'ël came from heaven, took his body out of the pot, raised him up, and brought him back to life. After he had risen from the sleep of death, (people) met him walking in the city and they told the king that he was alive. (The latter) ordered him to be brought; immediately four circles (of iron) were placed around his body: one around his neck, the second around his flanks, the third around his loins, and the fourth around his knees. His hands and feet were tied and he was thrown into the fire. Mikä'ël and Gabr'ël came and cooled the fire. The angel 'Ura'el (= Uriel) took him and made him ascend to heaven; he remained (there) for fourteen years. Then he returned to earth, remained for fourteen years preaching the faith, and eighty-five thousand pagans believed through his mediation. God gave him a pact until the seventh generation for whoever would invoke his name and for whoever would make his commemoration. May God, by his prayer, have pity on our king 'lyäsu forever and ever.]
ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካፅበ ፣ ልደቱ ፡ ለፊቅጦር ፡፤ ሰማዕት!!! ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also (there is) the birth of Figtor (= Victor) the martyr. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.


[p. 542] {#po.45.p542}

### READING FOR THE 14TH OF YAKKATIT
አመ ፡ ፲፪ለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፤ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘእለእስክንድርያ ፡ ዘይሰመይ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ልቀልቀ- ። በረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳዊሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፤ መምህሮሙ ፡ ለርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'ኮነ ፡ ዘመዱ ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ሮም፤ ። ወኮነ ፡ አበ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሳዊሮስ ። ወኮነ! ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አበው ፡ ፪፻ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ኤፌሶን ። ወር እየ ፡ ራዕየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዘይትወለድ ፡ ለወልድከ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ያቀም ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ርትዕተ ፡ ወይሰመይ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ሳዊሮስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ስምከ ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡፤ ውእቱ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ወለዶ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወሰመዮ ፣ ሳዊሮስ ፤ ወተምህረ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ዘአፍአ ። 'ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ ተምህረ" ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ወሕገ፤" ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወእንዘ፤! ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ እምሀገሩ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ብእሲ፣ ጻድቅ ፡ ዘይነብር፤" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዖማዕት ፡ ሙቁሕ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ 'ወተፈሥሐ ፤ ቦቱ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፤ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ምጽአትከ ፡ ኦሳዊሮስ ፡ መምህሮሙ ፡ ለርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። 'ወአንከረ ፡ ሳዊሮስ፤ ሶበ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ኢያአምሮ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ተናገረ ፡ በዘ ፡ ይከውን ፡ እምኔሁ፤ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይኩን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በገቢረ፤ ፡ ትሩፋት፤' ፣ ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ 'ወዝክሩ ፡ ሠናይ!" ። ወመንኩሰ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ሮማኖስ ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ?" ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወተጸምደ ፡ "ውስተ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ" ፡ ፈድፋደ፡፤። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኢትክል ፡ሀገር ፡ ተከብቶ፣እንተ፣ ተሐንጸት ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ደብር ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. [On this day there is the commemoration of the holy father Abba Qêrellos (= Cyril), patriarch of Alexandria, who is called Walda Lequeleque (= Ibn Laglag). May his blessing be with us forever and ever.] On this day died the holy father Abba Säwiros (= Severus), patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch), doctor of the (believers) in the right faith. The family of this saint was from the land of Rom (= Rome); the father of his father, whose name (was) Säwiros, had been a bishop. He had come with the two hundred father bishops who had gathered in the city of 'Éféson (= Ephesus). He saw a vision: as someone who was saying to him: "The son who will be born to your son will maintain the right faith straight and his name will be Säwiros like your name." When the bishop died, his son begot this saint and called him Säwiros. (The latter) learned all profane wisdom and, after that, learned the wisdom and the law of the Church. While he was leaving the city, behold (there was) a just man who dwelt in a cell as a recluse outside the city; he rejoiced to (see) him (= Säwiros) and said to him: "Welcome, Säwiros, doctor of the (believers) of the right faith and patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä!" Säwiros was astonished when he called him by his name - while he did not know him at all - and at the way he said what would happen to him before it happened. Then this saint grew in the practice of virtues; his fame and his beautiful reputation spread. He became a monk in the monastery of the saint Romänos (= Romanus), led a great (spiritual) combat, and devoted himself much to the practice of justice. His fame spread as the holy Gospel says: The city that is built on a mountain cannot be hidden
ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ተሰናአዉ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ማእምራን፤፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚምዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳዊሮስ ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡ ዘእን በለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፤ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። ወበርሀት ፣ በሚ መቱ ፡ ቤተክርስቲያን ፡'ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ዓለም ፡ 'እስመ ፡ ቃላቲሁ ፡ኮኑ፣ ይበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዓላዊያን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ይበትክ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰይፍ ፡ በሊሕ ፡ ዘክልኤ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ወኢነበረ ፡ በሚመቱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወነግሠ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከሓዲ ፤ ዘየአምን" ፣ በማኅበረ፤ ከልቄዶን ፣ ዘስሙ ፣ ዮስጥያኖስ ። ወኮነት ፡
When the patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä died, the learned bishops agreed to appoint this saint Abba Säwiros. They took him against his will and consecrated him patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä. During his patriarchate, the Church shone in all the ends of the universe, for his words reached the impious in all the land, and his word struck among them like a sharp double-edged sword. He did not remain in his office for long (before) the emperor died. Another emperor reigned in his place, a renegade whose name (was) Yosteyänos (= Justinian) who had faith in the council of Kalgëdon (= Chalcedon). The empress whose name (was) Tewodorä (= Theodora) was of the right faith. The emperor (wanted)


[p. 544] {#po.45.p544}
ንግሥት ፡፤ ርትዕተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ዘስማ ፡ ቴዎዶራ0፥፤ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ያፈርሆ ፡ "ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ። ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባእ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሃይማኖቱ ፡፤ ርኩስት ፡፤ ወኢተአዘዘ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተቁጥዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወመሰሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይፈርህ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወይት ኤዘዝ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ኢሰምዓ ፡ ትእዛዞ ፣ ወኢፈርሀ ፤ እምቀ፡ጥዓሁቶ፤ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትሎ ፡ በኅቡእ ። ወአእመረት ፡ ንግሥት፣፣ ጻድቅት ፡ ቴዎዶራ። ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወአምከረቶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጉየይ ፡ እምገጸ ፤ ንጉሥ ። ወወፅአ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኢፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርፍ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡
to frighten this saint so that he would enter into his impure faith, but he did not obey him. After that, (the emperor) became very angry with him and it seemed to him that the (saint) was (then) afraid of him and would obey him. But the saint did not obey his order and was not afraid of his anger. The emperor wanted to kill him in secret. The just empress Téwodorä knew this and advised this saint to flee far from the face of the emperor. He left in secret, for God did not want this saint to die at that moment, but he kept him for the profit of many.
ቅዱስ ፡ "በውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ?ቶ ፡ አላ ፡ ዓቀቦ ፡ በእንተ ፣ በቆፉዔት ፡ ለብዙኃን ። ወኮነ ፡ የዐውድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ወአድባራት ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ መነ ኮስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ያጸንዖሙ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሰካ፥" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ባዕል ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ደርታዎስ፥፤ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይገብር። ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፤ ወመንክራተ ፡ ዓበይተ ፡ 'ወኮነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይምህሮሙ+ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፡ ወያጸንዖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ሰካ ። ወፈለሰቶ፥ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ እምድኅረዝ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ዝጋግ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀ ገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወውእቱ ፡ እምኖልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ዛፃ ። ዝንቱ፣ ቅዱስ ፤ ተሠይመ ፡ በ፲ ወ፪ዓመተ ፤ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለንጉሠ፥ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ እልም ፅዝ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር፥" ፡ 'ወዘይትኤዘዝ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔርቶ፥ ፡ እጉሁ ፡ ለመኩንን ፡ ወልደ ፡'ሀሮን ፡ ረሲድ። ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ተሐደሱ ፡ አድባራተ፤፣ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወተመይጡ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ መነኮሳት ። ወኮነ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ኔሮህ ። መጽአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አባ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ጳጳሳት ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወይባርክ ፡ ውስቴቱ ። ወሖረ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ 'ወኮነ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ወልዱ ፡ ሐመ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወነሥኦ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተሣሀ ለኒ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልብየ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ሞተ፣ እስእለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትስአሎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንቲአየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ በከመ ፡ አሚኖትከ ፡ ይከውነከ ። ወነሥኦ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወሐቀፎ ፡ ውስተ፣ ሕጽኑ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወዓተበ" ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ወተወክፈ ፡ 'እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ወገብአ ፡ ነፍሱ ፣ ውስቴቱ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወተንሥአ፤ ፡ እሞት“ ። ወመጠዎ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ንሣእ ፡ ወልደከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢሞተ፣ አላ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ንውመ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ላዕ
He traveled through the cities and the monasteries - he (was dressed) in the likeness of a monk. He strengthened the believers and dwelt in the city of Sakä (= Sakha) with a rich Christian whose name (was) Dartäwos (= Dorotheus). He performed many miracles and great wonders. He taught the believers continually and strengthened them in the right faith, (and this) until he died in the city of Sakä. After that, his body passed into the monastery of Zegäg (= Zougag). May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. On this day also died the holy father Abba Yä'qob (= Jacob), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarch fathers, he is the fiftieth. This saint was consecrated in the seventeenth year of the reign of the king of Egypt whose name (was) 'Elme'ez in God (= Al-Mu'izz) - T (which means) "he who obeys God" -, the brother of the governor son of Haron Rasid (= Haroun Al-Rashid). In the time of this father, the monasteries of the desert of 'Asgêtes (= Scetis) were restored and the monks returned there. In his time there was a man whose name (was) Magäres (= Macarius) from the city of Nëroh. He came to this Abba Yä'qob the patriarch and asked him to come to his house and to give a blessing there; (Yä'qob) went with him. The man had a son; he had fallen ill and had died at that moment. His father took him, brought him to Abba Yä'qob the patriarch, begged him, and said to him: "Have pity on me, my father, for I have no other son than this one and behold he is dead. I pray you to pray to God for me." Abba Yä'qob said to him: "Do not be afraid, it will happen to you according to your faith." He took him (= the child) from him, pressed him to his chest, prayed over him, made the sign of the cross over him, and implored God for him. God accepted his prayer; the soul returned into the child, he opened his eyes, and rose from the dead. (Yä'qob) returned him to his father and said to him: "Take your son, he was not dead but asleep." When the father saw this miracle, a


[p. 546] {#po.45.p546}
ሌሁ ፡ ፍርሃት ፤ ዓቢይ ። ወወሀበ ፣ መንፈቀ ፡፣ ንዋዩ ፡ ለምጽዋት ፡ ወፈነወ"" ፡ ወአሕነጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ" ፡ "ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም”" ፡ ከመ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ጸወነ ፤ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ይበጽሑ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ እምነ ፡ ርቱዓነ ፤ ሃይማኖት ።
great fear seized him. He gave half of his goods in alms and sent to have a church built in the city of Jerusalem so that it might be a refuge for all those among the people of the right faith who would come there.
ወዲዮናስዮስሰ" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ በጽሐ ፡፤ ኀበ ፡ ምስር ፡ ወተጋብአ"፡ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አብ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፡፣ ያዕቆብ" ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔሩ ። ወነበረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ዲበ መንበረ ሚመቱ ፡ ፲ዓመተ ፡ ወቿ አውራኃ ፡ ይመዓልተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አፅረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ቄርሎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ዘዕውቅ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዳዊት” ፡ ወልደ ፡ ልቀልቀ ። ወውእቱ ፡ እምፕልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ድወ፳ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ 'ኮነ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ ፍዩም ፡ ወተሠይመ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ ባቲ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ እምህየ ፡ ወበ ጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘአፍአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ምስር ፡ በዓዘቅተ ፡ ኢትዮጵያዊያን?" ፡ ወነበረ ፤ ውስቴቱ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ ተጋድሎ”፤ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ በአእምሮ ፡ በቅድስና ፡ ወአኀዝዎ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ፣ ወዓቀበ ፡ መርዓቶ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ተዓቅቦ” ። ሠርዐ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ዘይደልዎሙ”" ፡ ለካህናት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይትለአኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጸሎታት ፡ ወቅዳስያት ። ወነበረ ፣ በሚመቱ ፡ ፄዓመተ ፡ ወ(7)አውራኃጾ፤ ፡ ወ፳ወጂመዓልተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረ ከቱ ፡ ወትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለዳርዮስ ፡ ወለጳውሎስ ፣፤ ወአኩራዲስ ፡ ወሰንድናስ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
Diyonäsyos (= Dionysius), the patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch), came to Mesr (= Cairo) and met with the father patriarch Abba Yä'qob. He remained with him for some time and returned to his country. This father (= Abba Yä'qob) remained on his patriarchal throne for ten years, eight months (and) three days and died in peace. May God have pity on us by his prayer, amen. On this day also died the holy father Abba Qérellos (= Cyril III), patriarch of the city of Alexandria, who (is) known (by the name of) priest Dävwit (= David) Walda Lequeleque (= Ibn Laglaq); among the patriarch fathers, he is the seventy-fifth. This father was from the people of the city of Feyum (= Fayoum) and had been ordained a priest there. After that, he left it and came to the monastery of the saint Figtor (= Victor) the martyr, which (is) outside the city of Mesr (= Cairo) next to the well of the Ethiopians; he remained there for many years leading a great (spiritual) combat. His reputation for science (and) holiness spread, he was seized against his will, and he was appointed patriarch of the city of Alexandria. He guarded his flock with a beautiful guard. He established a rule that was appropriate for the priests when they perform their ministry in the church at the time of prayers and consecrations. He remained in his office for seven years, ... months and twenty-seven days and died in peace. May his prayer, his blessing, and his intercession be with us forever and ever, amen. [On this day also (there is) the commemoration of Däryos (= Darius), Päwlos (= Paul), 'Akuerädis, and Sandenäs. May their blessing be with their dear Za-Manfas Qeddus forever and ever.]

### READING FOR THE 15TH OF YAKKATIT
አመ ፡ ፲፳ለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ ዘእም ፲ወ፪ደቂቀ፤ ፡ ነቢያት። ዝንቱ ጸድቅ ፡ ኮነ ፤ እምነገደ ፡ ሌዊ ፡ 'ወኮነ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ በራኪዩ"፣ ወወለድዎ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ገላኣድ ፡ ወፄወውዎ” ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ከሌዳዊያንጾ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በህየ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ በትንቢት ፡ መለኮታዊ ፡ ዘወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወተናገረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በልሳኑ ፡ ቃላተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ማእምንተ ፡ ወበቋዒተ ። ወተነበየ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the just prophet Zakäryäs (= Zechariah) who (is part) of the twelve minor prophets. This just prophet was of the tribe of Lëwi (= Levi). The name of his father was Baräkiyu (= Berechiah). (His parents) begot him in the land of Galä'äd (= Gilead). He was taken into captivity in the land of the Chaldeans. While he was there, he prophesied


[p. 548] {#po.45.p548}
ለኢዮሴዴቅ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ትወልድ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ወይከውን፤" ፡ ካህነ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወወለዶ፤፤ ፡ ለኢያሱ፤ ፡ ወሐነጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፄዋዌ ፣ ወተቀብረ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወተነበየ ፡ ለበስላልኤል፤" ፡ ወባረኮ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ 'ሀለወከ ፡ ትለድ፤ ፡ ወልደ ፡ "ወይሰመይ ፡ ስሙ፤ ፡ ዘሩባቤል ። ወተወልደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዘሩባቤልሃ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፤ ምስለ ፤ ኢያሱ፤ ። ወተነበየ ፡ ለኩርስ፤" ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ትእምርታተ ፡ መዊእ ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ በአቱ፤ጾ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፣ ውስተ ፣ ኢየሩሳ ሌም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹዓን ፡ ዲበ ፡ አድግ ፡ ወዲበ ፡ ዕዋለ ፡ አድግ ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ቋብሩር ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ ይሁዳ!" ፡ በእንተ ፡ አግብኦቱ ፡ ለመድኃኒነ ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ዝርወቶሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ በሌሊተ ፡ ስቅለት ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጽልመት ፡ዘኮነት ፤ በዕለተ ፡ ስቅለት ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዘተኃብአ ። ወተነበየ"" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፣ በስብሐቲሁ፤፤ ወበእንተ ፣ ሐዘኖሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እለ ፡ ኢየአ ምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ወይኔጽርዎ ፡ ለዘረገዝዎ። ፣፡ ወይላሕዉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ ወይበዝኅ" ፡ ላሕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ መዋዕለ ትንቢቱ ፡ አፅረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወተቀብረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መቃብሪሆሙ ፡ ለነቢያት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
of a divine prophecy that descended upon him: God spoke through his tongue many truthful and profitable words. He prophesied for 'lyosëdëq (= Jehozadak) and said to him: "You will beget a son and he will become a priest of God in Jerusalem." And ('lyosëdëq) begot 'lyäsu (= Joshua), and (the latter) built the temple in Jerusalem after the Captivity and was buried there. He prophesied for Basläl'ël (= Salathiel), blessed him, and said to him: "You will beget a son and he will be called by the name of Zarubäbel (= Zerubbabel)." To him (= to Salathiel) was born Zarubäbel - it is he who built the Temple in Jerusalem with 'lyäsu. He prophesied for Kures (= Cyrus), the king of Färs (= Persia), and revealed to him the signs of victory. He prophesied about the entry into Jerusalem of our Lord mounted on a donkey, on a colt. He prophesied about the thirty (pieces of) silver that Yehudä (= Judas) received for having delivered our Savior. He prophesied about the dispersion of the apostles on the night of the crucifixion. He prophesied about the darkness that occurred on the day of the crucifixion and about the light that hid itself. He prophesied about the coming of our Lord in his glory and about the sorrow of the children of Israel who did not believe in him; he said thus: "They will see the one whom they have pierced and they will lament over the only son; great will be the lamentation in Jerusalem." When he had completed the days of his prophecy, he died in peace and was buried in the tomb of the prophets.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘቅዱሳን ፡ ጣሐራ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ዘሀገረ ፡ ስብስጥያ ፡'ወይእቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ቀዳሚት ፡ ዘተሐንጸት ፡ በስሞሙ ። ቀደሳ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ወደረሰ ፡፣ ድርሳነ ፣ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ወውዳሴ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
May his prayer be with us, amen. On this day also (there is) the commemoration of the consecration of the church of the forty martyr soldiers of the city of Sebestyä (= Sebaste); it is the first church that was built in their name. The great saint Bäselyos (= Basil) consecrated it on that day and composed a homily and many glorifications about them (= the martyrs), and he celebrated for them a great feast on the same (day) as today. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ጽሙድ ፡ ወመስተጋድል ፡ አባ፣ በፍኖትዮስ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ መንኩሰ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወተጸምደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወጸሎት ፡ ወሰጊድ ፡ ወትጋህ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ምግባራተ" ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፣ ልዑል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጤ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ዘርሑቅ ፡ እምአድባራተ ፡ መነኮ ሳት ፤ ወየሐውጾሙ ፡ ለገዳማዊያን ፡ ቅዱሳን ፤ ወይጽሐፍ፤" ፡ ገድሎሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ በቀዔት ፡ ለእለ ፤ ያነብብዎሙ ። ወአድነነ፤ ፡ ለትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወቦአ ፤
On this day also died the holy, devout, and fighting (spiritual) father Abba Bafnotyos (= Paphnutius). This saint became a monk from his childhood, he led a great (spiritual) combat, and followed very assiduously the way of the monastic state in fasting, prayer, adoration, and vigil. When he had brought to completion this beautiful conduct, God the Most High ordered him to penetrate into the interior of the desert which (is) far from the monasteries of the monks, to visit the holy hermits, and to write their (spiritual) combat for the profit of those who would read it. He submitted to the order of God, penetrated into the interior of the deserts


[p. 550] {#po.45.p550}
ውስተ ፤ ውሳጤ ፡ ገዳማተ ፡ በድው ፡ ወዓየለ ፡ በማእከሌሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘየዓ ይል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ። ወረከቦሙ ፡ ለገዳማዊያን ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ወጠየቀ ፡ እምኔሆሙ፡ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ 'ወጸሐፈ ፣ ገድሎሙቶ፥ ፡ ወእምኖልቆሙ፥ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ገዳማዊ፤ ፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ 'አባ ፡ ነፍር ።
desolate, and wandered in the midst of them like a man who wanders in a city. He found many hermits, inquired of them about their history, and wrote their (spiritual) combat. Of their number (was) Timotëwos (= Timothy) the hermit and the saint Abba Nafer (= Onuphrius).
ወበጽሖ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በፍኖትዮስ" ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ እምረኃብ ፡ 'በጥ ንተ ፤ በዊኦቱ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ውሳጤ ፡ ገዳም ፡፣ ወአስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፣፤ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወአጽንዖ ። ወነበረ ፡ 8መዓልተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሲሳይ ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ርኅበ ፡ ካዕፅበ፤ ፡ ወቀርበ ፡ ለመዊት ፡ ወአስተርአዮ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ "ወመዝመዘ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወአፉሁ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ፲ ወ፪መዓልተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሲሳይ ፡ ካዕበ። ወተናገረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፣ በእንተ ፡ አበው ፡ ገዳማዊያን ፡ መንክራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ እምንብረቶሙ ፤ ፳ዓመተ ፡ ወዘይፈደፍድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወኢርእዩ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ግሙራቶ ፣ መጠነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕል ። ወተናገረ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘበጽሖሙ ፡ ለገዳማዊያን ፤ ምንዳቤ ፣ በጥንተ ፣ በዊኦቶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወተቃርኖ ፡ ጠባይዓት ፤ ሎሙ ፡ ወፀቢኦቶሙ ፡ ለመናፍስትቶ" ፡ ርኩሳን ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ገረሩ ፡ ሎሙ ፣ ወተአዘዙ ፡ ሎሙ+ ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡፤ ታሕተ ፤ እገሪሆሙ፥0 ። ወከማሁ ፡፤ አራዊትኒ፤ ፡ ወአናብስትኒ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይትለአክዎሙ ፣ ወይትኤዘዙ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይትኤዘዝ ገብር ፡ ለእግዚኡ ። ወተናገረ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይትሜጠዉ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቀድሳት ፡ በዕለተ ፡፣ ቀዳሚት ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ወበዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያመጽእ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ ወደሞ ፡ ክቡረ ፤ ወይሁቦሙ፥ ፡ እምፍርያተ ፡ ገነት ። ወደለዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ከመ ፡
A great distress reached this saint Bafnotyos because of hunger at the beginning of his stay in the interior of the desert. An angel of God appeared to him and strengthened him. He remained four days without food; after that, he was hungry again and was near to dying. The angel of God appeared to him again and rubbed his body and his mouth, and he remained again seventeen days without food. This saint told about the hermit fathers many wonders regarding their stay in the desert of sixty years and more - they had not seen a man at all during all those days. He spoke of the torments that had reached the hermits at the beginning of their stay in the desert, (of) the struggle of (their) nature against them, and (of) the attacks of the impure spirits. After that, (the latter) had submitted to them, had obeyed them, and had prostrated themselves at their feet. Likewise, both the wild beasts and the lions served them and obeyed them as a servant obeys his master. He told about them that they received the holy Mysteries on Saturday and Sunday: an angel of God brought them the holy body and the glorious blood and gave them fruits of Paradise. This father was worthy to see and to hear prodigious actions. After that, he died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us forever and ever, amen.

### READING FOR THE 16TH OF YAKKATIT
ውእቱ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ወሰምዓ ፡ ምግባራተ ፡ መንክራተ ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፲፪ለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፤ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ጸድቅት ፡ ኤልሳቤጥ ፡ እሙ ፡ ለዮሐንስ መጥምቅ ። ዛቲ ፡ ጸድቅት፤ ፡ ኮነት ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወስማ ፡ ለእማ ፡ ሶፍያ ፡ ወስሙ ፡ ለአቡሃ ፡ ማጣት ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሌዊ" ። እስመ ፡ ሌዊ ፡ ወለደ ፡ ፻ አዋልደ ፡ ስማ፣ ለእንተ ፡ ትልህቅ ፣፤ ማርያም ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ እማ ፡ ለሰሎሜ ፡፣ እንተ ፡ ተቀበለታ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ በጊዜ ፤ ልደታ ፡ መንክር ። ወስማ ፡ ለዳግሚት ፡ ሶፍያ ፤ ወይእቲ ፡ እማ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፣፤ ኤልሳቤጥ ። ወስማ ፡ ለእንተ ፡ ትንእስ ፡ ሐና ፣ ወይእቲ ፡ እንተ ፡ ወለደታ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡፤ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ። ወኮና ፣ ሰሎሜ ፡ ወኤልሳቤጥ ፡ ወእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ አዋልደ ፡ አሐት ። ወአውሰሳባ ፡ ዘካር ያስ ፤ ካህን ፡ ለዛቲ ፤ ጸድቅት ፡ ኤልሳቤጥ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይቤ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy (and) just 'Élsäbet (= Elizabeth), mother of Yohannes (= John) the Baptist. This just woman was from the city of Jerusalem. The name of her mother (was) Sofyä (= Sophia) and the name of her father, Mätat (= Matthat) son of Lëwi (= Levi). Lëwi begot three daughters; the name of the eldest (is) Märyäm (= Mary) - it is the mother of Salomë (= Salome) who welcomed our Lady Mary at the time of her wonderful birth. The name of the second (is) Sofyä (= Sophia) - it is the mother of this saint 'Élsabët. The name of the youngest (is) Hannä (= Anne) - it is she who gave birth to our Lady the holy Virgin Mary. Salomë, 'Élsäbët, and our Lady Mary were maternal cousins. Zakäryäs (= Zechariah) the priest married


[p. 552] {#po.45.p552}
በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ 'ጸድቃነ ፡ ወንጹሓነ ፣ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ወየሐ ውሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ 'ሕገ ፡ እግዚአብሔር” ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ነውር ። ወኮነት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ጻድቅት" ፣ መካን ፣ ወበእንተ ፤ አስተብቀ'ዖተ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወምታ ፡፣ ወስእለ ቶሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅቡረ ፤ ወወሀቦሙ!" ፡ እግዚአብሔር!! ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ መጥምቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ጽድቆሙ ፡ ወፍቅሮሙ ፡ 'ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዕ በየ ፡ አሚኖቶሙ፡ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዘኢወሀቦሙ ፡ ስእለቶሙ፤ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወኢናፈቁ ፡ ወኢተከዙ ፡አላ ፡ጸንዑ ፡ውስተ ፡ስእለቶሙ ፡'ከመ ፡ ሶበ፤፣ልህቁ፡፣፡በመዋዕሊሆሙ፣ ወእንዘ እሙንቱ ፡ መካናት፤፤ ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ፤ ፡ ይወልዱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ይጠይቁ፤ ፣ ኀይለ ፡ መለኮታዊተ፤ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዘትፀንስ ፡ እግዝእት ፡ ቅድ ስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ለቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ዘለዓለም ፡ ሀሎ፤ ። ወይሰብክ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ እሙ ፡ ወይሰግድ" ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤ ፡ እሙ፤፤ ፡ እስመ ፡ እጓል ፡ አንፈርዓፀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሣ" ፡ በፍሥሐ ፡ ወበሐዔት ፣
This righteous Elizabeth; the holy Gospel says of them both that they were righteous and pure and followed all the law of God without blemish. This righteous woman was barren; because of her continuous supplications—hers and her husband's—and their joint requests to God, God gave them the holy Yohannes the Baptist to reveal their righteousness, their love of God, and the greatness of their faith in Him, (He) who did not grant their request quickly—(despite this), they neither doubted nor grieved, but persisted in their request—(and) so that, when they were of advanced age, being barren and, after that, (having) begotten a son, the divine power might be known with certainty; and also so that the time would come when (our) Lady the holy Virgin Mary would conceive the Word of God who is forever. Yohannes, being in his mother's womb, would announce Him and adore Him as his mother says: the child, in her womb, had leaped for joy and exultation when (our) Lady Mary had entered to her.
ሶበ፤፤ ፣ ቦአት ፤ እግዝእት ፡ ማርያም ፡ ኀቤሃ ። ወሶበ ፤ ልህቀት ፡ ወልህቀ ፡ ምታ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መልአከ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ ወዜነዎ" ፡ በእንተ ፣ ዮሐንስ ፤ ወልዱ፤ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ እምኔሁ፤" ። ወፀንሰት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወወለደቶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወሰባኪ ፡ ወመጥዎቅ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ልባ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ 'ወተአተተ ፡ ትእይርት ፡ እምላ ዕለ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ዘመዳ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ርእየቶ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወመድኃኒነ፥ ፡ ወተአመነት ፡ በመለኮቱ ፡ ወተፈሥሐት ፡ በአድኅኖቶሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ አምኑ" ፡ ቦቱ፤ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሐይወት ፤ በንጽሕና" ፡ ወበጽድቅቶ" ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወአዕረፈት ፣፡ በሰላም፤ ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
When she had grown old and her husband Zakäryäs had grown old, God sent the angel Gabr'ël (= Gabriel), and (he) announced to him (= to Zakäryäs) (the birth of) Yohannes his son and taught him what would become of him. This saint conceived and gave birth to the holy, the prophet, the announcer, the baptizer Yohannes; her heart rejoiced at this and the reproach was removed from the sons of her race. Then she saw our Lord and Savior, believed in his divinity, and rejoiced in the salvation of those who would believe in him. After that, she lived in purity and righteousness as she had been before and died in peace. May her prayer and her blessing be with us, amen.
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ በዐለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ለእግዝእ ትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ በዘነሥአት ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ እምወልዳ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፤ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ተዝካራ ፡ ወለዘይፄውዕ ፡ ስማ ፡ ወለዘይሁብ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ እስከ ፡ ማየ ፡ ቁሪር ። ወዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ማርያም ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዓርገ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፤ ነሚኦ፥ ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በየማነ ፡ አቡሁ ፡፤ ፈጺሞ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ትስብእት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ባሕቲታ ፡ ወሠሊጦ ፤ ሕማማተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ በሥምረቱ ፡ ወበፈቃዱ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ በእንተ ፣ መድኃኒትነ ፡ ወኃደጋ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ዘያፈቅሮ ፡ በከመ ፡ አማኅፀና ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ነዋ ፡ ወልድኪ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለውእቱኒ ፡ ረድእ ፤ ነያ ፡ እምከ ። ወእምአሜሃ ፡ ነበረት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተሐውር ፡ ኀበ ፡ መቃብረ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ጎልጎታ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትጸሊ ፡ በህየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእይዋ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ መልኡ ፡
[On this day also the whole family of Christians celebrates a feast for our Lady Mary, Mother of God, because on it she received from her Son our Savior Jesus Christ a covenant of mercy for whoever would make her commemoration, for whoever would invoke her name, and for whoever would give alms to the poor, including (simply) fresh water. (As for) this saint Mary, after her Son had ascended to heaven having taken her (= Mary's) flesh completely and had sat at the right hand of his Father, having fulfilled all the law of human nature with the sole exception of sin and having endured the sufferings of the cross of his own free will and by his volition—(a thing) which he did for our salvation—(as for Mary then), he left her, (her) his mother Mary, in the house of Yohannes, his disciple whom he loved, just as he himself had entrusted her (to him) saying: "Behold your son,"—and he said to the disciple: "Behold your mother." From then on she remained going to the tomb of her Son, that is to say Golgotä (= Golgotha), to pray there. When the Jews saw her, they were


[p. 554] {#po.45.p554}
መዓተ ፡ ወቅንዓተ ፡ ወፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውግርዋ ፡ ወሠወራ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ እምአዕይንቲሆሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተማከሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚሙ ፡ ዓቀብተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትብጻሕ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ትጸሊቶ፤ ፡ በህየ ፡ ወይእቲሰ ፡ ኢታጸርዕ ፡ ሐዊረ ፡፤ ለለዕለቱ ። ወዓቀብትኒ ፡ ኢይሬእይዋ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሠወራ ፡ መንጦላዕተ ፡
filled with anger and fury and wanted to stone her, but God hid her from their eyes. Then they decided to establish guards near the tomb so that she would not return to pray there. But she did not cease going (there) every day. The guards did not see her, for the mantle of the glory of her Son hid her.
ስብሐቲሁ ፡ ለወልዳ ። ወበኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትለአክዋ ፡ ወወልዳ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ ይሔውጻ ፡ ዘልፈ ፡ ወይፌጽም፡ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ ዘኃሠሠት ፡ ቦአመ ፡ ያዓርግዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወያርእይዋ ፡ መካነ ፡ ዕረፍቶሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ። ወተቀበልዋ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ አበው ፡ እለ ፡ አፅረፉ ፡ እምአዳም ፡ እስከኔሁ ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ላቲ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘፈጠረ ፡ ለነ ፡ ኪያኪ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ እምሥጋኃነ ፡ ወዓፅመ ፣ እምአፅምነ ፤ ወብኪ ፡ ረከብነ ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ ወኮንክነ፥ ፡ መርሶ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ እሙስና ፡ በተሠግዎቱ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ አምላክ ፡ እምኔኪ ። ወእምህየ ፡ ወሰድዋ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወአብጽሕዋ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ወኀበ ፡ መንበሩ ። ወተቀልዑ ፡ መንጠዋልዕ ፡ ዘነደ ፡ እሳት ፡ ይምነ ፡ ወጽግመ ፡ ወአኃዛ ፡ እዴሃ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወሰዓመ ፡ አፉሃ ፡ ወይ ቤላ ፡ መጻእኪኑ ፡ ኦወላዲትየ ። ወአዕረጋ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ አንበራ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወዜነዋ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትፍሥሕተ ፤ ወኃተ ፡ ዘዓይን ፤ ኢርእየ ፡ ወእዝን ፡ ኢሰ ምዓ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘኢተሐለየ ፡ ዘአስተዳለወ ፡ ላቲ ። ወእምታሕተ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ። ፡ ርእየቶ ፡ ለዳዊት ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ምስለ ፡፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ ማኅ በረ ፤ ነቢያት ፡ ወመላእክት ፡ ወነፍሳተ ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ከቢቦሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ አረፍት ፡ ይዜምር ፤ በመሰንቆ ፡ ወይብል ፡ ስምዒ ፡ ወለትየ ፡ ወርእዩ ፡ ወአጽምዒ ፡ እዝነኪ ፡ ርስዒ ፤ ሕዝበኪ ፡ ወቤተ ፡ አቡኪ ፡ እስመ ፡ ፈተወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ስነኪ ፡ እስመ" ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚእኪ ። ወእምህየ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ወሰድዋ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርእይዋ ፡ መካነ ፡ ኩነኔ ፤ ወአብጽሕዋ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ጸናፌ ፡ ጽልመት ፡ ዘድልው ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ ወለሠራ ዊቱ ፡ ወለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ በፍናዊሁ ፡ ወትቤ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ 'ቅድስት ፡ ወድንግልት ፡ በክልኤ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ መኑ ፡ እምዜነዎሙ ፡ ለው ሎዶ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይምጽኡ ፡ ዝየ ። ወይቤላ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ኢትፍርሂ ፡ ኦማርያም ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ለኪሂ ፡ ወለእለ ፡ እምድኅሬኪኒ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጾርዋ ፡ መላእክት ፣ ወአግብእዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካና ። ወእምአሜሃ ፡ ነበረት ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተኃዝን ፡ ጥቀ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኃጥኣን ።
At every moment, the angels came to her to serve her; her Son our Lord Jesus Christ visited her constantly and accomplished for her everything she asked. Sometimes (the angels) made her ascend to heaven and showed her the place of rest of the righteous, where 'Abrehäm (= Abraham), Yeshaq (= Isaac), and Yä'qob (= Jacob) are; all the souls of the fathers who have died from 'Adäm (= Adam) until now welcomed her and prostrated themselves before her saying: "Glory to God who created you for us, flesh of our flesh and bone of our bones! In you we have found salvation, you have been for us a haven of life far from corruption through the incarnation, from you, of the Son of God." From there the angels led her and brought her to her beloved Son and to his throne. The veils which (were of) flame of fire, to the right and to the left, were removed; our Lord took her by the hand, kissed her mouth, and said to her: "Have you come, my mother?" He made her ascend to the throne of glory, made her sit with him, and described to her all the joy and gladness that the eye has not seen, (that) the ear has not heard, which are not imagined by the heart of man (and) which he had prepared for her. Under the throne of his glory, she saw Däwit (= David) her father, the king of Israel—with all the assembly of the prophets, angels, and souls of the righteous (surrounding her) like a wall—singing with the harp and saying: "Hear, my daughter, see and incline your ear. Forget your people and your father's house, for the king has desired your beauty, for he is your master." The angels took her again from there to show her the place of punishment; they brought her to where the extremity of darkness is which (is) prepared for Satan, for his armies, and for those who follow his ways. Our Lady the holy and twice-virgin, Mary Mother of God, said: "Who will describe (this) to the children of men so that they do not come here?" An angel said to her: "Do not be afraid, Mary, God (is) for you and also for those who (will come) after you." Then the angels took her and brought her back to her home. From that moment, our Lady Mary remained grieving extremely for all sinners.
ወበከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ለየካቲት ፡ ቆመት ፡ መካነ ፡ ቀራንዮ ፡ ወሰአ ለት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ኣምሕለከ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ፡ አቡከ ፡ ወበክርስቶስ ፡ ስምከ ፡ ወጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ መንፈስከ ፡ ወበማኅፀንየ ፡ ዘጾረከ ፡ አውራኃ ፡ ወ፳ዕለተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ምድር ፡ ኢትክል ፡ ጸዊሮተከ ፤ ወመላእክትኒ ፡ ኢይትሀበሉ ፡ ወኢይክሉ" ፡ ቀሪቦተከ ፤ ኣምሕለከ ፤ ኦወልድየ ፡ በፀአትከ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡
On the same (day) as today, the sixteenth of Yakkätit, she stood at the place of the Skull and prayed to her Son saying: "I adjure you, my Son, by God your Father, by your name of Christ, by the Paraclete your Spirit, and by my womb which carried you nine months and five days while the earth cannot carry you and (while) the angels dare not nor can approach you; I adjure you, my son, by the fact that you came out of me without pain and (that) you were born without sorrow; I adjure

by my breasts which fed you and by my lips which kissed you; I adjure you by my arms which embraced you and by my feet which walked with you; I adjure you by the manger in which you were laid and by the swaddling clothes in which you were wrapped. My Son, I ask and beseech you to hear the voice of my request (and) to come to me and to accomplish everything that is in my heart." When our Lady the holy Virgin Mary, Mother of God, Mother of the light, had spoken thus, our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ descended to her—and with him, surrounding him, myriads of myriads of angels—and said to her: "What must I do for you, Mary my mother, and what do you want? (Tell me) so that I may do your will." Our Lady the holy twice-virgin, Mary Mother of God, answered her beloved Son and said to him: "My beloved, my Son, my Lord, my Savior, in you (is) my trust; through you I was made in the womb of my mother, in (her) breast you protected me; you (are in) my memory at every moment. Now hear my prayer and pay attention to the word of my mouth that I, your mother Mary, I, your servant, am going to say to you: as for the one who will make my commemoration and the one who will build a church in my name or the one who will clothe the one who is naked and the one who will visit the sick or the one who will feed the hungry and the one who will give drink to the thirsty or the one who will console the afflicted and gladden the saddened or the one who will have written my praise and will have called his son by my name and the one who will have sung a song during my feast, (as for all these), Lord, prepare for them a beautiful reward (coming) from you, which the eye has not seen, (which) the ear has not heard, and which has not been imagined by the heart of man. My Lord and my God, I pray and beseech you: deliver from Sheol whoever believes in me, remembering the hunger, the thirst, and all the trials that reached me with you." Our Lord Jesus Christ answered her and said to her: "Let it be as you have said to me, I will accomplish for you all your request. Did I not become man through you? I swear by myself that my promise will not be false regarding you." May her (= Mary's) prayer and her blessing be with her dear Za-Manfas Qeddus in the ages of ages.]

### READING OF THE 17 YAKKATIT
አመ ፡ ፲፪ለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሚናስ ፡ መነኮስ ፣ ወሰማዕት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ኮነ፣፤ እምብሔረ፤ ፤ አክሚም ፡ እምላዕላይ" ፡ ግብጽ ። ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡፣ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ወይትጌበሩ ፡ ምድረ ። ወፈተወ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይልበስ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወይመንን ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ዓለመ ፣ ወመንኩሰ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፳ደብር ፡ በሀገረ ፡ አክሚም ፡ ወነ በረ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸውም ፡ በበ ፪መዓልት ፡ ፪መዓልት ፡ ወይጸመድ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day there is the holy Minäs (= Menas), monk and martyr. This saint was from the region of 'Akmim (= Akhmim) in Upper Egypt. His parents were Christians and cultivated the land. This saint desired to put on the monastic garment and despise this world. He became a monk in a monastery in the city of 'Akmim. He remained for many days fasting every two days and practicing asceticism in his food and drink. After that, he went to the region of 'Esmunayn (= Ashmunein) and stayed in a monastery

"በሲሳዩ ፡ ወስታዩ ። ወእምድኅረዝ" ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ ኅበ ፡ 'ብሔረ ፡ እስሙነይን! ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፳ደብር ፤ ፲ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸመድ ፡ ወይትጋደል ፤ ወኢይወፅእ ፡ እምጥኅተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ። ወሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ተንበላት ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወሰምዓ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡እሙንቱ ፡ይብሉ ፡'እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር!! ፡ ወልደ ፡ እምጠባይዓቱ ፤ ወእመለኮቱ ፡ ዘሠረጸ፤ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ 'በዘህልው ፡ ዘለዓለም፤ ። ሐዘነ ፡ ፈድፋዶ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ነገር ፡ ወተባረከ ፡ እምነ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስሙነይን፤ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሰራዊተ ፡ ተንበላት ። ወይቤሎ ፣ አማንኑ ፤ ትብሉ ፡ አንትሙ ፡፥ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወልደ ፡ እምጠባይዓቱ ፡፤ ወመለኮቱ ። ወይቤሎ!" ፡ እወ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ናርሕቅ ፤ እምነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘንተ! ፡ ወኢንትአመን፤ ፡ ኪያሁ፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሚናስ ፡ ኢይደልወከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትትአመን" ፡ ቦቱ፤፤ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ለእመ ፡ኮነ ፡ ወልድ ፡ እምተራክቦ ፡ ወዘርእ ። ወእምአኮሰ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ 'ዘእምብርሃን ፡ ወአምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡፤ እምአምላክ ፣ ዘበአማን ። ወይቤሎ ፣ መኩንን ፡ ኦመነኮስ ፡ ዝንቱሰ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ዚአነ ፡ ክሕደት ፡ ውእቱ ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይ ቤሎ ፡ አእምር ፡ እስመ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘየአምን ፡ በወልድ" ፡ ቦሕይወተ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ወዘኢየአምን?፤' ፡ በወልድ ፡ ኢይሬኢ ፡ ሕይወተ”" ፡ አላ ፡ ይወርድ ፡ ላዕ ሌሁ ፡ መዓተ” ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ተምዓ ፡ መኩንን ፡ መዓተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ 'ይምትርዎ ፡ በአስይፍትቶ ፡ በበምታር፥፤ ፡ ወወገርዎ፥" ፡ ውስተ ፡
sixteen years, giving himself to asceticism and (spiritual) combat and without crossing the gate of the monastery. When the Muslims ruled over the land of Egypt and (he) heard about them that they said that God did not have a son of his nature (= tabäye') and of his divinity, (and) who proceeded from him while being eternal, he was very grieved because of this discourse. He received the blessing of the abbot of the monastery (who) prayed over him, he left and went to the city of 'Esmunayn. He stood before the commander of the Muslim soldiers and said to him: "Is it true that you say that God does not have a son of his nature and (of) his divinity?" (The commander) said to him: "Yes, we distance that from God and we do not believe it." The saint Minäs said to him: "It is not fitting that you do not believe it, unless (it) were a son (coming) from coitus and seed. It is not so; this one (is) light of light and true God of true God." The commander said to him: "Monk, this is an impiety in our law." The saint answered him and said to him: "Know that the holy Gospel says: He who believes in the Son has eternal life and he who does not believe in the Son will not see life, but the wrath of God will descend upon him." The commander entered into a great anger because of this; he ordered that he (= Minäs) be cut into pieces with swords, and he was thrown into the river. Believers took him, buried him beautifully, and made for him a commemoration on the same (day) as today. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

### READING OF THE 18 YAKKATIT
ባሕር ። ወነሥእዎ፥ መሃይምናን ፡ ወገነዝዎ ፡ 'ሠናየ ፡ ግንዘተ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፲ቿለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተአማኒ ፡ መለንዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማእምር ፡ የዋህ ፡ ወፍቁር ፡ በኀበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ቋ'መዓልተ፤ ፡ ወአውገዞሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ አርዮስ ፤ ወሰደዶሙ ፣ እምኩሎሙ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ወሰደዶ ፡ እምሚመቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ኮነ ፡ አርዮሳዌ ። ወተጋብኡ ፡ ሊሂቃነ ፡ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወኤኢስ ፡ ቆልሳት ፡ ወካህናት ፣ ወጸሐፉ ፡ ወለአኩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father, the confessor Malanyos (= Meletius), patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch). This saint was consecrated patriarch in the time of Quastantinos (= Constantius), son of Quastantinos (= Constantine) the Great. This saint was learned, gentle, and loved by everyone. When he was consecrated in the city of 'Ansokiyä, he remained there thirty days, excommunicated the followers of 'Aryos (= Arius), and drove them from all the churches of the city of 'Ansokiyä. When the emperor learned this, he dispatched and had him driven from his office, for (he) the emperor was an Arian. The great men of the city of 'Ansokiyä, the bishops, and the priests gathered, wrote, sent (their letter) about him to the emperor, and asked him to have him brought back to them. (The emperor) dispatched to the place where he had


[p. 560] {#po.45.p560}
ወሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚጦ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘሰደዶ ፡ ወሜጦ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡እስመ ፡ውእቱ፡ኃፈረ ፡እምኔሆሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኀበ፣ ሀገረ ፤ አንጾኪየ ፣ ኢኀደገ ፤ አውግዞቶሙ ፡ ወረጊሞቶሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ አርዮስ ፡፣ ወአውገዞሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ በቃሎሙ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ሥሕተቶሙ ፡ ወጽርፈቶሙ ፡ ገሃደ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይነብብ” ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ወያቤይን ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ እምባሕርይሁ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ዕሩይ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በመለኮቱ ፡ ወበሀልዎቱ! ፡ ወበግዝዓቱ፤! ። ወተመይጡ፤፡ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ወጸሐፉ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ 'እንዘ ፡ ያስተዋድይዎ!፤ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ወለጡ ፡ ልቦ ፡ ላዕሌሁ! ። ወፈነዎ፤ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ወሰደዶ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ዘይርሕቅ ፤ እምቀዳሚ፤‹ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ 'ውእቱ፡ ብሔር!?-፤ ፣ ዘሰደድዎ፤” ፡ ኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሕዝቡ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይፌኑ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ መልእክታቲሁ ፡፤ ወትምህርታቲሁ ፡ ወትርጓሜ ፡፤ መጻሕፍት ፡ መለኮታዊት ፡ ዘኅቡኣት ። ወኮኑ ፡ 'መልእክታቲሁ ፡ እምርሑቅ"፤፤ ፡ ያበጽሑ፡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መርዔቱ ፡ እለ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ወካህናት ፡ ማእምራን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰብክ ፡ በሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወበሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ዘለአበዊነ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ፻፻፲ ወ፳ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይረግሞ ፡ ለአርዮስ ፡ ወያወግዞሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ይተልውዎ። ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ስደት ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ወናሁ ፡ ወዶሶ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ አፈ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ በውዳስያት ፡ ዘደረሰ ፡ ሎቱ፤ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ውስቴታ?” ፡ ክብሮ፤ ፡ ወግርማሁ ፡ 'ወአጽንዓ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢይንእስ ፣ ወኢየኀጽጽ" ፣ እምነ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ 'በእንተ ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡
exiled him and had him brought back to them, for he had regard for them. When this saint had returned to the city of 'Ansokiyä, he did not cease to excommunicate and curse the Arians, and he excommunicated all those who believed in their word; he exposed openly their error and their blasphemy. He said to all and demonstrated that the Son (is) of the essence (= bährey) of the Father, equal to him in his divinity, in his being, and in his dominion. The followers of 'Aryos wrote again to the emperor accusing this saint, so much so that they changed his heart regarding him. (The emperor) sent him away again and exiled him a second time to a country that was further than the first. When he arrived in the country where he had been exiled, it was as if he were staying with his people, sending them his letters, his teachings, and the explanation of the obscure divine scriptures. His letters reached from afar his flock that (was) in the city of 'Ansokiyä—the learned bishops and priests—preaching the holy Trinity and the right faith of our holy fathers, the three hundred and eighteen. He cursed 'Aryos and excommunicated all those who followed him. He remained in exile for many years until he died in peace. Behold, the saint Yohannes Golden Mouth (= John Chrysostom) praised him on the day of his feast in a panegyric that he composed for him; he exposed in it his glory and his majesty and he assured that he was not less than the holy apostles nor inferior (to them) because of the misfortune and the reproach that had reached him because of the right faith. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ላዕሌሁ፤ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወኃሣር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፡ ጻድቅ ። በረከቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also (there is) the commemoration of the death of Yä'qob (= Jacob) the Just. May his blessing be with us in the ages of ages, amen.]

### READING OF THE 19 YAKKATIT
አመ ፡ ፲8ለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ፍልሰተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ፤ ፡ መርትያኖስ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ አቴና ፡እስከ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። ውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መከራ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ዘማዊት ፡ ወህየንተ ፡ ዘፈቀደት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትጊጦ ፡ ወታውድቆ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ውስተ ፣ መሥገርተ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ወሰሀባ ፤ ውእቱ፤ ፡ ወአብኣ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ንስሐ ፡ ወምንኩስና ። ወእ
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day (there is) the translation of the body of the saint Marteyänos (= Marcian), the monk from the city of 'Atênä (= Athens) to the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch). This (took place) thus: After there had happened to this saint a temptation with the prostitute and (that), instead of what she wanted, namely to seduce him and make him fall with her into the nets of sin, he drew her (to him) and brought her to repentance and to the monastic state, after that (then), he left her, went to Dasët (= Mesopotamia) and, from there, into many

cities. When he arrived at the city of 'Atënä, he stayed there for some time; his body was reached by a small illness and he died there, as it is written on the twenty-first of Genbot. When the saint Demêtros was named patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä in the time of Fäläryos (= Valerian) the impious emperor, this saint sent priests to the city of 'Atënä; they carried away the body of this saint Marteyänos and brought it to the city of 'Ansokiyä with great pomp and with many songs and canticles. This saint (= Demêtros) honored him, greeted him, had him placed in a sarcophagus in the church, and made for him a feast on the same (day) as today. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፳ለየካቲት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ብፁዕ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድ ርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምኖልቆሙ ፡ ለአብው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፳ወ፳፤ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አትናስዮስ" ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ እምነ ፡ዳርያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡'ወሕዝበ ፡ አርዮስ ፤ ከሐዲያን ። ወፈቀዱ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወጐየ ፤ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወተኃብአ ፡ ፪ዓመተ ። ወአንበሩ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሉቅዮስ ፡ ወረሰይዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አርዮሳዊ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ፪ዓመተ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፤ መሃይምናን ፡ ርቱዓነ ፣፤ ሃይማኖት ፣ ወተንሥኡ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አርዮሳዊያን ፡ ወሰደድዎ ፡ ለሉቅዮስ" ፡ አርዮሳዊ ፡ ከሓዲ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፣ መንበሩ ፤ ዓመተ ፡ በምንዳቤ ፤ ዓቢይ ፡ እንዘ ፡፤ ይትቃረኖሙ ፡ ለአርዮሳዊያን ፡ ወያወግዞሙ ፡ ወይሰድዶሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በሚመቱ” ፡ ፳ዓመተ ፡ አፅ ረፎ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምጻማ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ 'ወሖረ ፡፤ ውስተ ፣ ትፍሥሕት ፥
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the blessed father Pêtros (= Peter), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarch fathers, he is the twenty-first. This father was installed on the seat of Märqos (= Mark) the evangelist after the saint 'Atnäsyos (= Athanasius). Many misfortunes happened to him from Däryänos, the impious emperor, and the impious followers of 'Aryos (= Arius) wanted to kill him many times; he fled far from them and hid for two years. They installed in his place a man whose name (was) Luqyos (= Lucius) and instituted him patriarch; he was an Arian; he remained two years. After that, the believers in the right faith gathered, rose against the Arians, and drove out Luqyos, the impious Arian. This father Pêtros returned and remained on his seat for six years in great tribulations, opposing the Arians, excommunicating them, and driving them out. When he had spent eight years in his office, God made him rest from the pains of this world and he went into eternal joy. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

On this day also (there is) the commemoration of Bäselyos (= Basil), Tëwodosyos (= Theodosius), and Timotëwos (= Timothy), martyrs in the city of Alexandria. May their prayer and their blessing be with us forever, amen. [On this day also was martyred Filmon (= Philemon) the flutist. This Filmon the flutist played for 'Aryänos (= Arianus) the governor. One day, 'Aryänos the governor called 'Apelon (= Apollon) the reader and said to him: "Sacrifice to the gods."
