At that time there were two travelers (devoid) of goods but rich in faith. They went to Jerusalem to prostrate themselves and perform the holy fast. This (took place) after many years. When it was evening, they stayed in the deserted house of Herod the proud. The saint Yohannes appeared to one of them in (his) sleep, told him his name, indicated to him the place where his holy head (was), and ordered him to take it and carry it to his house. When (the man) awoke from his sleep, he told his companion what he had seen. They rose together, came to the place that the saint Yohannes had shown him (= the man), dug, and found a clay vase. When they opened it, a very sweet fragrant odor rose toward them; they found the holy head of the saint Yohannes and were blessed by it. They put it back in the vase and closed the opening of it as it was before. The man carried it with him to his house, rendered it great honors, and hung before it a lamp burning continually. When the death of the man was near, he spoke to his sister and ordered her - she was indeed a holy (woman) - to act as he himself acted. She in turn honored the holy head and lit a lamp before it. The head of the saint passed from man to man until it arrived at an Arian. This Arian thought that the miracles that the holy head performed (were due) to the faith of 'Aryos (= Arius) the heretic. The saint Yohannes raised against him a man who was stronger than him and (who) took it (= the head) away from that place. The place (where it was placed) was deserted until abba Qerellos (= Cyril) was appointed bishop of the city of Jerusalem and abba Neyanos (= Martian), bishop of the city of Hemed (= Emesa?). The saint Yohannes appeared to abba Neyanos in (his) sleep and indicated to him the place where the holy head (was). Abba Neyanos went (there) and it (= the head) was brought up the same (day) as today. It is the thirtieth of the month of Genbot that the holy head was found again after it had been found on the thirtieth of the month of Yakkatit. May the blessing of this saint, his prayer, and his intercession be with us, amen.
ወትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ አፈ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ነገረ ፤ እምዝንቱ ። ወውእቱኬ ፤ እስመ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ፥ ፡ ወእንዘ" ፡ ሀሎ ፡ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወአብጽሕዋ?ቶ" ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወወሀባ ፡ ለወለተ ፡ ሄሮድያዳ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጸሕል ። ሶበ ፡ ገሰሰታ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዘማ ፡ ርኩስትቶጾ ፡ ተሰፍሐ ፡ ስእርተ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጸሕል ፡ ወሰረረት ፡ ርእስ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ውስተ ፡ አየር ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ትጸርሕ ፡ እንዘ ፡
Yohannes Golden Mouth (= John Chrysostom) also says something other than this: When Herod the impious gave an order, the head of the saint was cut off, while (the latter) was in prison, it was brought to him and he gave it to the daughter of Herodyada on a platter. When this impure prostitute touched it, the hair of the head stood up on the platter and the holy head leaped into the air. It cried out saying: "It is not fitting that you take the


[p. 604] {#po.45.p604}
ትብል ፡ ኢይደልወከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንሣአ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ እቱከ ። ወነበረት ፡ ትሰርር ፡ ከመ ፡ ንስር ፡ ወትብል ፤ ከመዝ ፤ መጠነ ፤ ፳ወ፳ዓመት ፡ ወአዕረፈት ፤ ወተቀብረት ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ብሔረ ፡ አራብያ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ነጋድያን ፡ የኅድሩ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፣ ወበፈቃዶደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በጽሑ ፡፥ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ '፪አኃው ፡፣ ነጋድያን ፤ መሃይምናን ፡ ጸድቃን ፡፤ ወኀደሩ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ውእቱ ፤ መካን፤ ። ወአስተርአዮሙ ፡፤ ቅዱስ ፡፤ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርጉ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወአዕረግዋ ፣፡ እምው ስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ወጾርዋ ፡ ወወሰድዋ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤቶሙ ፡ ወአክበርዋ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡
wife of your brother." (The head) remained flying like an eagle and speaking thus for twenty-five years, and it died and was buried in the land of 'Arabya (= Arabia). Travelers stayed in this place. By the will of God, two brothers, believing (and) righteous travelers, came to this place and stayed in this place. The saint Yohannes appeared to them and ordered them to bring up his holy head. They brought it up from this place, carried it, took it with them to their house, and rendered it great honors. May the intercession of this saint Yohannes and his blessing be with us, amen.
ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሚናስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘእለ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። በረከቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also there is the commemoration of abba Minas (= Menas), patriarch of Alexandria. May his blessing be with our king 'Igabii for ever and ever, amen.]
ተፈጸመ ፡ ወርኃ ፡ የካቲት ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ በሰላመ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አብ ፡ ለዓለመ ፤ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን ። ለዘጸሐፎ ፡ ወለዘአጽሐፎ ፡'ወለዘአንበቦ ፡ ወለዘተርጐሞ ፡ ወለዘሰምዓ ፡ ቃላቲሁ ፣ ኅቡረ ፤ ይምሐሮሙ ፡ 'እግዚአብሔር ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያትቶ፤ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፤ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን። ወለዘ ፡ አተርጐሞ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ወአዕለዎ ፡ እምልሳነ ፡ ዓረቢ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ልሳነ ፡ ግዕዝ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ኃጥእ ፡ ግብጻዊ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ወመነኮስ ፡ በስም ፡ ዘእምደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ይስእል ፡ እምኅበ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ያነብብዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡፤ ከመ ፡ ይስአልዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስረይ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኃጣውኢሁ ፡ ወይዝክርዎ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘዘከሮ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ይዘክሮ ፡ በምሕረቱ ። ወየሀቦ ፡ ዕሜቶ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ጸወቋወቿወ፻ ። ወለኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፤ ይምሀሮሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡፤ አሜን ።
The blessed month of Yakkatit is completed in the peace of God the Father for ever and ever, amen and amen. May God have mercy jointly in the kingdom of heaven on him who wrote it (this book), on him who had it written, on him who read it, on him who explained it, and on him who listened to its words, for ever and ever, amen and amen. He who had this book translated and had it passed from the Arabic language into the Ge'ez language (is) Sem'on (= Simon) the sinner, Egyptian, priest and monk by name (only) who (is) from the monastery of the saint 'Entonyos (= Anthony). He asks all those who will read this book to intercede with God in his favor so that he may forgive him his sins, and to remember him at the time of their prayer. May God make mention in his mercy of whoever will have made mention of him (= Sem'on) for good and may he give him his reward, for one: thirty, sixty, hundred. And may God have mercy in the kingdom of heaven on all the people of the Christians of right faith, for ever and ever, amen.
